<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Conciousness</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;

}

a,

}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {

}

}

hr {

	display: block;

	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {

}

}

}

dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-820b-d54b37c59525" class="">1) Portfolio allocator + hedging equations (G10 FX, multi-asset-ready)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b6c6-e747e3aed74f" class="">1.1 Canonical objects</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-8bc3-f4822b8880dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Instruments (e.g., EURUSD, USDJPY, GBPUSD…)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-b783-c29ca8a67f5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prices , returns</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-9a5e-fc441f2c3095" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Forecast (expected return)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-92cf-c480469c632a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Covariance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-89ac-db5a48ac4820" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Portfolio weights (notional-normalized)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b362-fc527f30647f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk scalar from your risk overlay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-9a75-f29a70d9b11e" class="">1.2 Core allocator (mean–variance with hard constraints)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-bb60-f25d883851e3" class="">Objective (convex):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-982c-d72d4ca59820" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{w_t}\ \frac{1}{2} w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t\ -\ \lambda\, \mu_t^\top w_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9dff-c51e6550a6c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Leverage cap:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-afe7-cfa638d50477" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|w_t\|_1 \le L_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b7e9-e987bb1f3993" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|w_{i,t}| \le w_{i,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a5a1-e554d4c4d4a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{i\in c} |w_{i,t}| \le W_{c,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-903d-d5924e880860" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|w_t - w_{t-1}\|_1 \le TO_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-ba78-c1f032eb14bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t \le \sigma^2_{port,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-a62e-f49e48c13ea0" class="">Apply overlay scaling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-a4b3-f04055749c11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_t^{final} = \rho_t\, w_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-a55d-ca1d57dd1693" class="">1.3 Risk parity (alternative allocator; forecast-free baseline)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-ad17-c4fe5b29dbf3" class="">Define marginal risk contribution:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8ad2-d33e0dbfd1cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RC_{i,t} = w_{i,t}\,(\Sigma_t w_t)_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-bb94-dcf8af095871" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RC_{i,t} \approx \frac{1}{N} w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-87a9-c2c770706f6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{w_t} \sum_{i=1}^N \left(RC_{i,t} - \frac{1}{N} w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t\right)^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-af13-e7e1fece31e9" class="">1.4 FX base-currency exposure netting (example: USD)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-a249-f8bfa9d2dfcc" class="">Let  compute implied USD exposure from all pair weights. Enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-849d-f7a397400e3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\Delta_{USD}(w_t)| \le \Delta_{USD,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-abb5-daf8f6c02257" class="">1.5 Hedging module (factor hedge + residual alpha)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-8b14-fec53cbc618d" class="">Factor model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-a1b3-d62c08235743" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = B f_t + \epsilon_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-a969-f99ef227152d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Want portfolio factor exposure near target (often 0 for neutral)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9336-e97815a04586" class="">Portfolio factor exposure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-87b7-f42dc76bb090" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_t = B^\top w_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8451-c496252045f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|b_t - b^*\|_2 \le \delta_b</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-b4b6-e72a3123a728" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{h_t}\ \|B^\top (w_t + h_t) - b^*\|_2^2 + \eta \|h_t\|_1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-9ba5-cbda298f6029" class="">1.6 Execution-aware sizing (cost-adjusted optimizer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a053-f320c399da68" class="">Let expected execution cost per instrument be  (from the execution layer). Penalize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b6c1-fd2128e1870e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{w_t}\ \frac{1}{2} w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t\ -\ \lambda\, \mu_t^\top w_t\ +\ \alpha \sum_i c^{exe}_{i,t}\,|w_{i,t}-w_{i,t-1}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-8f9b-f4020fd21dfa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b6eb-f24a893ad741" class="">2) Predictive layer (feature → forecast → uncertainty → decision gates)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-8fae-f391a83ee950" class="">2.1 Feature system (deterministic, leak-safe)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-9c41-fdc5b71c28f8" class="">Define features  using only information available at .<br/>Examples (all computed from past data):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9f38-e6fa305fc3b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Momentum:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bae9-c535f5721fbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
mom_{k}(i,t)=\ln(P_{i,t}/P_{i,t-k})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-9822-d947c7b7bcd9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma^2_{i,t} = (1-\beta)\,r_{i,t-1}^2 + \beta\,\sigma^2_{i,t-1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-a2be-d760e1fc7aca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{mom}(i,t)=\frac{mom_k(i,t)}{\sigma_{i,t}+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-ad3b-ec4dc0ab142e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
carry_{i,t} \approx y^{base}_t - y^{quote}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-91bb-e6e8792b5add" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
mr(i,t)=\frac{P_{i,t}-\overline{P}_{i,t}^{(m)}}{\operatorname{std}(P_{i,t-m..t})+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-920c-f7686d5b9720" class="">Feature vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a07d-d45f2fe07a76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{i,t} = [1,\ z_{mom},\ mr,\ \sigma,\ \text{spread},\ \ldots]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-b5d3-f5e49f19fcd9" class="">2.2 Forecast models (choose 1+; all supported by the same interface)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-8621-f4729f1e8cf3" class=""><strong>Model A: Linear (stable baseline)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-8312-c79731102098" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{\mu}_{i,t} = \theta^\top x_{i,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-bd82-c9b550380b3d" class=""><strong>Model B: Regime-switch mixture (micro→macro bridge)</strong><br/>Let regime  inferred from observable regime features  (vol, dispersion, trend).<br/>Soft assignment:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a370-e5078e38b353" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_{k,t} = \frac{\exp(u_k^\top g_t)}{\sum_{j=1}^K \exp(u_j^\top g_t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-a7a8-ec3f16055ab1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{\mu}_{i,t} = \sum_{k=1}^K \pi_{k,t}\,(\theta_k^\top x_{i,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-872f-dff4a94dd9b9" class=""><strong>Model C: Online update (bounded drift; deterministic)</strong><br/>Recursive least squares (RLS) with forgetting :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a7cb-e86dc1e0ee2e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta_t = \theta_{t-1} + K_t\big(r_{t} - x_t^\top \theta_{t-1}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-98fe-d6315ca4383b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\theta_t-\theta_{t-1}\|_2 \le \Delta_\theta^{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-990f-f6e27f70e1c1" class="">2.3 Uncertainty and calibration (forecast must carry confidence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-a82c-ddcd08581a1d" class="">Residuals:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a1a4-ffb6eae82ca2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_{i,t} = r_{i,t} - \hat{\mu}_{i,t-1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-af3a-ca68a0bbff14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{\sigma}^2_{\mu}(i,t) = (1-\gamma)e_{i,t}^2 + \gamma \hat{\sigma}^2_{\mu}(i,t-1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a61c-d08487fd8d38" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SNR_{i,t}=\frac{|\hat{\mu}_{i,t}|}{\hat{\sigma}_{\mu}(i,t)+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-846e-e376a13af3d7" class="">2.4 Decision gates (no “alpha without stability”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-b1ca-cc225e9e059c" class=""><strong>Trade gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-b708-de008ed7e611" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{TradeAllowed}_{i,t} \iff SNR_{i,t} \ge \tau_{snr}
\ \wedge\ \text{PolicyOK}_t\ \wedge\ \text{RiskOK}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-9cd6-fd000426ff26" class=""><strong>Position target from forecast (risk-normalized)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-b078-ffa10bbc9596" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
pos^*_{i,t} = k \cdot \frac{\hat{\mu}_{i,t}}{\sigma_{i,t}^2+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-aec1-ca2c25e9e831" class=""><strong>Multi-horizon fusion (micro→macro across time)</strong><br/>Forecast on horizons  bars:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b456-ec61ae776757" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{\mu}_{i,t} = \sum_h \omega_h \hat{\mu}^{(h)}_{i,t},\quad \sum_h \omega_h = 1,\ \omega_h\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-b50b-f0b3741971f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega_h(t)=\omega_h(z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a27a-f084716fc651" class="">2.5 Cross-species / self–nonself translation (engine-relevant invariant form)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-8fd9-cd389e3c330d" class="">You can represent “agent sensing” (human/animal/machine) as the same control object:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a5aa-c123242596a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b3fd-f70e4ee8eea4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">state estimate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-88f4-c2b788f5d534" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-a8cc-d83aa9cec565" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">update with bounded error and bounded delay<br/>This is the same stability condition your recursion-depth model already uses; in trading it becomes:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-a5f6-c3a9336c8306" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{stability} \iff \text{update delay} \le \tau_{\max}
\ \wedge\ \text{error budget} \le \epsilon_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a67a-fdef5354a03c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a889-f997c5ba80b8" class="">3) Full backtest/sim spec (deterministic replay, walk-forward, robustness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-80c2-fb74b7231a7d" class="">3.1 Deterministic replay contract</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-a6c2-f39931c6674b" class="">Inputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9e15-faebd65a69d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Market data snapshots (bars/ticks) stored under TARGET_ROOT</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a5a1-e9c213a69b49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Config (single JSON canonical)<br/>Outputs (all written under <code>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/</code>):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul PnL^{fcst} \approx \sum_t w_{t-1}^\top r_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-9e7d-c86d5fba363b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost^{exe} = \sum_t \sum_i c^{exe}_{i,t}\cdot | \Delta w_{i,t} |</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-9e64-f6e976c3e2b2" class="">Report difference between “with overlay” and “without overlay” runs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-88c5-f04f2dcc7bd4" class="">3.5 Robustness tests (stress suite)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8a43-f0f1de6e3369" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Spread shock</strong>: multiply spreads by , re-run.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-ac47-d4f151d32670" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Latency shock</strong>: increase fill delay , re-run.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-bda6-fc3882f50573" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Vol shock</strong>: scale returns (deterministic).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-8cb7-ca379bdac743" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Regime permutation test (bounded)</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-86dd-fa130881defc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reorder regimes by contiguous blocks (preserve local structure), re-run.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-b954-c4592f620ec2" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Feature ablation</strong>: remove one feature group at a time.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-83dc-eb74dd400f9d" class="">Pass criteria examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-be27-da2d1f2f2325" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No single feature ablation collapses performance to near-zero if strategy claims multi-source robustness.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-8448-c895504bfb7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Under stress, drawdown remains within declared caps or strategy must classify as Bounded/Invalid.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a553-e4fde8a93c8f" class="">3.6 Termination classification (for the trading engine run)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8cec-c02710a20fce" class=""><strong>Structurally Valid</strong> if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-adc9-c70419dcf9e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic replay passes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-9b1a-c6f40e970760" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No leakage checks pass</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-8a62-f8270e7cd2e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Overlay gates never violated without logging</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-a538-fca17eaebe46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reports complete</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8673-f0326a6098ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stress suite within declared bounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a77b-dd06a84a001d" class=""><strong>Structurally Bounded</strong> if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-b6c9-ee1bdf270d36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Some data formats unsupported (e.g., missing spreads/volume) but explicitly logged and the engine degrades safely</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-9569-edb1a362600e" class=""><strong>Structurally Invalid</strong> if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-ae50-c54b25b56534" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any leakage detected</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-82b1-d686a9469b47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-determinism detected</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b9b3-ee4506854704" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Missing critical outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9332-dd4a3af3e5c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk caps violated without circuit breaker action</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-bdd8-e471ee9dfd5a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-91ad-d1819403b67f" class="">Next “max power” addition (immediately buildable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-ae49-f5f4c20427da" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Leakage auditor</strong>: formal tests that attempt to predict the future using “forbidden columns” and flags any accidental look-ahead.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-a47b-f4472c3bbaa0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Causal feature compiler</strong>: a DSL that only permits operators that are provably causal under the dataset’s timestamp semantics.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a69e-e411bbbc284e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Market microstructure mode</strong>: if you have tick/L2, add queue-position and fill-priority models; otherwise keep bounded.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-ac5c-c04874ac1d0e" class="">Next: Execution + order-types + risk overlays (features + equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-b11e-e93574a93198" class="">A) Execution layer (OMS/EMS features)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-aac4-daf647adb451" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Order intent</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b771-cf43167cf613" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>intent ∈ {enter, exit, rebalance, hedge, reduce_risk}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9543-eafb2cb0dcc1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Order type (offline-simulatable)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-bf29-da58c669ade8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>type ∈ {market, limit, stop, stop_limit, iceberg(sim), post_only(sim)}</code><br/>(If you do not have L2/orderbook, “sim” types are modeled via fill rules.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-96f0-d8d5c7fa76ce" class="numbered-list" style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TIF ∈ {IOC, FOK, GTC, DAY}</code> (modeled deterministically)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-83d4-c85268e66523" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Participation / schedule execution</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a302-dd07a94fae07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TWAP schedule (time-weighted)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-98cf-caa2dde78019" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fill model (deterministic rules)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-84b6-e7ddf01b3e21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>fill_prob</code> depends on distance-to-mid, spread, volatility, time-in-force, and queue proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-91f3-c5602d00d8f0" class="numbered-list" style="list-style-type:disc">decomposed into: spread cost + impact cost + adverse selection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-9dba-da604bc803a9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Partial fill + residual handling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a06e-d2977510b5ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if not filled: re-quote, convert to market, or cancel (policy-controlled)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-917a-d2e61b1eb320" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-venue / broker abstraction</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b758-f8753191d0ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Venue” is a config object; offline engine simulates per-venue
