style="list-style-type:disc"><code>latency_ms</code>, <code>decision_to_fill_delay</code>, with deterministic seedless simulation (no randomness; use threshold rules)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b623-d00771ef9942" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Execution telemetry</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8ed3-da41c77091a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>expected_cost</code>, <code>realized_cost</code>, <code>fill_rate</code>, <code>time_to_fill</code>, <code>reject_reason</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-b758-c96016f7c35e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-88bd-c36578bbbd92" class="">B) Execution equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-bba6-c17764bf615b" class="">B1) Order size from target position</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-ae6c-e079223d3045" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Position delta</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-af99-f6aa682c1c3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta pos_t = pos_t^* - pos_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-b660-fc9c381e3e00" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Order notional (single instrument)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-99f3-c73434c69e3e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t = |\Delta pos_t|\cdot E_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-ad30-cc320f023c07" class="">B2) Spread + impact + adverse selection cost</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-9984-c8fa00c69453" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Spread cost (per notional)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-8676-c95aeec73830" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^{spr}_t = \frac{spr_t}{2}\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{marketable}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9a3e-ce306310d16c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Impact cost (square-root / quadratic hybrid, model-bounded)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-9176-eea195a99b61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^{imp}_t = \eta_1\sqrt{\frac{Q_t}{V_t+\epsilon}} + \eta_2\left(\frac{Q_t}{V_t+\epsilon}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8ec5-ef34048fcea6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^{imp}_t = \eta_\sigma \cdot \sigma_t \cdot \sqrt{Q_t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8492-dcb3b9a2bc42" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Adverse selection proxy (cost increases when price moves against you during fill delay)<br/>Let fill delay be bars:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b85e-fc6003b9e14f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^{adv}_t = \gamma \cdot \max\big(0,\ -\operatorname{sign}(\Delta pos_t)\cdot r_{t:t+d}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-837f-c6245454a054" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Total expected execution cost</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-aabc-cc74108c1d87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^{exe}_t = c^{spr}_t + c^{imp}_t + c^{adv}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-8111-d53983fbab8b" class="">B3) Fill rules (limit order)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-80d1-fd122239536b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Limit price</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b8e1-cf2f6fe3e3cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t = mid_t - \operatorname{sign}(\Delta pos_t)\cdot \delta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b3cd-ffde842b4b93" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta_t \in \{0,\ 0.25\,spr_t\cdot mid_t,\ 0.5\,spr_t\cdot mid_t,\ 1.0\,spr_t\cdot mid_t,\ 0.5\,ATR_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-8796-ddc3eb3c378a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Deterministic fill condition (next-bar OHLC model)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-91d8-e268806df5cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Buy limit fills if</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9d75-c77e3c91a9ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sell limit fills if</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8c60-df87d8e1a747" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Partial fill proxy (if volume exists)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b959-d6deef46f2b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
fill\_frac_t = \min\left(1,\ \frac{\phi\cdot V_{t+1}}{Q_t+\epsilon}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9b91-d079148eb754" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
fill\_frac_t =
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; \delta_t=0\\
0.5 &amp; \delta_t \le 0.5\,spr_t\cdot mid_t\\
0.25 &amp; \text{else}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-bf1d-c7ca7b276afb" class="">B4) Execution decision gate (choose market vs limit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-8ff5-d13214c39d25" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Choose market if expected opportunity loss &gt; expected cost advantage<br/>Let expected drift during delay be per bar:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-8672-d036bb44c8ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{opp\_loss}_t = |\Delta pos_t|\cdot \max(0,\hat\mu_t)\cdot d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-812e-dfbc1fbf670b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{market}_t \iff \text{opp\_loss}_t &gt; (c^{exe,limit}_t - c^{exe,market}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a2ac-fdac042730b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b247-ff64136a5cd5" class="">C) Risk overlays (engine-level “immune system” gates)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9d90-fcaa041cfff4" class="">C1) Circuit breakers</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-9e56-dfa0d957fcf3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Volatility spike breaker</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b763-d07c9dd65d82" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\sigma_t}{\text{median}(\sigma_{t-w..t-1})} \ge \kappa_{\sigma}
\Rightarrow \text{mode}=\text{RISK\_OFF}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-946e-ef6426f17fb3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Spread spike breaker</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a2c5-db15b0dcfd14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
spr_t \ge \kappa_{spr}\cdot \text{median}(spr_{t-w..t-1})
\Rightarrow \text{block new entries}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-b9db-cac039e55444" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Gap/jump breaker</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8c3f-d81586c8181d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|r_t| \ge \kappa_r \sigma_{t-1}
\Rightarrow \text{halt for }h\text{ bars}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b914-d5b9a76215e8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Drawdown breaker</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9653-d5a71675e2d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DD_t \ge DD_{\text{soft}} \Rightarrow \text{reduce risk factor } \rho_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8d71-e6654e14cec3" class="">DD_t \ge DD_{\text{hard}} \Rightarrow \text{flat positions}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b63d-e0e45c415ff8" class="">C2) Dynamic risk scaling (continuous)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-9e7f-f2e478a2787a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Risk scalar from drawdown + vol + spread</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8db7-c8d92a27f9dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_t = \text{clip}\left(
1 - a\cdot \frac{DD_t}{DD_{\max}}
- b\cdot \frac{\sigma_t}{\sigma_{\text{ref}}}
- c\cdot \frac{spr_t}{spr_{\text{ref}}}
,\ 0,\ 1
\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-80a0-ea5fc26cbaad" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Apply risk scalar to target position</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-809a-d630f9d765a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
pos^{**}_t = \rho_t \cdot pos^*_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a366-e342d6f4a1d0" class="">C3) Correlation + concentration controls (multi-asset)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-b212-fb800352e549" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Portfolio variance constraint<br/>Let covariance be , weights :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a797-fc424668a5df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_t^\top \Sigma_t w_t \le \sigma^2_{port,max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8ecc-f5b926f5d7e4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Single-instrument concentration cap</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-9e68-efddb9d33c5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|w_i(t)| \le w_{i,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-96a7-c5408920defa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Cluster exposure cap (if assets grouped)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8188-c46f3ec79d30" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{i\in cluster\ c} |w_i(t)| \le W_{c,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-830d-ca1e071260c2" class="">C4) Exposure netting (FX-specific)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-a5bc-f6903fd569a2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Net USD exposure (example)<br/>If positions are in multiple USD pairs, compute net USD delta and cap it:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a165-c1e16d9923f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\Delta_{USD}(t)| \le \Delta_{USD,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-80f0-e06225227182"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-bcd4-c4fe08bd6f08" class="">D) Safety + compliance bounds (bounded autonomy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-8657-e69dd2cab510" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>No-trade list</strong>: symbols/time windows forbidden.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-ae76-cdd04858f0c2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Max orders per hour/day</strong>: deterministic throttle.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-9972-f07366d28210" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Kill-switch</strong>: a single gate that forces flat when any “BLOCKER” issue appears.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8327-c6e118938fa2" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Offline-only enforcement</strong>: no network calls in decision path.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-936c-eaf30e7e5206" class="">Formal policy gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-9223-d8c3f65e19db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{PolicyOK}_t \iff
\neg(\text{blocked\_symbol}\vee \text{halt\_window}\vee \text{order\_rate\_limit\_hit}\vee \text{kill\_switch})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9648-dc3ac39e8257"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-afd6-f7d1b976f8b3" class="">Next after this (choose 1 number)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-8acc-c7db076d1ca1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Portfolio allocator + hedging equations</strong> (G10 baskets, risk parity + convex constraints)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b9d2-f4e3d820d22e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Predictive layer</strong> (feature → forecast → uncertainty → decision gates; no leakage)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-85e3-d2669cdd5664" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Full backtest/sim spec</strong> (deterministic replay, walk-forward, regime splits, robustness tests)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-82a9-c4502e26ddae" class="">1) Feature set (FX engine) — grouped, with definitions</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8ffe-cc68dbae2b8d" class="">A. Data integrity + structure (must exist before any alpha)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-b690-d3d8df1f2887" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Gap score</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-b5fc-cb660727b5b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gap_ratio = missing_bars / expected_bars</code> per timeframe.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-853d-fbe396bab74c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Staleness score</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-b888-c749a6521394" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stale = (t_now - t_last_bar)</code> in bars.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-93c7-e096bfd7105f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Outlier flag</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9af8-cd6efa20cb5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">robust z-score on returns; flag if <code>|z| &gt; z_max</code>.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-807d-d78f61c6ef3b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Cross-timeframe consistency</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-ab18-f459bc71016a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>|ret_1h - sum(ret_5m over 1h)|</code> thresholded.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-a622-f4f4ead1f907" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Session segmentation</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-9f93-e56f8f8631c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">categorical: Asia/London/NY + overlap windows.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-99f6-dff9d33a68f2" class="">B. Price action (causal, leak-proof)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a821-fa63abcbb476" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Log return</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-85bf-f2a0fee139fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>r_t = ln(P_t / P_{t-1})</code></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-af1a-f9c66ca7106b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Multi-horizon returns</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ae32-da75b2b39c73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>r^{(k)}_t = ln(P_t / P_{t-k})</code> for k in {3, 12, 48, 288} (5m bars example).</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-987d-d4029b7f1176" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>EWMA trend slope (normalized)</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-8299-eb52d272d94e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">slope of <code>EWMA(P)</code> over window / volatility.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-b78a-e3befb764981" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Rolling z-return</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-b41f-c45b508016ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>z_t = (r_t - mean_w(r))/std_w(r)</code></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b4ed-eaae111bbd78" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Range / True Range / ATR</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-b109-fec50b506552" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TR_t = max(H_t-L_t, |H_t-C_{t-1}|, |L_t-C_{t-1}|)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-beff-cd85f1d3d1b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ATR_t = EMA(TR_t, n)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a26b-f4ad2b615559" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Candle structure</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-b769-e440bd568665" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>body = |C-O|</code>, <code>upper = H-max(O,C)</code>, <code>lower = min(O,C)-L</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-98fb-c2cba6c5f93f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Breakout proximity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8a68-c63c4147837d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>(C_t - max(H_{t-n..t-1})) / ATR_t</code> and symmetric for lows.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-840b-f69cdee06aba" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mean-reversion distance</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-a997-e4e5f53e7344" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>(C_t - SMA_n(C)) / ATR_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-b928-ec96e7268b7c" class="">C. Volatility + tail risk</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-84fd-c016cfcf558b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Realized volatility</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-99b1-c669877b794e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>σ_t = sqrt( Σ_{i=1..n} r_{t-i}^2 )</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-b25b-daf7505709fa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>EWMA volatility (RiskMetrics)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8983-f3a1e4d72b7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>σ^2_t = λ σ^2_{t-1} + (1-λ) r^2_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-9ebd-f8715ac66521" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Vol-of-vol</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-8f6a-db3ec2e22486" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rolling std of <code>σ_t</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-aa6c-cac5a171de70" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Jump indicator</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-b2a6-fa4a0ecce272" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>1[ |r_t| &gt; κ σ_{t-1} ]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b85e-fbea32150714" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Downside semivariance</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a2ed-c0f10840a366" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semi_t = Σ min(r_{t-i},0)^2</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-b32a-ec507a79a8be" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Drawdown state</strong> (strategy-level)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-92c2-f5453d0b78b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>DD_t = 1 - E_t / max_{u≤t} E_u</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8851-f15d5b4c4009" class="">D. Liquidity + microstructure (offline proxies)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b37c-d82d535598ed" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Spread proxy (if bid/ask available)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b594-f99584badaee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>spr_t = (ask_t - bid_t) / mid_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-844e-f044cbc77752" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Roll effective spread proxy (if only mid/close)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-98c8-cff6af9656af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>S_roll ≈ 2 * sqrt( -Cov(ΔP_t, ΔP_{t-1}) )</code> (use price changes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b6da-d96a374ff877" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Amihud illiquidity proxy (volume needed)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-a17c-d9e6981467c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ILLIQ_t = |r_t| / (V_t + ε)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-ae51-de3a17857c65" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Price impact proxy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a51d-de268f184a96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>impact_t = |ΔP_t| / (V_t + ε)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-a101-e6c96a579be7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Vol × spread stress</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a119-e49225b1095b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress_t = σ_t * spr_t</code> (or proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-815e-f4df33047143" class="">E. Regime + structure (world-model features)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-a875-c22e621935f5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Trend strength</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-8e80-ed60d45aa932" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TS_t = |SMA_fast - SMA_slow| / ATR_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8482-ebcc564814a0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mean-reversion score</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-9113-fa9205b92af5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>MR_t = -sign(C_t - SMA_n) * |C_t - SMA_n| / ATR_t</code> (positive if reverting)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-91f3-e1325c6f6276" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Volatility regime label</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b05e-cdb89946689b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>reg_vol ∈ {low, med, high}</code> by quantiles of <code>σ_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9bd7-f5bf8cc640fc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Market phase posterior</strong> (model-bounded)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8a0f-f7b755215c85" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>p(regime=j | x_t)</code> from HMM / change-point / logistic (offline trained)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9515-cad1c6be8237" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Autocorrelation / momentum sign</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9281-c5a36e9c0d8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ac1 = Corr(r_t, r_{t-1})</code> rolling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8f53-cf3cf26539e3" class="">F. Cross-asset / basket (G10) if multiple pairs exist</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-98c4-cba3e5d80022" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Correlation cluster loadings</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a193-f211a5f00ba8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">first PC loading of returns matrix.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-8721-de320e0d9887" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>FX risk-on proxy</strong> (model-bounded)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-92a9-d2baaabce346" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">e.g., JPY/CHF basket vs AUD/NZD basket return spread (only if in data).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b63d-d3af4fa0bc9e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Carry proxy</strong> (if rates provided offline)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-8dbf-f111b2a31949" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>carry ≈ i_base - i_quote</code> or forward points proxy.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-928b-eaa3cf18bd47" class="">G. Event + calendar (offline)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-9828-d79e6f32c6e3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Event window flag</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8742-c469e0d48f05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>event_near = 1[ |t - t_event| ≤ Δ ]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-93df-fa6de978e222" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Post-event volatility uplift</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9757-e1c7c7cf174d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">learned multiplier <code>m_event</code> from history (model-bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-bf51-e03ed7a96eaa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Weekday/time-of-day encoding</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-9497-f6d43e23fd74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>dow</code>, <code>tod_bin</code>, session overlaps.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-831f-c7b14f29f684" class="">H. Execution + risk control (controller-facing)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-980a-e38946bb4240" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Slippage model inputs</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-93d7-d757278fed36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>spr_t</code>, <code>σ_t</code>, <code>impact_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-820f-e83a80cb1bdc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Leverage utilization</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8786-c5eb9c71c1c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>lev_t = gross_exposure / equity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b9a7-d9dc01a44096" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Risk budget remaining</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-81a2-db873eb1af6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>RB_t = max(0, DD_max - DD_t)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-9e03-e88119ca91e5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Gate failure reasons</strong> (categorical telemetry)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-94d6-f2424966ffc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">which gate blocked trade at time t.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-a77c-fa1734929633"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-871d-db640ec04998" class="">2) Equations (core engine) — full set by subsystem</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b61c-fd093f6a2ec3" class="">A. Returns + volatility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-a01d-f23e3606fc04" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Log return</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-b47b-ec8f3359a1a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t=\ln\frac{P_t}{P_{t-1}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a8bb-f46d44c131a6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Multi-horizon return</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8cbe-ca80661365ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r^{(k)}_t=\ln\frac{P_t}{P_{t-k}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9a86-d2d0f5c3e146" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Realized volatility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8c74-c1dbbc22c463" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_t=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n} r_{t-i}^2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9bb3-f64398c19339" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>EWMA volatility (RiskMetrics)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-83ee-e902f1e020b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_t^2=\lambda \sigma_{t-1}^2+(1-\lambda) r_t^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-9c5c-efa312d48a1c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>True Range + ATR</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a0a6-f8f8aca139f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TR_t=\max\{H_t-L_t,|H_t-C_{t-1}|,|L_t-C_{t-1}|\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-a3a6-cd231f348265" class="">ATR_t=\text{EMA}(TR_t,n)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-b699-c551cbdc81fe" class="">B. Microstructure (if bid/ask available)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-a612-fa105e3d9013" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Mid price</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-bdf5-c2fccc9678cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
mid_t=\frac{bid_t+ask_t}{2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a52e-efd63c21554a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Relative spread</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-a70f-c48e9c5e0bd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
spr_t=\frac{ask_t-bid_t}{mid_t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-a21f-c83c22ea0725" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Roll spread proxy (if only trade prices)<br/>Let :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8d85-c462f17d557d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{roll}\approx 2\sqrt{-\operatorname{Cov}(\Delta P_t,\Delta P_{t-1})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-96ca-c44fb0ec1e23" class="">C. Regime model (model-bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8cb4-c003ba58ddbf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Regime posterior (generic)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-9516-f4f8c48c611e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(z_t=j\mid x_t)=\frac{\exp(\beta_j^\top x_t)}{\sum_k \exp(\beta_k^\top x_t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-a91d-d1db9c72926b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(z_t\mid x_{1:t}) \propto p(x_t\mid z_t)\sum_{z_{t-1}} p(z_t\mid z_{t-1})p(z_{t-1}\mid x_{1:t-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8165-f844c176633f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Confidence gate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-afd4-cfca78a901c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_j p(z_t=j\mid x_{1:t}) \ge c_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9462-d24c7ea98503" class="">D. Strategy signal → target position</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-854e-c09dce7fdc97" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Signal score (example: trend + MR blend)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-be53-deba706ad9bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_t=w_{tr}\cdot \text{clip}(TS_t,-1,1)+w_{mr}\cdot \text{clip}(MR_t,-1,1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a129-c516ba82856f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Target position (normalized by vol)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-bea6-d094567069ea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
pos^*_t = \text{clip}\left(\frac{s_t}{\sigma_t+\epsilon},-p_{\max},p_{\max}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-9cd2-eebc2f22a320" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Risk-parity weights across instruments</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-86e7-fa4a1a4a3a55" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_i(t)=\frac{\frac{1}{\sigma_i(t)+\epsilon}}{\sum_j \frac{1}{\sigma_j(t)+\epsilon}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b02a-dceda7b839b9" class="">E. Execution cost model (offline)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-85dc-f646c832356d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Quadratic impact + spread</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9c0f-e152de5fa9ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
cost_t = spr_t\cdot |\Delta pos_t| + \eta \cdot |\Delta pos_t|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-8732-df7a2c543c93" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Net PnL (per instrument)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b1f2-e168ae52dc82" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PnL_t = pos_{t-1}\cdot r_t - cost_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-ac47-e24a613b332f" class="">F. Control + stability (delay-aware)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-8087-f632b0706816" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Controller update with delay <br/>Let error be :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-9211-ef75c47fce2e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
pos_{t+1}=pos_t + k_p e_{t-\tau} - k_d (e_{t-\tau}-e_{t-\tau-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-a24a-f08f05f6486c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stability gate (simple discrete sufficient condition)<br/>For a first-order proportional controller (no derivative), conservative:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-b84e-ff48695a29fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
0 &lt; k_p &lt; \frac{2}{1+\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-b9a8-c0353e605d06" class="">G. Risk metrics + gates</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-b9a8-fac93478f7b0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Equity curve</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8c23-ea5a595fd276" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_t = E_{t-1}(1+\sum_i PnL_{i,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8592-f89856c273e3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Drawdown</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8ee0-cf5df1bb6248" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DD_t = 1-\frac{E_t}{\max_{u\le t} E_u}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9b1b-f7bd997421a9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Max drawdown gate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b239-d6fea03c50d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DD_t \le DD_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-ba13-e776c0883b51" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>VaR (model-bounded, historical)<br/>For horizon h:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-95af-d286c6bf8b10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
VaR_{\alpha,t} = -\text{Quantile}_\alpha(\{PnL_{t-h..t-1}\})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-af70-d13c5110e65c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Leverage constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-9164-e49418b200cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{gross\_exposure}_t \le L_{\max}\cdot E_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-ac21-d27211161378" class="">H. Master trade condition (gated trading)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-91b9-f0c84616060a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Trade iff all gates pass</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-90ab-cfb4d613d69c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Trade}_t \iff
\Big(\text{DataOK}_t \wedge \text{SpreadOK}_t \wedge \text{RegimeConfOK}_t \wedge \text{ControlOK}_t \wedge \text{RiskOK}_t \wedge \text{PolicyOK}_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-bdd4-ecfa53e06388"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-a0d6-d0823e4e8dc6" class="">3) Minimum “Most-Advanced” additions (feature+equation slots AMOS should build next)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-b98d-e0b2276cba57" class="">A. Structural uncertainty (not just point estimates)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9759-eca30741c82e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Predictive interval for return (model-bounded)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-918f-f81cacb2b3b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_{t+1}\in[\mu_t - z\hat\sigma_t,\ \mu_t + z\hat\sigma_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b314-e0fa82dc9dd5" class="">B. Regime-switch stress propagation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a7ce-c78884e3658c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Regime transition shock score</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-8014-e612d66243c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
shock_t = 1[z_t \ne z_{t-1}] \cdot \Delta\sigma_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a964-c5bf507e523a" class="">C. Multi-objective controller (return vs drawdown)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-a394-c2f805dd72d4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Utility objective (offline sim)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-862f-c455fe1ea777" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J=\sum_t \left( \mathbb{E}[PnL_t] - \lambda_{dd}\,DD_t - \lambda_c\,cost_t \right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-97b5-c9d9b01b332d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-b033-fb2aa8ecb56f" class="">If you want the next listing, say <strong>“Next: Execution + order-types + risk overlays”</strong> and I will enumerate execution features (TWAP/VWAP proxies, partial fills, order book proxies if present) plus the full overlay equations (kill-switches, circuit breakers, exposure netting, correlation caps).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-926c-eb0b8673db5a" class="">A) AMOS-Repo-Fix — MASTER EXECUTION PROMPT (SSOT STAGING + DUPLICATE CONSOLIDATION + RUBBISH QUARANTINE)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-9d1e-e1792f628aac" class="">SYSTEM_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8927-cae501bdfcb0" class="">TARGET_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-8c63-f7e2dc21d9ea" class="">STAGE_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/00_ROOT/ARCHIVE/<strong>STAGE</strong>&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9e82-da041c99778b" class="">QUARANTINE_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/00_ROOT/ARCHIVE/<strong>QUARANTINE</strong>&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-bf66-f92932486581" class="">AUDIT_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/17_OS/audits&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-af21-f6128b69e864" class="">ABSOLUTE RULES</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-9506-d115a0e4d745" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Offline only. No network.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a4aa-ec1e024a40c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Python 3.9 compatible outputs.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b5f0-fcd15cd6cb53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic IDs (sha256). No uuid4. No time.now in logic paths (timestamp allowed only in run_header.json for human logging).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a6f5-f504ddb967c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-destructive: never delete, never overwrite originals during scan/stage.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b7dd-c612b148c8a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">COPY-ONLY into canonical SSOT. Originals remain until CUTOVER.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b1da-d83a5dc6260d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No parallel systems. One canonical import graph.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-94e3-ed0e3eb04f85" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ARCHIVE is never imported.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-951c-e654900c5946" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any “rubbish” file patterns are quarantined (not merged) and reported.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a2ce-ca337251b8df" class="">GOAL</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-acea-de82569e1b0e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Detect and consolidate duplicates/near-duplicates and “agent rubbish files” across ALL folders.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-93c7-db61a612a9c7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Produce a single SSOT staged tree inside 00–17.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8644-f6c5e485f1d0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Produce deterministic reports + a safe CUTOVER plan (rewrite imports only after staged build passes).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-a275-f0cbb75019fe" class="">PHASE 0 — RUN ID + cost(t)=spread(t)\cdot | \Delta pos(t) | + \eta \cdot |\Delta pos(t)|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-bdb9-ee258d35f4e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-b3bf-e0841febb04a" class="">SIMULATION (paper trading only)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-afb2-f24dde4a8ec6" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-ab60-c8d21bb7a2ec" class="">STRESS TESTS (required)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-a25f-de68793d1ac5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Spread shock</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8555-f4b97375320a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Volatility spike</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bd8d-f1258f819d15" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Liquidity drought proxy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-acf9-e38514c83424" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Regime flip (trend→mean-revert)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-9f4b-e32fb87150d2" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Data gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a95f-f0f699e26814" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Valid: deterministic + gates enforced + reproducible backtest + logs complete</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-90da-ca0f652f9bd6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bounded: any reliance on
