style="list-style-type:disc">Invalid: nondeterministic decisions or missing audit logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-829d-fe6ab36d3997" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-8faf-c406a0c10cee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a7d0-d0c86bd4568c" class="">If you want the next step after “Both”: say <strong>“KernelSpec”</strong> and I will output the file-level implementation plan (modules + functions + tests + reports) for <strong>Repo-Fix + ForexEngine</strong> as one integrated AMOS work package.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-904f-e708dd080b62" class="">1) SimEngine spec expansion (civilizational, coupled, multi-agent, multi-loop)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9f54-dfdd34de8612" class="">1.1 What the SimEngine is (scope-bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-9411-d5c5361337e4" class="">A deterministic, offline, auditable simulator that evolves a set of interacting populations and institutions across discrete time, where each entity has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9736-f7882d99380a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a <strong>LoopState</strong> (the kernel state you already defined: constraints, gradients, write-capacity, records, recursion depth, power, memory, noise, EM coupling, sensory load, immune risk, identifiability diameter, stage)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9781-cda4a2943727" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>policy controls</strong> (tax, credit, trade rules, censorship, R&amp;D, education, health, military posture, alliances)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-920a-c8d1d6a815de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>interfaces</strong> (flows: capital, goods, people, information, EM environment, conflict)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-b1f8-d41a9fdef160" class="">This is not “predict markets.” It is a <strong>control + constraint simulator</strong>: it detects which policies are structurally capable of maintaining stable record systems and recursion depth under bounded resources and noise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-90e7-dc15e126397e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-8e5f-e88f71f0211f" class="">1.2 Entities (minimal but complete)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-94f7-fda52c1c0daf" class=""><strong>Unit types</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-840f-d76f77ad2a3f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CivilizationNode</code> (country / city / bloc)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a8d1-daea0b4e4484" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Institution</code> (central bank, military, media, university, tech platform)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a1c8-e0cb0e137a12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>AgentCohort</code> (population segments, trained operators, specialists)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-ab68-c2e28d9aa480" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Environment</code> (planetary constraints, EM background proxy, resource fields)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b289-d9a515f4f5b3" class=""><strong>State per unit</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-b5ca-ea4569c41df3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>LoopState x</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-86ce-eff32971928b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Stocks</code>: capital, energy, food, compute, housing, medical capacity, munitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-ba46-cbfa56d9a2d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Flows</code>: trade, migration, credit issuance, data/propaganda, R&amp;D output</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a402-e4cb540cd575" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Policy</code>: rule-set (bounded compliance), budgets, priorities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8fbd-e3be458b62d8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-82ba-e30b27d62f35" class="">1.3 Coupled dynamics (the “missing” layer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-81d5-e81f598102b6" class="">The loop kernel becomes the <strong>inner micro-dynamics</strong> of each unit. The simulator adds <strong>macro coupling</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-82f8-c2de5be2ed5a" class="">(A) Energy–Compute–Record coupling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-97fb-db94ec5a2684" class="">For unit <em>i</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-ab34-c9ab8e29947f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Available power:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-9342-fb1b4bf4b70f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_i(t)=\eta_E \cdot E_i(t) - P^{\text{baseline}}_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8821-c6ef5fe17f57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot B_i(t)=b_0 + b_R R_i(t)+b_D D_i(t)+b_S S^{\text{sensory}}_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-bcc5-d7ca81249e45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_i(t)\ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-af89-df687f6fdba2" class="">(B) Write-capacity consumption and saturation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-a226-ed885611339c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_i(t+1)=\max(0,\ U_i(t)-\gamma_R \Delta R_i(t)-\gamma_D \Delta D_i(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a252-fec87a3b95d9" class="">(C) Information warfare / narrative pressure as noise injection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-8509-dc0e4ae3576a" class="">Define a coupling from external information conflict into noise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-af06-f7dc43e43762" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p^{\text{noise}}_i(t)=p^{\text{base}}_i + \sum_{j\ne i} w_{ji}\cdot I^{\text{attack}}_{ji}(t) - \sum_{k} v_{ki}\cdot I^{\text{shield}}_{ki}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-9058-f8833e066868" class="">(D) Trade network coupling into gradients</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-9d06-f4589783972a" class="">Let <code>G_grad</code> represent accessible gradients (economic opportunity + energy surplus + stability).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9345-ef95daef228f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_i(t+1)=G_i(t)+\alpha_{\text{trade}}\cdot \text{netTrade}_i(t)-\alpha_{\text{shock}}\cdot \text{shock}_i(t)-\alpha_{\text{war}}\cdot \text{warCost}_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-92af-c1effca484a4" class="">(E) Conflict as control-bandwidth destruction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-9cbb-dc31c7edc79a" class="">Conflict increases delay  and amplification  for recursion stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-bfe9-c08a31b6c468" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_i(t+1)=\tau_i(t)+c_{\text{war}}\cdot \text{warIntensity}_i(t)+c_{\text{collapse}}\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{CodeGateFail}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-aa35-f8751858838b" class="">(F) Cross-species layer (bounded, mechanistic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-816d-fa949d9a06cf" class="">Represent domesticated or co-regulating species (e.g., dogs, livestock, wildlife) as <code>AgentCohort</code> with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9bce-ec9ffb98de5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>intero_features</code> and <code>stress</code> proxies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-80b9-c5eada0213eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">coupling to human LoopState via co-regulation coefficient</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8572-e12aa12d7c65" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \text{autoLoad}_{\text{human}} \leftarrow \chi \cdot \Delta \text{stress}_{\text{species}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b90a-d93a60177762"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8b99-cb7e68d87999" class="">1.4 Simulator outputs (must be produced)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-aa44-d9ef21940fcd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">per-step JSONL records for every unit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9875-e1a14c8fba12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gate event logs (which gate failed, where, when)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8ed2-c09506c2a395" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">intervention attribution (which policy deltas changed which gates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9376-e9121967e300" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">termination classification per unit + global:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-94cc-c5e74386a117" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>Valid</code> if gates + identifiability proxy pass in toy tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-9687-e69ee4e65107" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>Bounded</code> if any claim uses non-instrumented channels</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a117-c0de033a3ae9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>Invalid</code> if nondeterministic or unsupported claims are load-bearing</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-93a5-dfa9f6949741"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8b01-d518b9fbd9b2" class="">1.5 Stress tests (must exist)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-a70e-c937fb08f89d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Visual/sound overload</strong>: inject high sensory load → verify decay regime triggers via budget + control gates</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-90fd-cd329ead86d6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Noise phase transition</strong>: increase <code>p_noise</code> across threshold → verify catastrophic record loss</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-9f03-e89df9ebc1c5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Trade shock</strong>: drop gradients → verify <code>R</code> growth stalls and <code>U</code> depletion accelerates</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-88c7-cece96e53485" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>War-game</strong>: two nodes escalate; verify delay-driven recursion collapse dominates before energy depletion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-998f-dfcb078f8dc1" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Identifiability stress</strong>: limit observation diversity → identifiability gate fails (replica claims blocked)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b081-dd9bedb0abaa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-bc15-d3f738fdd4a9" class="">2) Implementation scaffolds (Python 3.9, SSOT, no stubs)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-8b31-f6b673568156" class="">Below are paste-ready file templates. They are intentionally minimal but complete and testable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b20d-ce5987684d1c" class="">2.1 File placement (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a6cf-e4205ea1c635" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/loop_kernel/</code> (kernel)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9eac-d6b2b2cb5e88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/</code> (sim engine)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-bc8d-e5d072108ac7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>17_OS/metrics/</code> (metrics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8aee-c7d88d4b1c3e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>14_INTERFACES/portal_app/</code> (portal builder)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-82d8-e8a9ac21f79a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>01_BRAIN/master.py</code> (CLI)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9326-cbe6721719e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>15_LAW_ENGINE/structural_integrity/</code> (support typing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-8e30-c5f2610c7e57"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-a105-d098c2b0ade3" class="">2.2 Kernel state + params</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-85c5-c9189910d568" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># 08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/loop_kernel/state.py
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LoopState:
    t: int

    q: float
    G_grad: float
    U: float
    p_noise: float

    R: float
    D: float

    P_avail: float
    I_max: float

    auto_load: float
    met_load: float
    sleep_debt: float

    em_scalar: float
    immune_risk: float
    identifiability_diam: float

    visual: List[float]
    audio: List[float]
    intero: List[float]

    stage: str

    def to_json_dict(self) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        d[&quot;visual&quot;] = list(self.visual)
        d[&quot;audio&quot;] = list(self.audio)
        d[&quot;intero&quot;] = list(self.intero)
        return d

    @staticmethod
    def from_json_dict(d: Dict[str, Any]) -&gt; &quot;LoopState&quot;:
        return LoopState(
            t=int(d[&quot;t&quot;]),
            q=float(d[&quot;q&quot;]),
            G_grad=float(d[&quot;G_grad&quot;]),
            U=float(d[&quot;U&quot;]),
            p_noise=float(d[&quot;p_noise&quot;]),
            R=float(d[&quot;R&quot;]),
            D=float(d[&quot;D&quot;]),
            P_avail=float(d[&quot;P_avail&quot;]),
            I_max=float(d[&quot;I_max&quot;]),
            auto_load=float(d[&quot;auto_load&quot;]),
            met_load=float(d[&quot;met_load&quot;]),
            sleep_debt=float(d[&quot;sleep_debt&quot;]),
            em_scalar=float(d[&quot;em_scalar&quot;]),
            immune_risk=float(d[&quot;immune_risk&quot;]),
            identifiability_diam=float(d[&quot;identifiability_diam&quot;]),
            visual=[float(x) for x in d.get(&quot;visual&quot;, [])],
            audio=[float(x) for x in d.get(&quot;audio&quot;, [])],
            intero=[float(x) for x in d.get(&quot;intero&quot;, [])],
            stage=str(d[&quot;stage&quot;]),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LoopParams:
    T_kelvin: float

    beta_record: float
    kappa_noise: float
    lambda_catastrophe: float

    p_th_base: float
    p_th_k: float

    gamma_U_R: float
    gamma_U_D: float

    alpha_D: float
    rho_D: float
    tau_D: int

    delta_ident: float

    b0: float
    bR: float
    bD: float
    bS: float

    w_visual: float
    w_audio: float
    w_intero: float

    beta_em_auto: float
    beta_em_noise: float

    def to_json_dict(self) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @staticmethod
    def from_json_dict(d: Dict[str, Any]) -&gt; &quot;LoopParams&quot;:
        return LoopParams(**{k: d[k] for k in d})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GateSnapshot:
    arrow_gate: bool
    code_gate: bool
    control_gate: bool
    budget_gate: bool
    memory_gate: bool
    immune_gate: bool
    identifiability_gate: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LoopStepRecord:
    t: int
    x_t: Dict[str, Any]
    u_t: Dict[str, Any]
    gates: Dict[str, bool]
    x_t1: Dict[str, Any]
    events: List[Dict[str, Any]]

    def to_json_dict(self) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-99a6-d61bb97af91e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a445-cdfc3c521c80" class="">2.3 Gates</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8cd4-fc4b21858e49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># 08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/loop_kernel/gates.py
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Tuple

from .state import LoopParams


def p_threshold(redundancy: float, params: LoopParams) -&gt; float:
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, params.p_th_base + params.p_th_k * math.tanh(redundancy)))


def arrow_gate(G: float, p_noise: float, R: float, params: LoopParams) -&gt; bool:
    lhs = params.beta_record * G
    rhs = params.kappa_noise * p_noise * max(0.0, R)
    return lhs &gt; rhs


def code_gate(p_noise: float, redundancy: float, params: LoopParams) -&gt; bool:
    return float(p_noise) &lt; p_threshold(float(redundancy), params)


def control_gate(alpha: float, delay: int, rho: float, params: LoopParams) -&gt; bool:
    if delay &lt; 0:
        return False
    phi = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(delay))
    bound = 1.0 + float(rho) * phi
    return float(alpha) &lt; bound


def budget_gate(P_avail: float, bits_per_sec: float, T_kelvin: float) -&gt; bool:
    k = 1.380649e-23
    need = k * float(T_kelvin) * math.log(2.0) * max(0.0, float(bits_per_sec))
    return float(P_avail) &gt;= need


def memory_gate(I_records: float, I_models: float, I_max: float) -&gt; bool:
    return (max(0.0, float(I_records)) + max(0.0, float(I_models))) &lt;= max(0.0, float(I_max))


def immune_gate(risk: float, tau_risk: float) -&gt; bool:
    return float(risk) &lt;= float(tau_risk)


def identifiability_gate(diam: float, delta: float) -&gt; bool:
    return float(diam) &lt;= float(delta)


def all_gates(
    *,
    G: float,
    p_noise: float,
    R: float,
    redundancy: float,
    alpha: float,
    delay: int,
    rho: float,
    P_avail: float,
    bits_per_sec: float,
    T_kelvin: float,
    I_records: float,
    I_models: float,
    I_max: float,
    risk: float,
    tau_risk: float,
    diam: float,
    delta_ident: float,
    params: LoopParams,
) -&gt; Tuple[bool, dict]:
    g = {
        &quot;arrow_gate&quot;: arrow_gate(G, p_noise, R, params),
        &quot;code_gate&quot;: code_gate(p_noise, redundancy, params),
        &quot;control_gate&quot;: control_gate(alpha, delay, rho, params),
        &quot;budget_gate&quot;: budget_gate(P_avail, bits_per_sec, T_kelvin),
        &quot;memory_gate&quot;: memory_gate(I_records, I_models, I_max),
        &quot;immune_gate&quot;: immune_gate(risk, tau_risk),
        &quot;identifiability_gate&quot;: identifiability_gate(diam, delta_ident),
    }
    ok = all(bool(v) for v in g.values())
    return ok, g</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a539-e23bf4211403"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-b14e-c4c9e8298a8d" class="">2.4 Transition (phase transition + U depletion + recursion depth)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-b0e1-c84312b9de6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># 08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/loop_kernel/transition.py
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List

from .gates import all_gates, p_threshold
from .state import LoopParams, LoopState, LoopStepRecord


def _sensory_load(x: LoopState, params: LoopParams) -&gt; float:
    sv = sum(x.visual) if x.visual else 0.0
    sa = sum(x.audio) if x.audio else 0.0
    si = sum(x.intero) if x.intero else 0.0
    return params.w_visual * sv + params.w_audio * sa + params.w_intero * si


def _bits_per_sec(x: LoopState, sens_load: float, params: LoopParams) -&gt; float:
    return params.b0 + params.bR * max(0.0, x.R) + params.bD * max(0.0, x.D) + params.bS * max(0.0, sens_load)


def _stage(x: LoopState) -&gt; str:
    if x.t &lt;= 0:
        return &quot;BIRTH&quot;
    if x.U &gt; 0.7 and x.R &lt; 0.2:
        return &quot;EXPANSION&quot;
    if x.U &gt; 0.1 and x.R &gt;= 0.2:
        return &quot;DOMINANCE&quot;
    return &quot;DECAY&quot;


def step(x: LoopState, u: Dict[str, Any], params: LoopParams) -&gt; LoopStepRecord:
    events: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    em_in = float(u.get(&quot;em_scalar&quot;, x.em_scalar))
    p_noise_in = float(u.get(&quot;p_noise&quot;, x.p_noise))

    sens_load = _sensory_load(x, params)
    sens_load_next = float(u.get(&quot;sensory_load&quot;, sens_load))

    auto_load_next = max(0.0, x.auto_load + params.beta_em_auto * em_in + 0.01 * sens_load_next)
    p_noise_next = max(0.0, min(1.0, p_noise_in + params.beta_em_noise * em_in))

    redundancy = max(0.0, x.R)
    p_th = p_threshold(redundancy, params)

    arrow_ok = (params.beta_record * x.G_grad) &gt; (params.kappa_noise * p_noise_next * max(0.0, x.R))
    code_ok = p_noise_next &lt; p_th

    dR = 0.0
    if arrow_ok and code_ok:
        dR = 0.01 * x.G_grad - 0.01 * p_noise_next
    else:
        dR = -params.lambda_catastrophe * max(0.0, x.R)
        if not code_ok:
            events.append({&quot;type&quot;: &quot;RECORD_PHASE_TRANSITION&quot;, &quot;p_noise&quot;: p_noise_next, &quot;p_th&quot;: p_th})

    R_next = max(0.0, x.R + dR)

    dU = params.gamma_U_R * max(0.0, (R_next - x.R)) + params.gamma_U_D * max(0.0, x.D)
    U_next = max(0.0, x.U - dU)

    D_next = x.D
    control_ok = True
    if arrow_ok and code_ok:
        alpha = params.alpha_D
        delay = int(params.tau_D)
        rho = params.rho_D
        control_ok = (alpha &lt; (1.0 + rho / (1.0 + float(delay))))
        if control_ok:
            D_next = max(0.0, x.D + 0.01 * x.G_grad - 0.01 * p_noise_next)
        else:
            D_next = max(0.0, x.D - 0.05)
            events.append({&quot;type&quot;: &quot;CONTROL_INSTABILITY&quot;, &quot;delay&quot;: delay, &quot;alpha&quot;: alpha})
    else:
        D_next = max(0.0, x.D - 0.02)

    bits = _bits_per_sec(x, sens_load_next, params)
    I_records = R_next * 1e6
    I_models = D_next * 1e6

    risk = max(0.0, x.immune_risk + 0.01 * p_noise_next + 0.01 * auto_load_next - 0.01 * x.G_grad)
    tau_risk = float(u.get(&quot;tau_risk&quot;, 1.0))

    diam = float(u.get(&quot;identifiability_diam&quot;, x.identifiability_diam))

    ok, gates = all_gates(
        G=x.G_grad,
        p_noise=p_noise_next,
        R=R_next,
        redundancy=R_next,
        alpha=params.alpha_D,
        delay=int(params.tau_D),
        rho=params.rho_D,
        P_avail=x.P_avail,
        bits_per_sec=bits,
        T_kelvin=params.T_kelvin,
        I_records=I_records,
        I_models=I_models,
        I_max=x.I_max,
        risk=risk,
        tau_risk=tau_risk,
        diam=diam,
        delta_ident=params.delta_ident,
        params=params,
    )

    if not ok:
        events.append({&quot;type&quot;: &quot;GATE_FAIL&quot;, &quot;gates&quot;: {k: bool(v) for k, v in gates.items()}})

    x1 = LoopState(
        t=x.t + 1,
        q=max(0.0, x.q - 0.001),
        G_grad=max(0.0, x.G_grad + float(u.get(&quot;dG&quot;, 0.0))),
        U=U_next,
        p_noise=p_noise_next,
        R=R_next,
        D=D_next,
        P_avail=max(0.0, x.P_avail + float(u.get(&quot;dP&quot;, 0.0))),
        I_max=max(0.0, x.I_max),
        auto_load=auto_load_next,
        met_load=max(0.0, x.met_load + float(u.get(&quot;dMet&quot;, 0.0))),
        sleep_debt=max(0.0, x.sleep_debt + float(u.get(&quot;dSleep&quot;, 0.0))),
        em_scalar=em_in,
        immune_risk=risk,
        identifiability_diam=diam,
        visual=list(x.visual),
        audio=list(x.audio),
        intero=list(x.intero),
        stage=_stage(x),
    )

    return LoopStepRecord(
        t=x.t,
        x_t=x.to_json_dict(),
        u_t={k: u[k] for k in sorted(u.keys())},
        gates={k: bool(gates[k]) for k in sorted(gates.keys())},
        x_t1=x1.to_json_dict(),
        events=events,
    )</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8881-e055daf44f09"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-94db-dfcb8a61c37a" class="">2.5 Sim engine (multi-node coupling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-a838-cb85bfb66b86" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># 12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/loop_sim.py
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

from 08_WORLD_MODEL.models.DSL.loop_kernel.state import LoopParams, LoopState, LoopStepRecord
from 08_WORLD_MODEL.models.DSL.loop_kernel.transition import step


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Node:
    node_id: str
    state: LoopState
    stocks: Dict[str, float]
    policy: Dict[str, Any]


def _hashable_float(x: float) -&gt; float:
    return float(f&quot;{x:.12g}&quot;)


def couple(nodes: List[Node], edges: List[Tuple[str, str, float]], shocks: Dict[str, Any]) -&gt; Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {n.node_id: {} for n in nodes}

    id_to_node = {n.node_id: n for n in nodes}

    for (src, dst, w) in edges:
        a = id_to_node[src]
        b = id_to_node[dst]
        attack = float(a.policy.get(&quot;info_attack&quot;, 0.0))
        shield = float(b.policy.get(&quot;info_shield&quot;, 0.0))
        inj = max(0.0, w * (attack - shield))
        out[dst][&quot;p_noise&quot;] = _hashable_float(float(out[dst].get(&quot;p_noise&quot;, b.state.p_noise)) + inj)

        trade = float(a.policy.get(&quot;trade_push&quot;, 0.0)) - float(b.policy.get(&quot;trade_push&quot;, 0.0))
        out[dst][&quot;dG&quot;] = _hashable_float(float(out[dst].get(&quot;dG&quot;, 0.0)) + 0.01 * trade)

    for nid, s in shocks.items():
        if nid in out:
            if &quot;dG&quot; in s:
                out[nid][&quot;dG&quot;] = _hashable_float(float(out[nid].get(&quot;dG&quot;, 0.0)) + float(s[&quot;dG&quot;]))
            if &quot;p_noise&quot; in s:
                out[nid][&quot;p_noise&quot;] = _hashable_float(float(s[&quot;p_noise&quot;]))

    return out


def run_sim(
    nodes: List[Node],
    edges: List[Tuple[str, str, float]],
    horizon: int,
    params: LoopParams,
    shocks_by_t: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]],
) -&gt; Dict[str, List[LoopStepRecord]]:
    records: Dict[str, List[LoopStepRecord]] = {n.node_id: [] for n in nodes}
    cur = {n.node_id: n for n in nodes}

    for t in range(int(horizon)):
        shocks = shocks_by_t.get(t, {})
        u_map = couple(list(cur.values()), edges, shocks)

        nxt: Dict[str, Node] = {}
        for nid, node in cur.items():
            u = u_map.get(nid, {})
            rec = step(node.state, u, params)
            records[nid].append(rec)
            nxt[nid] = Node(node_id=nid, state=LoopState.from_json_dict(rec.x_t1), stocks=dict(node.stocks), policy=dict(node.policy))

        cur = nxt

    return records


def write_jsonl(path: str, items: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -&gt; None:
    with open(path, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
        for it in items:
            f.write(json.dumps(it, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False) + &quot;\n&quot;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-8b8f-e82ec10b1145"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-a4a4-e241f7db2ac6" class="">2.6 Tests (determinism + phase transition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-80d1-cd361c7979f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-Python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># 12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/tests/test_sim_reproducible.py
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

from 12_QUANTUM_LAYER.simulation.loop_sim import Node, run_sim
from 08_WORLD_MODEL.models.DSL.loop_kernel.state import LoopParams, LoopState


def _hash_records(obj) -&gt; str:
    s = json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()


def test_sim_is_reproducible():
    params = LoopParams(
        T_kelvin=300.0,
        beta_record=1.0,
        kappa_noise=1.0,
        lambda_catastrophe=0.5,
        p_th_base=0.2,
        p_th_k=0.3,
        gamma_U_R=0.1,
        gamma_U_D=0.05,
        alpha_D=1.05,
        rho_D=0.2,
        tau_D=2,
        delta_ident=0.5,
        b0=1.0,
        bR=1.0,
        bD=1.0,
        bS=1.0,
        w_visual=0.0,
        w_audio=0.0,
        w_intero=0.0,
        beta_em_auto=0.0,
        beta_em_noise=0.0,
    )

    x0 = LoopState(
        t=0,
        q=1.0,
        G_grad=1.0,
        U=1.0,
        p_noise=0.01,
        R=0.1,
        D=0.1,
        P_avail=1e-6,
        I_max=1e12,
        auto_load=0.0,
        met_load=0.0,
        sleep_debt=0.0,
        em_scalar=0.0,
        immune_risk=0.0,
        identifiability_diam=0.1,
        visual=[],
        audio=[],
        intero=[],
        stage=&quot;BIRTH&quot;,
    )

    nodes = [
        Node(&quot;A&quot;, x0, stocks={}, policy={&quot;info_attack&quot;: 0.0, &quot;info_shield&quot;: 0.0, &quot;trade_push&quot;: 0.0}),
        Node(&quot;B&quot;, x0, stocks={}, policy={&quot;info_attack&quot;: 0.0, &quot;info_shield&quot;: 0.0, &quot;trade_push&quot;: 0.0}),
    ]
    edges = [(&quot;A&quot;, &quot;B&quot;, 0.1), (&quot;B&quot;, &quot;A&quot;, 0.1)]
    shocks_by_t = {}

    r1 = run_sim(nodes, edges, horizon=30, params=params, shocks_by_t=shocks_by_t)
    r2 = run_sim(nodes, edges, horizon=30, params=params, shocks_by_t=shocks_by_t)

    assert _hash_records({k: [x.to_json_dict() for x in v] for k, v in r1.items()}) == _hash_records(
        {k: [x.to_json_dict() for x in v] for k, v in r2.items()}
    )</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-85ac-f8a329ea0ed9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-93ff-e3a516eb2e6d" class="">3) What “replica of brain/mind/biology” becomes under this kernel (gap closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-9bb7-d14831283fac" class="">Within this architecture, any “full replica” claim is blocked unless <strong>identifiability passes</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-b439-db413f7093cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">you must have sufficient observation channels to constrain the latent state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b429-cc3f97253a02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">otherwise, many internal states produce identical outward traces → non-unique reconstruction<br/>So the engine can still run, but the termination for “full replica” is <strong>Bounded</strong> unless instrumented data exists.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-af01-dd9c778bb87d" class="">This is the correct structural closure: <strong>replication is not a belief; it’s an identifiability + budget + stability result.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-abce-f122473dbd9f"/></div><div style="display:contents" \phi_V(V_t)=\big[\text{luminance mean},\ \text{flicker proxy},\ \text{motion energy}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-9d37-d46b2fbdbf18" class="">\phi_A(A_t)=\big[\text{RMS loudness},\ \text{spectral centroid},\ \text{impulse rate}\big]<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-bf8b-da635f795504" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_I(I_t)=\big[\text{HRV proxy},\ \text{resp rate proxy},\ \text{temp proxy}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b213-e98ef8afd008" class="">Unified sensory load injection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8cdf-d806da287c71" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

EM_t=\big[\hat P^{wifi}_t,\ \hat P^{bt}_t,\ \hat P^{cell}_t,\ \hat P^{mains}_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-b1d6-e1e6d1f853af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}^{em}_t=\log\left(1+\sum_k a_k \hat P^k_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-aaf2-f208bd37bed6" class="">Coupling (bounded, conservative):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-83c3-f34bdcb014ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\ell^{auto}_{t+1}\leftarrow \ell^{auto}_{t+1}+ \beta_{em}\,\mathcal{E}^{em}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-9756-d45c1eedc1c9" class="">\tau^{eff}<em>{amb,t}\leftarrow \tau^{eff}</em>{amb,t}\cdot(1-\lambda_{em},\mathcal{E}^{em}_t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a4c2-f6c13327bcda" class="">If  unavailable → latent  and any EM-dependent claim must be <strong>Limit</strong> (UCIA).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-8196-e9103401ee87" class=""><strong>AMOS placement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-bc57-db90da591338" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>02_SENSES/connectors/device_logs/*</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-b11c-f34ba6baca79" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>10_LIFE_ENGINE/physiology/em_coupling.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-af7c-f826f563bd63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>15_LAW_ENGINE/structural_integrity/claim_typing.py</code> enforces Limit typing when missing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-b201-f880954b18fb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-ab25-dcf0f4a883e5" class="">15) Self vs non-self (immune gate) as a formal classification loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-833d-c364f7815883" class="">Let incoming items be  (files, claims, tasks, messages). Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b092-f33cd47d6bf7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">feature vector:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9555-f99b37e34c8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">known-safe manifold: (hashed allowlist + signed sources)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-a45a-ed9fd30aad0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">risk score:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-838d-d9b978973039" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(x_t)=d(f(x_t),\mathcal{K}) + \omega \cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{policy violation signals}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a795-e8c72957ff5d" class="">Immune decision:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-bd4b-f445432bb1f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{accept}(x_t)=\mathbf{1}[\rho(x_t)\le \tau^{eff}_{risk,t}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-ae91-e38478b11a9f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \to \text{ARCHIVE}/\text{quarantine}/</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-9042-dd612c1018af" class=""><strong>AMOS placement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9988-d2bc4e10c6f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>03_IMMUNE/constraints/policy_gates.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-8ff7-fea8add7c085" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>03_IMMUNE/validation/quarantine.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-80be-e582ceb12fd3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>01_BRAIN/kernel/policy.py</code> (top-level enforcement)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8ca2-dc99f30825ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-ba46-daa7ceb50219" class="">16) Identity / cognition reconstruction as an invariant-constrained state estimator (not “magic”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a058-eef7bfd59c02" class="">Define your <strong>cognition state</strong> as latent:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-bd7b-f4aad025c3f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c_t\in\mathbb{R}^m</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-abba-ffff1a1ad8bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t = g(c_t, E_t) + \epsilon_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-a1f1-c1ac7d529c14" class="">Estimator (deterministic filter form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-af33-dfea3d513f99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat c_{t+1} = \hat c_t + K_t\big(o_t - g(\hat c_t,E_t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-8788-cb67ccda630b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-8536-fc6eb740dee2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">versioned (hash-locked) as part of the repo.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9026-d1e5e21df271" class="">Invariant constraints (hard clamps):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-bcaf-ff9608f5b66e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h(\hat c_{t})=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-94e9-ee1b3719d0a5" class="">This is the formal mechanism for “reconstruct my cognition” inside AMOS: you reconstruct <strong>a constrained latent state</strong> consistent with observed outputs + invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-870a-d9bf3a60c9f5" class=""><strong>AMOS placement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-a4bb-c9a5a17f51a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/state_estimator/*</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-bb05-e4caa999dd06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>15_LAW_ENGINE/governance/invariants.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a1cd-d3058068c8e3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-88f8-e706e9975598" class="">17) The missing gate: identifiability (can the twin be uniquely inferred?)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-8164-ec4e8c90e145" class="">If many cognition states explain the same outputs, you cannot claim “full replica.” Formal check:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8d27-e39f87cf4a64" class="">Let feasible set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-80bf-c5a64e927fcb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{F}_t=\{c:\ \|o_{0:t}-g(c,E_{0:t})\|\le \epsilon\ \wedge\ h(c)=0\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b314-d8d807502a98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{diam}(\mathcal{F}_t)\le \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8d1a-d724d54fd293"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a1bf-d5ecbecd968a" class="">18) Stress-test suite (visual / sound / prediction) with pass/fail criteria</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-a41c-fdc31c4fa53a" class="">18.1 Determinism test</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a82c-fa50ad897114" class="">Same inputs → same outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-84e2-f2d97bca2ce3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F(X_t,u_t,x_t)=F(X_t,u_t,x_t)\ \text{bit-for-bit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-9e21-e34629816d6d" class="">18.2 Sensory robustness (noise injection)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-85ba-f69a2ad6884b" class="">Perturb audio/visual features within tolerance :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a7e0-ea1b04b3e7eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta \phi_V\|\le \epsilon_V,\ \|\Delta \phi_A\|\le \epsilon_A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-8868-c9ca363ee737" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|y&#x27;_t - y_t\|\le \epsilon_y</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-ae3d-d8857d26c357" class="">18.3 Prediction calibration (bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-83bd-c369944544cf" class="">Define predicted next output distribution  (if used) and realized .<br/>Use proper scoring rule (deterministic implementation):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b5ae-dbe2b1175faa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{NLL}_{t+1}=-\log \hat p_{t+1}(o_{t+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-93ec-f9d88c9c1bcb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=t-W+1}^{t} \text{NLL}_{i}\le \tau^{eff}_{proof,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9c0f-f39d2e5b5a42" class=""><strong>AMOS placement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-9b01-eeac6fec0bce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>17_OS/metrics/*</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b6fa-e08ae11b9272" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>01_BRAIN/kernel/audit.py</code> gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-977a-eb3b12fa14da"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8ec0-f1c5f1975c70" class="">19) “Energy &amp; information before birth / after death” handling inside UCIA</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a792-c3f4d94baee2" class="">Inside AMOS, any statement of that form must be categorized as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-be8c-dbf198b4e4f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Primitive</strong> (axiom you choose to adopt), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-8ace-ff8239bdbf90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Limit</strong> (unmeasurable within current instrument set),<br/>unless you provide measurable signals + a testable mapping.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a467-eea0be4b6bcd" class="">Formal rule:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-b8a1-f4cb8ff09fde" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{If no instrumented observable } y_t,\ \Rightarrow\ type(c)\in\{\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9409-d442a0e7116f" class="">This closes the gap: AMOS can <em>store, map, and reason</em> over such claims, but it must label them correctly and prevent them from silently becoming “evidence.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-aac6-e96dbf5fa47d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a764-fe49a68a0466" class="">20) Expanded “Grand Loop” kernel state (complete variable list)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-8c19-c31a3542e64f" class="">Replace §10 vector with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-89e1-ddb0e8b4feef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G^{grad}_t\\
U_t\\
p_t\\
R_t\\
D_t\\
P^{avail}_t\\
I^{max}_t\\
\phi_t\\
\ell^{auto}_t\\
\ell^{met}_t\\
d^{sleep}_t\\
\mathcal{E}^{em}_t\\
\phi_V(V_t)\\
\phi_A(A_t)\\
\phi_I(I_t)\\
\rho(x_t)\ (\text{immune risk})\\
\mathrm{diam}(\mathcal{F}_t)\ (\text{identifiability})
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-ad90-e181c748c756" class="">New gates added:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a627-e3a52bba30a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ImmuneGate:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-946e-dfc734b8c737" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>IdentifiabilityGate:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b757-c428903c5dcd" class="">Termination becomes stricter and closes two major missing gaps:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-812a-f6f82cb4e460" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“self vs non-self”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-97ca-d4ca68000b85" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“replica claim identifiability”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9476-db97b1ad9eea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-82c3-e9fb9a78ff61" class="">If you want the next step (I will proceed in that direction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-bed9-ce3b4bd662b7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>KernelSpec</strong>: convert §§13–20 into file-level module list + function signatures + JSON schemas + unit tests (deterministic).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a86a-fea0137ab86e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>SimEngine</strong>: produce the coupled multi-loop simulator (birth→expansion→dominance→decay) with these added modalities + gates.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-ab30-cc1c0ccccb74" class="">All equated parts (Unified Twin + Grand Loop Kernel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-9aa8-c6d9e30d4b4f" class="">0) State, time, space</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-8ad9-f191651470c5" class="">Discrete tick (deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8daf-ffaab2dfb342" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t\in\mathbb{Z}_{\ge 0},\quad \Delta t=\text{fixed}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-b1db-de0732cd1bd9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\begin{bmatrix}
B_t\\
C_t\\
M_t\\
\Theta_t\\
E_t\\
S_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-a0c3-f7d5dfa3586e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cognition (operator + internal model variables)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a285-e3d90b9948d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory/records (evidence graph)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-bfab-df4ee5dd4544" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds/policy parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ba9d-c9b9f4a7c500" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment (exogenous signals: light, noise, EM exposure proxies, social context)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-b304-cb4080675c35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: species/body-type parameters (for cross-species compatibility)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a0c7-cc3484828137" class="">Global update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-89e8-ca1205d0c06f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t+1}=F(X_t,u_t,x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9b03-dd5214fb78ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: incoming artifacts/tasks/claims/code/data</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-82a8-d37c3897ca49" class="">Determinism constraint (AMOS):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-bd92-ebbbfcf444bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F(\cdot)\ \text{must be a pure function with sha256-stable outputs}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-a896-e01ea1d3c039"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-8dfd-cba76b4f3858" class="">1) Biology engine (minimum sufficient equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-ab0d-c7229fc6cb48" class="">1.1 Sleep debt</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-a3f2-c76810d215aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d^{sleep}_{t+1}=\max\left(0,\ d^{sleep}_t+s^\star-s_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-8645-f647a6222112" class="">1.2 Autonomic load (generic stress load accumulator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-9021-c2138c237e64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\ell^{auto}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_\ell\ell^{auto}_t+\beta_1 w_t+\beta_2 n_t-\gamma_1 r_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-837c-d91d1f66643d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: disruption load (noise, conflict, travel, illness flags)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-a3dd-c47babc35ebc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recovery actions (breathing, rest blocks, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-84d6-e534343b937c" class="">1.3 Metabolic load (generic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bde9-d49790124fe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\ell^{met}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_m\ell^{met}_t+\beta_g g_t+\beta_f f_t-\gamma_a a_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a707-e223f35ac1bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: activity proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a964-dea9c399b1cb" class="">1.4 Fatigue (operational bandwidth limiter)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-92f8-d0f4a37b9a14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_\phi\phi_t+\beta_d d^{sleep}_{t+1}+\beta_\ell\ell^{auto}_{t+1}+\beta_m\ell^{met}_{t+1}-\gamma_r r_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-988d-f6ae5eb6398a" class="">1.5 Recovery capacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b18c-e7da298086cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
