\kappa^{rec}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_k\kappa^{rec}_t+\eta_s s_t+\eta_q q_t-\eta_\phi \phi_{t+1}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-a659-d91f303b59dc" class="">Biology vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a431-ccea9f7d57c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=\big[d^{sleep}_t,\ \ell^{auto}_t,\ \ell^{met}_t,\ \phi_t,\ \kappa^{rec}_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b1f9-cdf35196c4f1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b9f4-c0b899dc4b62" class="">2) Threshold/policy engine and biology coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8b13-e97803a8a779" class="">Base thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8225-f8e3482c9431" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta_t=\big[\tau_{proof,t},\ \tau_{amb,t},\ \tau_{risk,t},\ \tau_{noise,t},\ \tau_{delay,t}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b9d3-dcc3a6f10499" class="">2.1 Effective proof burden (fatigue raises it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-b3c9-ef9adcad5050" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{proof,t}=\tau_{proof,t}\cdot(1+\lambda_\phi \phi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-9cb8-f45e50984f2f" class="">2.2 Effective ambiguity tolerance (sleep debt lowers it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a64d-f4dc115ade79" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{amb,t}=\tau_{amb,t}\cdot(1-\lambda_d \,\sigma(d^{sleep}_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-b056-f08a678e6621" class="">\sigma(z)=\frac{1}{1+e^{-z}}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bbbd-f59fbc873406" class="">2.3 Effective risk tolerance (autonomic load lowers it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-80a7-e8a461f076b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{risk,t}=\tau_{risk,t}\cdot(1-\lambda_\ell \ell^{auto}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-a433-ef22a8e27bce" class="">Effective threshold bundle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8f9a-e5760622da38" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta^{eff}_t=h(\Theta_t,B_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-8d2e-c62f56d2ba92"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b40e-d06244abdd1d" class="">3) Cognition kernel : operator + error-corrected recursion</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-8477-de6e0db0a5f3" class="">3.1 Core operator (decision function)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-8d43-c8e58752fc73" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t=\pi(C_t,M_t,\Theta^{eff}_t,x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-abb6-e790b1d66df0" class="">3.2 Multi-level model error (depth )</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-a40f-f99e3632ec13" class="">For levels :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-8800-c4253e2bde35" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta^{(d)}_t-r^{(d)}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-88a3-edac355b5c99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[r^{(d)}]\ \ge\ \mathbb{E}[\eta^{(d)}]+(\alpha_d-1)\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon^{(d)}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-8a7b-ee1d8c45c5f0" class="">3.3 Delay-limited stability (control ceiling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8db0-dc5459073538" class="">With update delay :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9202-f580bd570e4c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta^{(d)}_t-\rho_d\,u^{(d)}_{t-\tau_d}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-8225-de71a848e2db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_d\cdot \psi(\tau_d)\ &lt;\ 1
\quad\text{with}\quad
\psi(\tau)\ge 1\ \text{and increasing in }\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-9e23-fee07cb6c353" class="">3.4 Compute/repair budget (no randomness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-ae28-ef9ecf1f69ad" class="">If  bits/sec must be erased for maintenance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-b203-e3a773370c6b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{min}_t(D_t)\ \ge\ kT_t\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8d2c-cd15ca2d2f65" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{avail}_t\ \ge\ P^{min}_t(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-9d95-eb923784b48c" class="">Cognition summary state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-ba65-c64bc8168583" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t=\big[D_t,\ \{\varepsilon^{(d)}_t\}_{d\le D_t},\ \text{operator params}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-a857-cf78578c6a28"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-aa61-c70f8003f0d8" class="">4) Memory/records : evidence graph + redundancy + write-capacity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-9359-c9f4ec2065ef" class="">4.1 Evidence graph</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-8fc2-eef31a7c6809" class="">Graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-b013-dad38ed3effb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t=(V_t,E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-8090-eb6ce808f1b9" class="">Edge types: supports / contradicts / derives_from / depends_on.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b2ea-d1411ef2dcd6" class="">Graph update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-8a5b-ee2b0a013460" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=G_t\ \cup\ \Delta G(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-b6af-cc72954e0db3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
id(v)=\mathrm{sha256}(\text{canonical\_path}\Vert \text{content\_hash}\Vert \text{schema\_tag})[:n]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9e42-e779a06aef24" class="">4.2 Record redundancy (operational direction)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-87f0-c14ef3a8fc90" class="">Mutual information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a17b-e4e73b8e51e1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(S:E_i)=H(S)+H(E_i)-H(S,E_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-ab58-e5b2338b2836" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_\theta(S:E)=\max\left\{N:\ I(S:E_i)\ge\theta\ \text{for many distinct }E_i\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-94c1-e556aa4dfd83" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_\theta(t)=R_\theta(t+1)-R_\theta(t)\ &gt;\ 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-976a-d242c0d5ed54" class="">4.3 Write-capacity budget (environment “unwritten DOF”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8d2d-fd22152863eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma\,\Delta R_\theta(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-aba0-e0ab3d7bbdc7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-8a90-f936f95f14d4" class="">4.4 Code stability (record as error-correcting code)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-8075-dee28d655b40" class="">Noise rate , redundancy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9555-e95bade020a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-886d-d762b405d9f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9d2d-c987f58d385a" class="">Record update with gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8c46-fb742ab73b48" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G^{grad}_t-\kappa\,p_t R_t-\lambda\,\mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-9f47-f3709e406565"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-9ad7-d4a9dc18f6db" class="">5) Environment : sensory, EM, social, cosmic proxies (equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b7c4-da2b34050e10" class="">Environment vector (minimum):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9182-e1e4851904d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_t=\big[L_t,\ N_t,\ \mathcal{E}^{em}_t,\ \mathcal{S}^{soc}_t,\ \mathcal{C}^{cos}_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-aac2-f13b6bce7f25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: acoustic/noise proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-a9cd-d7c421c1dd3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: EM exposure proxy (measurable via device logs if available)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-83b9-c55cc0565dcd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: social load proxy (interaction intensity/conflict)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8f86-e6232bc4f5f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cosmic/planetary proxy (bounded: only if measured inputs exist)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-be48-fbe939ff03d6" class="">5.1 Sensory transduction into load (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9ace-e0974786651f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_t = w_L L_t + w_N N_t + w_{em}\mathcal{E}^{em}_t + w_s\mathcal{S}^{soc}_t + w_c\mathcal{C}^{cos}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b546-f9029c69ff52" class="">5.2 EM coupling proxy (bounded, measurable-only)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-9ef7-d38a073167ce" class="">If only device-level power density proxy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-b8a3-fedf8ff646cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}^{em}_t=\log(1+\hat P^{em}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9388-cbfd9aac85ce"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a4bb-d9b8fb59645f" class="">6) Micro ↔ macro bridge (constraint-counting + compressibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9dd6-e13f7390c424" class="">6.1 Constraint density (macro “specialness” as constraints, not scalar entropy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8c2f-f9a243b07822" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q(t)=\frac{\#\text{independent constraints at }t}{\text{volume}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-a94c-d56a924df1a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dq}{dt}\le 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a2cd-fa0ddc4ad5f6" class="">6.2 Accessible microstate volume</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-8b0d-f7586056edbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega(t)\propto e^{S_{cg}(t)/k}
\quad\Rightarrow\quad
\frac{d}{dt}\log\Omega(t)\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-95b3-fd4b3ae728b5" class="">6.3 Compression-based record criterion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-ae92-eac2c5ba2931" class="">Let  be a coarse-graining map, and  be compressed length (computable).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-913a-f6941f9cd348" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Record exists} \iff \ell\big(C(x_{0:t})\big)\ll \ell(x_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-ad7f-eccaf55fa1c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \Big(\text{stable compressible macro-trace volume}\Big) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b8b3-d6ede8b51d77"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-ba04-ce9af49a78df" class="">7) Cross-species coupling (single formalism, different parameters)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8f7d-c7d3710f2925" class="">Species parameter vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-ba17-ce459f7fddbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S=\big[\text{timescale},\ \text{metabolic sensitivity},\ \text{sensory weights},\ \text{repair coefficients}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-b244-e34bb9a1c93d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B^{(s)}_{t+1}=f(B^{(s)}_t,u^{(s)}_t,E_t;S_s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a6bf-fe8226356822" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde B^{(s)}_t = \Lambda_s(B^{(s)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-94e9-cf05c0c469e6" class="">Cross-species record stability condition (same gates):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-83ca-f9a25b71f993" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0,\quad p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t),\quad P^{avail}_t\ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b6b2-d74b97155c24"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-9fc4-db9c70c70713" class="">8) UCIA support typing (equated decision gate)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-9eef-c9942c038616" class="">For each claim :<br/>Assign exactly one support type:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-aecd-c45f394c72c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
type(c)\in\{\text{Emp},\text{Inf},\text{Def},\text{Model},\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-8709-d1862187cb76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma(c)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; \text{Emp with citation + reproducible artifact}\\
\alpha &amp; \text{Inf with valid derivation from supported premises}\\
\beta &amp; \text{Def from canonical definitions store}\\
\gamma &amp; \text{Model-bounded with explicit domain + tests}\\
0 &amp; \text{Primitive/Limit (no support required, but must be labeled)}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-ac04-cc68f086e54b" class="">Load-bearing constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-a83c-cae2bc679613" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c\in \mathcal{L}:\ \sigma(c)\ge \tau^{eff}_{proof,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8645-ea6ba8989b06" class="">Analogical disallowed as load-bearing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8aaf-d1eda7a56697" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c\ \text{analogical} \Rightarrow c\notin \mathcal{L}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9503-f57cdb1b9e2b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-b737-cabc51ce14e5" class="">9) Termination classification (equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-ad32-f5d65589f54a" class="">Let gate functions output 0/1:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-88a4-e1d30f9da494" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
g_i(X_t)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; \text{pass}\\
0 &amp; \text{fail}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-ab93-db8eaaaa9483" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G(X_t)=\prod_i g_i(X_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-b2b4-ddbc7ebe9254" class="">Let unsupported load-bearing count:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-b5da-f033a2ef75cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_L=\#\{c\in\mathcal{L}:\sigma(c)&lt;\tau^{eff}_{proof,t}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9e04-dc70f2f29d6e" class="">Classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-85a5-de985fabc5dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T=
\begin{cases}
\text{Invalid} &amp; G(X_t)=0\ \text{or}\ U_L&gt;0\\
\text{Bounded} &amp; G(X_t)=1\ \text{and}\ \exists c:\ type(c)\in\{\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}\ \text{in load-bearing chain}\\
\text{Valid} &amp; G(X_t)=1\ \text{and all load-bearing claims supported}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-9907-c64347958615"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b5f1-e0dabe90510c" class="">10) The Grand Unified Loop Matrix (fully equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9e5f-d67a29f09afa" class="">Kernel variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-8003-e11fb8990ad8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G^{grad}_t\\
U_t\\
p_t\\
R_t\\
D_t\\
P^{avail}_t\\
I^{max}_t\\
\phi_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-a02b-d1f8c6317980" class="">Updates (minimal closed form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-b52f-ee8a01c7da51" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{aligned}
q_{t+1}&amp;=q_t-\delta_q\,\chi_t\\
G^{grad}_{t+1}&amp;=G^{grad}_t+\delta_G(1-q_{t+1})-\xi_G p_t\\
U_{t+1}&amp;=U_t-\gamma(R_{t+1}-R_t)\\
R_{t+1}&amp;=R_t+\beta G^{grad}_t-\kappa p_t R_t-\lambda \mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t\\
D_{t+1}&amp;=D_t+\mathbf{1}[\text{all depth gates pass}]-\mathbf{1}[\text{depth fails}]\\
\phi_{t+1}&amp;=\text{(from biology §1.4)}\\
\end{aligned}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-bef4-e5701374c175" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{cases}
\text{ArrowGate: } \beta G^{grad}_t &gt; \kappa p_t R_t\\
\text{CodeGate: } p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t)\\
\text{ControlGate: } \alpha_d\psi(\tau_d)&lt;1\ \forall d\le D_t\\
\text{BudgetGate: } P^{avail}_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)\\
\text{WriteGate: } U_t&gt;0
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-bd14-c3444d1af6db"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-af4a-ed3548e8cf7e" class="">11) What is “intangible” in this system (formal handling, no free claims)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a8b6-f4f4ad097662" class="">If a signal source is not measurable in the current run (no instrumented input), it must be represented as a bounded latent variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-9779-d90f9ef67994" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t\in\mathbb{R}^k,\quad z_t\ \text{unobserved}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-a4c9-d0316c457780" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=A z_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8af2-d1da58bb99e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = H z_t + \nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9c06-e1f4c0a45d4c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
type(c)=\text{Limit}
\quad\text{or}\quad
type(c)=\text{Primitive}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-ad91-e58a8c205fe1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-95d8-e46e14dad80c" class="">12) AMOS file-level placement (where each equation lives)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-8366-c7433a6ff907" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>10_LIFE_ENGINE</strong>: §1 equations (<code>biology_state.py</code>, <code>step_biology</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-9176-d2a8189908ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>15_LAW_ENGINE</strong>: §2, §8, §9 gates (<code>thresholds.py</code>, <code>gates.py</code>, <code>classifier.py</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-9972-fa9eeb7bb0ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>07_METABOLISM</strong>: §4 graph + claim typing (<code>claims.py</code>, <code>graph.py</code>, <code>digest.py</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8acc-e8ba75b374e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>04_MOTOR_SYSTEM</strong>: global step (§0, §10) (<code>unified_twin.py</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b6d5-f0ca83d22359" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>04_BLOOD</strong>: event trace emission (inputs, gate results, termination)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-b8ff-f39e27816ca2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>08_WORLD_MODEL</strong>: variable registry + model runner for simulation of §10 kernel</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9680-e3f3057003e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-839a-e89002694040" class="">If you want the same thing in <strong>KernelSpec form</strong>, I will convert each equation block above into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9911-c853254778ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exact module names,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8be7-ee41443568aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">function signatures,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-b424-ede57c9d7ce4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">JSON schemas,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-855d-e9eba436dec5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and unit tests (determinism + gate correctness + coupling tests).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8387-d98383c11c5e" class="">All equated parts (Unified Twin + Grand Loop Kernel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-acb2-ea8ba8718f98" class="">0) State, time, space</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a6a8-d9b7dea05218" class="">Discrete tick (deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-ab83-c98be91f9baa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t\in\mathbb{Z}_{\ge 0},\quad \Delta t=\text{fixed}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-ae46-c65f87943a0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\begin{bmatrix}
B_t\\
C_t\\
M_t\\
\Theta_t\\
E_t\\
S_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-9f71-e462c89089ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cognition (operator + internal model variables)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-9504-dc7989fd2790" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory/records (evidence graph)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b4b3-f992392ce1de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds/policy parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b567-e6c523bf9aa6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment (exogenous signals: light, noise, EM exposure proxies, social context)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-a5a3-f33d2afdb7ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: species/body-type parameters (for cross-species compatibility)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-91e2-c026a4d52f70" class="">Global update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b910-cd1f0275c10a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t+1}=F(X_t,u_t,x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-bcfa-eeb611c005b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: incoming artifacts/tasks/claims/code/data</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9e13-db0d34d46b44" class="">Determinism constraint (AMOS):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b5b4-c885b76a1eaf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F(\cdot)\ \text{must be a pure function with sha256-stable outputs}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-b763-f4db65f0b8dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-ad8b-efe8d172910d" class="">1) Biology engine (minimum sufficient equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-bc7e-ef07a8643c9c" class="">1.1 Sleep debt</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a9f9-e335d7f10a20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d^{sleep}_{t+1}=\max\left(0,\ d^{sleep}_t+s^\star-s_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-841c-f6c631aede11" class="">1.2 Autonomic load (generic stress load accumulator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-96fd-ccb100271fdf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\ell^{auto}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_\ell\ell^{auto}_t+\beta_1 w_t+\beta_2 n_t-\gamma_1 r_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-b61b-e27d7d5d5c71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: disruption load (noise, conflict, travel, illness flags)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9ba8-d177937d5b8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recovery actions (breathing, rest blocks, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-8346-d404a1588894" class="">1.3 Metabolic load (generic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-8e38-e4b13af57cac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\ell^{met}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_m\ell^{met}_t+\beta_g g_t+\beta_f f_t-\gamma_a a_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-84fb-d9847d290953" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: activity proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-9b9e-fd0fdcbc8af5" class="">1.4 Fatigue (operational bandwidth limiter)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bbad-fdf527da5db1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_\phi\phi_t+\beta_d d^{sleep}_{t+1}+\beta_\ell\ell^{auto}_{t+1}+\beta_m\ell^{met}_{t+1}-\gamma_r r_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-9739-c973044276e1" class="">1.5 Recovery capacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9cfa-faeac71a6745" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa^{rec}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\Big(\alpha_k\kappa^{rec}_t+\eta_s s_t+\eta_q q_t-\eta_\phi \phi_{t+1}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bfc9-f7adca5973a1" class="">Biology vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-bc41-c95aa8b7e63f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=\big[d^{sleep}_t,\ \ell^{auto}_t,\ \ell^{met}_t,\ \phi_t,\ \kappa^{rec}_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-970e-d21e49201ee2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-85e8-ea173b99b5dc" class="">2) Threshold/policy engine and biology coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9845-ddc16de2574c" class="">Base thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-80ed-d5321bc40a87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta_t=\big[\tau_{proof,t},\ \tau_{amb,t},\ \tau_{risk,t},\ \tau_{noise,t},\ \tau_{delay,t}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-a2ee-cd12dbe7e57e" class="">2.1 Effective proof burden (fatigue raises it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-a3ba-d2d3f702464e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{proof,t}=\tau_{proof,t}\cdot(1+\lambda_\phi \phi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-ad84-f051c12950ee" class="">2.2 Effective ambiguity tolerance (sleep debt lowers it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-965e-efabdd306539" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{amb,t}=\tau_{amb,t}\cdot(1-\lambda_d \,\sigma(d^{sleep}_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-9c44-e8f694359c92" class="">\sigma(z)=\frac{1}{1+e^{-z}}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-af25-e7d2dc78b7ad" class="">2.3 Effective risk tolerance (autonomic load lowers it)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-bb51-c0bdc3cb5767" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{eff}_{risk,t}=\tau_{risk,t}\cdot(1-\lambda_\ell \ell^{auto}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8433-ee0c0d4da9fe" class="">Effective threshold bundle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-ab3c-fcb7efb1a056" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta^{eff}_t=h(\Theta_t,B_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b00c-ff54b5ada188"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-9c3e-c8dba978b8e0" class="">3) Cognition kernel : operator + error-corrected recursion</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-813c-dae41925d123" class="">3.1 Core operator (decision function)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-8cc2-feda923d5074" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t=\pi(C_t,M_t,\Theta^{eff}_t,x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-bc11-e1f18d2615d5" class="">3.2 Multi-level model error (depth )</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-9863-f72b61decb2e" class="">For levels :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-bb4a-c94aaa1cb2da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta^{(d)}_t-r^{(d)}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8b51-c80fddb4c57a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[r^{(d)}]\ \ge\ \mathbb{E}[\eta^{(d)}]+(\alpha_d-1)\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon^{(d)}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-867e-cd529d87d81b" class="">3.3 Delay-limited stability (control ceiling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-a905-e177cef23d1e" class="">With update delay :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-8119-f74b4f220c05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta^{(d)}_t-\rho_d\,u^{(d)}_{t-\tau_d}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-aef5-d4b97df18abd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_d\cdot \psi(\tau_d)\ &lt;\ 1
\quad\text{with}\quad
\psi(\tau)\ge 1\ \text{and increasing in }\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-94ae-e0f971c9789f" class="">3.4 Compute/repair budget (no randomness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b3bf-c8f467926a2c" class="">If  bits/sec must be erased for maintenance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8872-fa2199bb4fa4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{min}_t(D_t)\ \ge\ kT_t\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-a686-fca8ecc597ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{avail}_t\ \ge\ P^{min}_t(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b140-ce8398f91316" class="">Cognition summary state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-93dc-f96f3fed7833" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
