C_t=\big[D_t,\ \{\varepsilon^{(d)}_t\}_{d\le D_t},\ \text{operator params}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-bcf3-d01f20ce706c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-8909-c7023928fd87" class="">4) Memory/records : evidence graph + redundancy + write-capacity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-92de-f5d4bfc9f5da" class="">4.1 Evidence graph</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9472-f7538562a93d" class="">Graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9523-dddb5741136e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t=(V_t,E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-9591-c716621cda1c" class="">Edge types: supports / contradicts / derives_from / depends_on.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-acff-fcc7e23532c3" class="">Graph update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-916f-e1b82ed14854" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=G_t\ \cup\ \Delta G(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-8c72-df72d462b192" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
id(v)=\mathrm{sha256}(\text{canonical\_path}\Vert \text{content\_hash}\Vert \text{schema\_tag})[:n]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9872-ec2a750d6fff" class="">4.2 Record redundancy (operational direction)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-873b-c6a16d31b576" class="">Mutual information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8439-d6e5c02617a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(S:E_i)=H(S)+H(E_i)-H(S,E_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-aa9e-d1b7772062e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_\theta(S:E)=\max\left\{N:\ I(S:E_i)\ge\theta\ \text{for many distinct }E_i\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a8b5-f1a4cd1f4bfb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_\theta(t)=R_\theta(t+1)-R_\theta(t)\ &gt;\ 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-b1ef-e8af7be709a0" class="">4.3 Write-capacity budget (environment “unwritten DOF”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-a524-dba13899c749" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma\,\Delta R_\theta(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-92bd-dd6c99f44a90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8230-fe8acf5c5f5d" class="">4.4 Code stability (record as error-correcting code)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8608-ca1981838e65" class="">Noise rate , redundancy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-911b-f726ffbf860d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-b4d0-da8b3662143e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bb5b-e92349ccde81" class="">Record update with gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-af08-eedc0c378510" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G^{grad}_t-\kappa\,p_t R_t-\lambda\,\mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8b75-edbdc5ab16f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-8351-edd4676567e2" class="">5) Environment : sensory, EM, social, cosmic proxies (equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b179-f69102bee0ff" class="">Environment vector (minimum):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9944-c14281d96c5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_t=\big[L_t,\ N_t,\ \mathcal{E}^{em}_t,\ \mathcal{S}^{soc}_t,\ \mathcal{C}^{cos}_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8f22-f2eca866cd60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: acoustic/noise proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b4cf-d7c5a946ecaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: EM exposure proxy (measurable via device logs if available)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-9a74-f73af9ac26d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: social load proxy (interaction intensity/conflict)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-a453-eff2f1ae18e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cosmic/planetary proxy (bounded: only if measured inputs exist)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-80c8-f806d7ee9414" class="">5.1 Sensory transduction into load (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-b47e-e765a85afca1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_t = w_L L_t + w_N N_t + w_{em}\mathcal{E}^{em}_t + w_s\mathcal{S}^{soc}_t + w_c\mathcal{C}^{cos}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8af9-eea071f9effc" class="">5.2 EM coupling proxy (bounded, measurable-only)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-b1d3-d76dc3c071d7" class="">If only device-level power density proxy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-8929-c9dbca3210cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}^{em}_t=\log(1+\hat P^{em}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-ae10-d44ce79701ef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8a4f-dece6f3ad32a" class="">6) Micro ↔ macro bridge (constraint-counting + compressibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-acc3-fac0849ce0a5" class="">6.1 Constraint density (macro “specialness” as constraints, not scalar entropy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-af27-c7fecabc47c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q(t)=\frac{\#\text{independent constraints at }t}{\text{volume}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-9162-fade50d3ffff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dq}{dt}\le 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-a6ec-f72b1150fa07" class="">6.2 Accessible microstate volume</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-b4a5-d85130bdc892" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega(t)\propto e^{S_{cg}(t)/k}
\quad\Rightarrow\quad
\frac{d}{dt}\log\Omega(t)\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-82d9-c4f565778eb9" class="">6.3 Compression-based record criterion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-96fa-f897b4201377" class="">Let  be a coarse-graining map, and  be compressed length (computable).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-a757-edaf5bdc0fe3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Record exists} \iff \ell\big(C(x_{0:t})\big)\ll \ell(x_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9bc7-ef3f0b8314ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \Big(\text{stable compressible macro-trace volume}\Big) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-8567-e03379692133"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-be09-e2fed8f0c564" class="">7) Cross-species coupling (single formalism, different parameters)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-96e7-fdc12ee4550c" class="">Species parameter vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b421-f4afdf8f7aea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S=\big[\text{timescale},\ \text{metabolic sensitivity},\ \text{sensory weights},\ \text{repair coefficients}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-8716-c99eae966784" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B^{(s)}_{t+1}=f(B^{(s)}_t,u^{(s)}_t,E_t;S_s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-be07-e6a2ca16efc5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde B^{(s)}_t = \Lambda_s(B^{(s)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9aa2-e986d1faff0a" class="">Cross-species record stability condition (same gates):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-9785-ef19b45d5fd4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0,\quad p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t),\quad P^{avail}_t\ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-a4b0-f059fcde8c9d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a425-f60bfe2b646e" class="">8) UCIA support typing (equated decision gate)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-914b-fab7f12cc3e9" class="">For each claim :<br/>Assign exactly one support type:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b714-d5625505bbd7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
type(c)\in\{\text{Emp},\text{Inf},\text{Def},\text{Model},\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-ab56-cdace70da297" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma(c)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; \text{Emp with citation + reproducible artifact}\\
\alpha &amp; \text{Inf with valid derivation from supported premises}\\
\beta &amp; \text{Def from canonical definitions store}\\
\gamma &amp; \text{Model-bounded with explicit domain + tests}\\
0 &amp; \text{Primitive/Limit (no support required, but must be labeled)}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b8cb-c53c3680b6f2" class="">Load-bearing constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-afb8-d6033e7fa7b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c\in \mathcal{L}:\ \sigma(c)\ge \tau^{eff}_{proof,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-b36c-e507326a5e67" class="">Analogical disallowed as load-bearing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a37e-cce762ca368a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c\ \text{analogical} \Rightarrow c\notin \mathcal{L}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a5f7-d0460a869468"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a57d-ead68640372b" class="">9) Termination classification (equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8379-d0d02034dcf0" class="">Let gate functions output 0/1:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-bd8e-ea380b9d2576" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
g_i(X_t)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; \text{pass}\\
0 &amp; \text{fail}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8bd2-d764b942546c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G(X_t)=\prod_i g_i(X_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9375-dbe433586301" class="">Let unsupported load-bearing count:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a5c8-cd5ba653152b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_L=\#\{c\in\mathcal{L}:\sigma(c)&lt;\tau^{eff}_{proof,t}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b08d-f872eba6048a" class="">Classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-85b4-f42a6803f439" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T=
\begin{cases}
\text{Invalid} &amp; G(X_t)=0\ \text{or}\ U_L&gt;0\\
\text{Bounded} &amp; G(X_t)=1\ \text{and}\ \exists c:\ type(c)\in\{\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}\ \text{in load-bearing chain}\\
\text{Valid} &amp; G(X_t)=1\ \text{and all load-bearing claims supported}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-9d9e-dae5f90d61b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-99e9-fb91ee995eb5" class="">10) The Grand Unified Loop Matrix (fully equated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-bd07-f7677407c077" class="">Kernel variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-8a20-e7d69ef11f16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G^{grad}_t\\
U_t\\
p_t\\
R_t\\
D_t\\
P^{avail}_t\\
I^{max}_t\\
\phi_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-8c99-dcb70e11a49a" class="">Updates (minimal closed form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9ff5-f33dab4b9a72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{aligned}
q_{t+1}&amp;=q_t-\delta_q\,\chi_t\\
G^{grad}_{t+1}&amp;=G^{grad}_t+\delta_G(1-q_{t+1})-\xi_G p_t\\
U_{t+1}&amp;=U_t-\gamma(R_{t+1}-R_t)\\
R_{t+1}&amp;=R_t+\beta G^{grad}_t-\kappa p_t R_t-\lambda \mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t\\
D_{t+1}&amp;=D_t+\mathbf{1}[\text{all depth gates pass}]-\mathbf{1}[\text{depth fails}]\\
\phi_{t+1}&amp;=\text{(from biology §1.4)}\\
\end{aligned}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-9950-fc437e912163" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{cases}
\text{ArrowGate: } \beta G^{grad}_t &gt; \kappa p_t R_t\\
\text{CodeGate: } p_t&lt;p_{th}(r_t)\\
\text{ControlGate: } \alpha_d\psi(\tau_d)&lt;1\ \forall d\le D_t\\
\text{BudgetGate: } P^{avail}_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)\\
\text{WriteGate: } U_t&gt;0
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-8c78-ffc2e135ec5c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b30a-f51a9e725197" class="">11) What is “intangible” in this system (formal handling, no free claims)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-9820-c4e088d008f7" class="">If a signal source is not measurable in the current run (no instrumented input), it must be represented as a bounded latent variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b04b-ec715f9b8357" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t\in\mathbb{R}^k,\quad z_t\ \text{unobserved}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-969c-ebadc00e1dbe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=A z_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b0cc-e89ed50e6db4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = H z_t + \nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b35d-e21a0e8777d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
type(c)=\text{Limit}
\quad\text{or}\quad
type(c)=\text{Primitive}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-958b-f258a8b0fb89"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-b640-c18d17a534ac" class="">12) AMOS file-level placement (where each equation lives)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-979d-ce2f4ee75032" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>10_LIFE_ENGINE</strong>: §1 equations (<code>biology_state.py</code>, <code>step_biology</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-b89b-eabba115817e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>15_LAW_ENGINE</strong>: §2, §8, §9 gates (<code>thresholds.py</code>, <code>gates.py</code>, <code>classifier.py</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-a204-fb419eac5682" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>07_METABOLISM</strong>: §4 graph + claim typing (<code>claims.py</code>, <code>graph.py</code>, <code>digest.py</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-b6cf-c19b41b10edd" class="bulleted-list"><li id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-abf3-d11dc2da1da9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and unit tests (determinism + gate correctness + coupling tests).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-92c1-f96b973ac050" class="">Unified Twin (Coupled Loops) — SSOT Build Spec (AMOS 00–17)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9384-e1c3a2556d16" class="">Objective</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-8ab3-e873fd271afc" class="">Implement a <strong>single deterministic operating model</strong> that couples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8fc2-fd43acda2167" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Biology</strong> → physiological state evolution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-937b-fe51b63678e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cognition</strong> → your decision operator + constraint gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9c22-e6ac837f4284" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Memory/Records</strong> → evidence-linked artifacts + provenance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-932d-cd981a982195" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Thresholds/Policy</strong> → strictness, proof burden, risk gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b783-e95d87ec1fbd" class="">Single state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-ad96-ef1a9a17f004" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\begin{bmatrix}B_t\\C_t\\M_t\\\Theta_t\end{bmatrix}
\quad\Rightarrow\quad
X_{t+1}=F(X_t, u_t, e_t, x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-ba2e-efb215b45baa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment (time zone, light, temp, disruptions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8069-df0879fa036f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: tasks/claims/code/artifacts presented to the system</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-bd28-d91d8cbb5d9c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-a545-f65eb9108106" class="">1) The Coupling Laws (deterministic, testable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b4c3-cc2ea2cebca0" class="">1.1 Biology loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a7e4-e1bd822302fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1}=f(B_t, u_t, e_t) + \epsilon^B_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-bc06-d1a250f6ca9f" class="">Recommended minimum  vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9650-d9f3d60d1f39" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=
[\text{sleep\_debt},\ \text{autonomic\_load},\ \text{glucose\_load},\ \text{inflammation\_proxy},\ \text{fatigue},\ \text{recovery\_capacity}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-b045-c4283550d9d8" class="">Deterministic update example (no randomness):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-b2c7-d1b8444a2f1c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sleep debt:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a781-c986237bc8b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{sleep\_debt}_{t+1}=\max(0,\ \text{sleep\_debt}_t + s^\star - s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-ad09-cadcf0657106" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{fatigue}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\big(\alpha\cdot \text{fatigue}_t+\beta\cdot \text{sleep\_debt}_{t+1}+\gamma\cdot \text{work\_load}_t-\delta\cdot \text{recovery}_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-8fbd-c4b11718c780" class="">1.2 Cognition loop (your gate-based operator)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bf91-e503a7e0ac30" class="">Cognition update is <strong>constraint-governed</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b55d-f2c7695255fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1}=g(C_t, x_t, M_t, B_t, \Theta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-921c-da24c7ab29f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(x_t)=
\begin{cases}
\text{Valid} &amp; \text{all gates pass and all claims supported}\\
\text{Bounded} &amp; \text{all gates pass but some claims are Primitive/Limit}\\
\text{Invalid} &amp; \text{any gate fails or load-bearing unsupported claim exists}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-972c-fea2c5145707" class="">1.3 Biology → Cognition coupling (critical)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-84df-c78f15f42b79" class="">Biology modulates strictness + bandwidth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9fed-cfe6582be73b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta^{\text{eff}}_t = h(\Theta_t, B_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-84d8-e11e25ac07e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proof burden increases with fatigue:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a9b1-e0ce1619bebe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{proof\_burden}^{\text{eff}}_t=\text{proof\_burden}_t\cdot(1+\lambda\cdot \text{fatigue}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-aeee-d5647f7e6f1c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{ambiguity\_tol}^{\text{eff}}_t=\text{ambiguity\_tol}_t\cdot(1-\mu\cdot \text{sleep\_debt}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-93ef-f299498d93cc" class="">1.4 Memory/Records loop (evidence-linked only)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a6f8-c1ce0c0f9252" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1} = \mathrm{update}(M_t, x_t, \text{artifacts}, \text{citations})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b841-cc96b892f85b" verifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b1ae-d7a43733ae0a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b29d-c127eac93aed" class="">If you want the <strong>file-level implementation plan</strong> next, reply with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a094-e6a530693aaa" class=""><strong>KernelSpec</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-91a1-d68460064aab" class="">and I will output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-bfaf-fead1201aad3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the exact files to create under each directory,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b390-c788f7c8e290" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">function signatures + docstrings (strict, minimal),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-b3cb-e1a808de2a5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">JSON schemas,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-8bfe-d46fc628a9e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">test list + expected fixtures,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-8f7f-fac0513672c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and the build stage integration wiring for <code>01_BRAIN.master build</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-9edf-f080c3d17e01" class="">A “full replica” splits into three different targets that are often conflated. If you separate them, you can build something extremely powerful without making impossible claims.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-a17c-e65dcc7821aa" class="">1) Define the replica target (3 layers)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a253-eeb7ad4a5839" class="">Layer A — Biological Twin (body + nervous system dynamics)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9859-df093b8e16da" class="">Goal: predict your physiological state transitions.<br/>Output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-93f3-f7a6affe66c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: biomarker state (sleep, HRV, glucose, hormones proxies, inflammation proxies, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-9ad8-e9bbe4fc88b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: inputs (food, movement, light, meds/supplements, stressors)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8659-f95d2d7a66ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment (time zone, temperature, social load)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-8487-c2459f7ac90c" class="">This is replicable to high accuracy with enough instrumentation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b6e6-d375a962026b" class="">Layer B — Cognitive Twin (your decision operator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-bdfe-ef5b400214a6" class="">Goal: replicate “how you choose / update / reject.”<br/>Output:  and</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-a23e-fc03cfc56072" class="">This is the highest-value part for “replicating you” in practice.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bce1-e41a2c4fc07e" class="">Layer C — Phenomenal Consciousness (subjective awareness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-92c8-c25188c74761" class="">Goal: “a copy that experiences being you.”<br/>This is not something you can currently guarantee or verify with known measurement. You can build systems that <em>behave like you</em> (Layer B) and <em>track your biology</em> (Layer A) without claiming Layer C is solved.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-89b4-ff49f062c1e3" class="">If you still want to pursue Layer C, you must treat it as <strong>a hypothesis with explicit limits</strong>, not as a guaranteed deliverable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a769-ec1655196fcc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9f8c-c638b0314caa" class="">2) Architecture for a “full replica” (AMOS-compatible, deterministic, SSOT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8997-d13d08e30351" class="">2.1 State model (single source of truth)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-93ce-ed35719ef2a9" class="">Define one canonical state vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-95dd-f8422a5f0448" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t =
\begin{bmatrix}
B_t \\
C_t \\
M_t \\
\Theta_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a192-f6f0e187370a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: biological state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-bc30-ff7d782b1770" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: cognitive state (belief graph + constraint gates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b501-dd76302ba80f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory (artifacts, references, episodes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-9c00-ef5afaee42e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds (risk tolerance, proof burden, strictness)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9caf-d7890a215fd5" class="">2.2 Two-loop system: physiology ↔ cognition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-99bb-f0a5321ca739" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1} = f(B_t, u_t, e_t) + \epsilon^B_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-bccb-e9539e7189fb" class="">C_{t+1} = g(C_t, x_t, B_t) + \epsilon^C_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-a5f8-c6a4fbfad097" class="">Key coupling term is : sleep/energy/inflammation change attention, tolerance, speed, and rejection thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-9a83-edd1994eeffe" class="">2.3 Deterministic cognition kernel (your “accept/reject” operator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-bea3-cb564d99383c" class="">Your conversation indicates you operate with hard gates (meta-law, Rule-2/4, structural integrity, support typing).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-aa2e-e94a7d48be1e" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-903d-d00b439b1b8e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Accept}(x) = \prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}[k_i(x)=1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-95fb-ffcb5c8544b2" class="">and termination:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-bf33-cca059c57a13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(x)=
\begin{cases}
\text{Valid} &amp; \text{Pass} \wedge \forall c:\text{Supported}(c)\\
\text{Bounded} &amp; \text{Pass} \wedge \exists c:\text{Primitive/Limit}(c)\\
\text{Invalid} &amp; \neg \text{Pass} \vee \exists c:\text{LoadBearingUnsupported}(c)
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-b92c-f54053209085" class="">This is replicable if we can extract your  precisely and encode them.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-926b-c185f445474d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-84de-ce4858a22f40" style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-97d0-f4607b26b213" class="">If you answer with <strong>1 / 2 / 3</strong>, I will produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a9b8-e7c81b932c9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the exact module spec (files, functions, schemas, tests),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8708-f3def5f08960" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the minimal dataset schema required,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-beb7-d9dcd7df5bc5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the deterministic evaluation suite (accuracy + trace accuracy + red-team harness),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b12d-d9f454dd80b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and the SSOT integration points under your AMOS 00–17 structure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-9f32-e5849abb9ab5" class="">1) Invariant Kernel Mapped From This Conversation (with equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9e73-efebc86f20ef" class="">1.1 Kernel definition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a041-c2539f0e5a9f" class="">Let your cognition be a state  and your invariant kernel be a set of constraints .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-ad5b-e951a90845fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K = \{k_1,\dots,k_n\},\quad k_i: \text{constraint over }(C,\text{language},\text{claims},\text{process})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b50e-e38bf5790a19" class="">A statement qualifies as <strong>kernel-invariant</strong> if violating it causes systemic collapse of your acceptance function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a73c-e4a4041a122b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
k_i \in K \iff \Delta \mathcal{A}(C_t; \neg k_i) \ll 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-9e15-fcc58a03e465" class="">where  is your “accept/terminate” decision function.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-bf77-e6321e702ff4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-b163-d3da1d697e34" class="">1.2 Your extracted kernel invariants (directly evidenced by this conversation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-a907-d4006937d4c9" class="">K1 — Meta-law governance (“Law of Law”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b20d-d95c2fbc7f06" class="">All other rules are subordinate to a governing constraint layer.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-bbd9-c128020e5d3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall r \in \mathcal{R},\quad r \preceq k_{\text{MetaLaw}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-adde-ffba68c320d5" class="">Operational gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-965c-c91a95d22e67" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Accept}(x) \Rightarrow \text{MetaLawPass}(x)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-91bf-edb31c1f3c7f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b02a-e45ba4566112" class="">K2 — Rule-of-2 and Rule-of-4 checks are mandatory</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-bbea-eb047e0d41a9" class="">Every candidate model must be decomposed into duality + quadrant mapping.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-bdc4-e497068d1478" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Rule2}(x)=1 \;\wedge\; \text{Rule4}(x)=1 \;\Rightarrow\; x \text{ is structurally checkable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-896b-d119872e07aa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8516-fb6f3ff00e92" class="">K3 — Structural Integrity is non-negotiable</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-9af3-e67027c4615f" class="">No ambiguous language, no metaphor as load-bearing, no ungrounded claims.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-b421-db0885eb2138" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c \in \text{Claims}(x):\quad \text{SupportType}(c)\in \{\text{Emp},\text{Inf},\text{Def},\text{Model},\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-b66e-f4a2ad75fa6d" class="">and disallow analogical support as load-bearing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8035-efea8a04f90c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{LoadBearing}(c)\Rightarrow \text{SupportType}(c)\neq \text{Analogical}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9869-d45a1b79d22a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b67f-e07dd0a8c5b5" class="">K4 — Determinism gates for systems</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-912b-c17197799e53" class="">No nondeterministic identifiers and no time-based logic paths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a6f0-d62711335154" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{ID}(o)=\mathrm{sha256}(\text{stable\_inputs})[:n]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-b99c-d3f37a23858e" class="">\neg(\text{uuid4}) ;\wedge; \neg(\text{time.now in logic}) ;\wedge; \neg(\text{network calls})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-b9d1-f79a7827c375"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8988-f55eee9b0318" class="">K5 — Single Source of Truth (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-a936-d87d64a38a9d" class="">Exactly one canonical location for each authority object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-a239-d68956f7d499" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall \text{role } r,\quad |\text{AuthoritySources}(r)| = 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-8015-e9d15dbdb5aa" class="">If duplicates exist:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-96db-f34c356e3a3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Resolve}(r)=\arg\min_{f\in F_r}\Big(\alpha\cdot \text{StubCount}(f)+\beta\cdot \text{Deps}(f)+\gamma\cdot \text{DistanceToCanonicalName}(f)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-9c67-cda1466fc9b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a5c0-da41970e0ef8" class="">K6 — “No-stub” enforcement for required routes</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-b9f4-e12bd554d880" class="">Required execution paths cannot be placeholders.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a616-f3e8ee24d416" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall f\in \text{RequiredRoutes}:\quad \neg \text{HasStub}(f)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-8226-d58b6f5d2dc6" class="">If stub exists, it becomes an explicit issue:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-a0a3-d7561c480485" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{HasStub}(f)=1 \Rightarrow \text{Issue}(f,\text{BLOCKER})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b0aa-cd7d33fd43f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-881a-d0c981f4ca8c" class="">K7 — Evidence-linked claims; science is one verification layer, not the only layer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ae65-cc4724a7dfc2" class="">You require a multi-source validation hierarchy (scientific, experiential, logical, integrative), but still enforce explicit typing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-a773-caa467c5eb4c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
