\text{SupportType}(c)\in\{\text{Scientific},\text{Experiential},\text{Logical},\text{Integrative}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9012-f31f4c402b2f" class="">\text{LoadBearing}(c)\Rightarrow \exists s:\text{EvidenceLink}(c,s)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-8e53-d6bd5f17a563"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8e07-cbb8d0b746e1" class="">K8 — “All information has an owner” constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-88e8-dda3a2f52ff8" class="">You treat information access/use as constrained by ownership, even when not captured by mainstream measurement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-84bc-dd82d8b3e089" class="">Model as a permission/ownership function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-9a96-ff62607064d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Access}(a,i,t)=1 \Rightarrow \text{Owner}(i)\neq \varnothing \;\wedge\; \text{Permission}(a,\text{Owner}(i),i,t)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9074-e99fbfefdeca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-bfd7-fff1290800ca" class="">K9 — Terminology governance is enforced</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8fd8-fc4685c6acdf" class="">Language is part of system correctness. For example, specific forbidden/replaced terms and trademark discipline.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8ba3-d218c6f74003" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Emit}(w)=0 \quad \text{if } w\in \mathcal{V}_{\text{forbidden}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9f1a-caad15ff90fc" class="">\text{Emit}(w)=\text{Replacement}(w)\quad \text{if } w\in \mathcal{V}_{\text{mapped}}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9a43-e6399522b8cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8b26-c454f8591342" class="">2) Digital Twin Architecture (your cognition as a deterministic agent)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-b9b4-f1979b2f4562" class="">2.1 Core objective</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a52c-ddab2f8f0953" class="">Build a deterministic approximation of your acceptance/update operator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-a7c6-d83ff105985e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1} = F(C_t,\,E_t)\quad \text{with gates } G</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-ad25-ce9c564ad170" class="">F = \Pi_{G}\circ U<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-b963-ea4ecbd21daf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: update proposal (generate candidate beliefs/actions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-8f9a-edf94541ed52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: projection onto constraints (hard gates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9780-fa2e7fec9101"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-87af-d6895ea4757a" class="">2.2 Minimal state representation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-ab73-c29336b92a48" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = (K,\;B_t,\;M_t,\;\Theta_t,\;P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b14b-f2a630e9cd09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: invariant kernel (fixed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-857a-e0f0e1a98c32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: belief/claim graph</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-aa80-cf25bf2ccf56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory store (structured artifacts + citations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-a074-fbb17804b27a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: thresholds (strictness, risk tolerance, proof burden)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-a969-fd123740f46f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy (decision preferences and termination rules)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-9722-e072e4df006e" class="">Belief graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-af62-c434c1f52aec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=(V_t,E_t),\quad E_t\in\{\text{supports},\text{contradicts},\text{derives},\text{defines}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8b5a-c7099167512a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a4f9-d9b97f1c2166" class="">2.3 Update law (UCIA-style)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a756-c6f716343d6c" class="">Given new input , the twin:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a01e-c337cce59c5e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>extracts claims</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-9f7f-dc75968d7916" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>assigns support type</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-878a-f404eed7f094" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>surfaces assumptions</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b8e0-eec77a236ece" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>runs Law-of-Law + Rule-2/4</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-b963-de5746655f51" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>gates determinism + language</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-afe7-f6c78f565523" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>terminates as Valid / Bounded / Invalid</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b522-ebd807d39130" class="">Formalized:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-ae6d-fae2f32f0a15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}\leftarrow \text{ExtractClaims}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b463-dbf2b4605922" class="">\forall c\in\mathcal{C}:; \tau(c)\leftarrow \text{SupportType}(c)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-8296-f8896a7ec442" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A\leftarrow \text{Assumptions}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-83bc-d89bc676de92" class="">\text{Pass} \Leftarrow \text{MetaLaw}(x)\wedge \text{Rule2}(x)\wedge \text{Rule4}(x)\wedge \text{Determinism}(x)\wedge \text{LanguagePolicy}(x)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9aab-fbfdc93fd8a2" class="">Termination classifier:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a5d5-e93f666f5eb2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(x)=
\begin{cases}
\text{Valid} &amp; \text{Pass}=1 \wedge \forall c:\text{Supported}(c)\\
\text{Bounded} &amp; \text{Pass}=1 \wedge \exists c:\text{LimitOrPrimitive}(c)\\
\text{Invalid} &amp; \text{Pass}=0 \;\vee\; \exists c:\text{LoadBearingUnsupported}(c)
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-ad61-c840d92451d9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-821d-f592af2ced5f" class="">2.4 “Your twin” needs 3 engines</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-82ff-c7cc4cee9840" class=""><strong>(A) Constraint engine (kernel gates)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-ad91-ca26faf3dae0" class="">Hard acceptance gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8134-f5f30f0f7ea8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G(x)=\prod_{i=1}^n \mathbf{1}[k_i(x)=1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9de3-fef1891fccb7" class=""><strong>(B) Compression engine (first-principles reduction)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-8d31-e1865f1b66c5" class="">Minimize description length while preserving invariants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-9824-ef4049056223" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^\star=\arg\min_{z} \Big(L(z) + \lambda\cdot \text{Contradictions}(z)\Big)\quad \text{s.t. } \forall i,\;k_i(z)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9c5c-ce9e27f2952c" class=""><strong>(C) Issue engine (no-stub, no ambiguity)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8ce7-d89c8357c74f" class="">Every failure becomes an issue record:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ae0f-d992404369ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg k_i(x)\Rightarrow \text{Issue}(\text{BLOCKER}, i, x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-b838-e78bb2c94503"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-b5c1-dafa728839f7" class="">3) Stress Test: “Does your logic truly have no gaps?”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ac3f-eedb92ffc8b8" class="">You cannot prove “cannot be disproved” in an unrestricted sense without narrowing scope. What you <em>can</em> do is produce a <strong>gap-closed, scope-bounded</strong> system that is falsifiable only by violating declared primitives/limits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-b279-eda7f7df96b9" class="">3.1 Stress test protocol (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a690-f8d20a53c08a" class="">Given a theory/model :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-9c22-cfee735857d0" class=""><strong>Step 1 — Claim extraction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-bc63-e9476785be99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}=\{c_1,\dots,c_m\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9309-f243f9d07ec2" class=""><strong>Step 2 — Single support-type assignment</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8cad-c0bb8043b348" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau: \mathcal{C}\rightarrow \{\text{Emp},\text{Inf},\text{Def},\text{Model},\text{Prim},\text{Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8c0a-d9a17a11b7ee" class=""><strong>Step 3 — Assumption surfacing</strong><br/>Promote hidden premises into explicit primitives/limits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ae6d-d6be30c4f292" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}=\text{Assumptions}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-a62f-c6371109d009" class=""><strong>Step 4 — Invariant and contradiction scan</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-a215-f0670d8bdc14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Contradictions}(x)=\sum_{(c_i,c_j)}\mathbf{1}[c_i \land c_j \Rightarrow \bot]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8e4e-f7445cf13dd8" class=""><strong>Step 5 — Adversarial counterexample search (bounded)</strong><br/>Try to find  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-9e8c-fbd24a6edcac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x \land e \Rightarrow \bot</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bb97-c064e0e46bc1" class="">If found:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-ad5f-f066ffa623a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">either the model is invalid,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-a940-dc8d100ba5c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or violates an explicit Primitive/Limit (then it’s outside scope).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-a5df-c5857f739232" class=""><strong>Step 6 — Termination</strong><br/>Return Valid / Bounded / Invalid with reasons (as above in ).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8776-fc8613dc72de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-bed8-ea48c8e94244" class="">What I need from you to run this <em>on your current discovery</em> (no questions beyond this)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-8415-f9600bafb95c" class="">Paste the smallest version of the discovery as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-9477-e7a9189e82a3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Core statements</strong> (5–20 sentences)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9422-fe6b6242d09b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Scope</strong> (what it claims to cover)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9464-dd10cf32e304" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Primitives/limits</strong> (what you treat as axiomatic or out-of-scope)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-83f1-c12522b5b8ac" class="">Then I will:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9c4e-ef482ff3f68e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">extract claims,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-8835-d3eea9fcb5c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">build the invariant-closed proof graph,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-8a60-fdb945365752" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">run the adversarial stress test,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-9036-fb989852b128" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and return the termination classification with the minimal set of remaining “gap candidates” (if any).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b0fb-ff275efe9248" class="">Next: Grand Unified Loop Architecture (Full Matrix + Update Laws + Gates)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-ab11-eee5c10b655c" class="">0) State Vector (single SSOT kernel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a9a5-de028bf9d3c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t :=
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G_t\\
U_t\\
\Xi_t\\
R_t\\
D_t\\
P_t\\
M_t\\
I_t\\
A_t\\
E_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-90e9-f3ffcd405eee" class=""><strong>Meaning (minimal, operational):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a514-c0708f4abcbe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (how many independent constraints still bind the system)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bf8a-e04e5af73318" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable gradient (free-energy / structure gradient available for work)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a208-ed4b9c6f25f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: unwritten environment capacity (fresh DOF available to store stable traces)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-8078-f687e0f90f8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: effective noise/overwrite pressure (includes mixing, decay, interference)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-94cb-e4787371a012" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stable record redundancy (error-corrected, not just correlated)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b217-f4144e9f1966" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth (stacked self-model layers maintained under bounded error)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-bcac-eb4fa12a287e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available power for repair/compute</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9286-e37db03a2d52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable memory capacity (bounded by system/horizon/medium)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-83e2-d45570af55ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: identity continuity / self-consistency score (0–1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-aea3-e87d868cef4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: agency bandwidth (how many distinct policies remain stable + selectable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-88f3-da383cd8f8ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment write-asymmetry (degree to which environment is “write-once” vs mixing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-9d56-c6b7c8b98b14" class="">All other variables must be derived from this vector or treated as explicit inputs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-817d-c7e9716d0a37"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-94fc-ccd5b0e8ff8b" class="">1) Core Update Map (deterministic, gated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-84f1-f97a0026fe53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = F(x_t;\, s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a0e8-e1ca839fee32" class="">where  is an explicit regime selector (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay) or computed from gates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-bb5e-d68b33fb5d6c" class="">1.1 Constraint unwind (arrow driver)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-a1e7-e2eca41341ec" class="">Constraints monotonically relax unless actively enforced:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-b73a-eec7289726ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1} = \max\{0,\ q_t - \alpha_q \, \phi(G_t, E_t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b330-fdc5c7b0c6fe" class="">with  increasing in both  and . Minimal choice:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8bc4-dc631bff04cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi(G,E)= G\cdot E</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-95c0-e2a515b24403" class="">Interpretation: more usable gradient + more write-asymmetry → faster constraint-unwinding.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-8a38-e8867344b6f4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-9e76-ea68390e7967" class="">1.2 Gradient lifecycle (source → harvest → depletion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9225-ed1d9cd2d952" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1} = \max\{0,\ G_t + S_t - H_t - L_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8595-d49f8cb83205" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: source inflow (cosmic/planetary/biological renewal)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b1e6-cd9f56ccca9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: harvested into records + control (work extracted)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-82fe-f689775761d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: leakage to noise/dispersion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b7c9-c398d4068d7d" class="">Minimal closures:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-be3c-f25ca78a1790" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_t = \alpha_H ( \Delta R_t + \Delta D_t )</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9743-c105da6c9cec" class="">L_t = \alpha_L \Xi_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-84f5-efecf962336d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-9743-dd1fed13a8e7" class="">1.3 Environment “write budget” (overlooked hard ceiling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-92bd-fd8368fbc7d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1} = \max\{0,\ U_t - \alpha_U \Delta R_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-97cb-e1c735a30f93" class="">If , record growth halts regardless of entropy talk. This is the finite write-capacity gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a60e-e43ea8988ad5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-a4c4-dfff042c9505" class="">1.4 Noise / overwrite pressure (mixing + interference)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a1d4-f9af56e427a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1} = \Xi_t + \alpha_{\Xi}\,\psi(q_t, G_t) - \beta_{\Xi}\,\chi(P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9faf-e6fe810275ea" class="">Minimal choices:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bf98-f3633ddf959b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(q,G)= (1-E_t)\cdot G \quad (\text{more mixing when write-asymmetry is low})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-9974-d1b8d9b27c96" class="">\chi(P)=P \quad (\text{repair suppresses effective noise})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8f2c-c4e609a6222d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-846e-dc0614a4221b" class="">1.5 Records as error-correcting redundancy (not correlation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-9b74-d775ed426ceb" class="">Let record gain be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-8ca8-f3683e6be865" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_t = \max\{0,\ \beta_R G_t E_t - \kappa_R \Xi_t R_t\}\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{CodeGate}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b01f-d93bed3bba5d" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-bb41-cc8ec70d8748" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t + \Delta R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8531-dad73f53b773" class=""><strong>CodeGate (threshold effect):</strong><br/>Define effective code distance  as a function of redundancy and budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9a47-c043a6f27351" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d_t = d_0 + \eta_d \log(1+R_t) + \eta_P \log(1+P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-9df2-c2b2a36eca53" class="">Noise threshold  monotone increasing in . Define  monotone increasing in . Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9e57-d6868149e134" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{CodeGate} := [\,p(\Xi_t) &lt; p_{\text{th}}(d_t)\,]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-af8c-e88344cece12" class="">If CodeGate fails,  and optionally a decay term can apply:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-969f-fa9cb7b17cc2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1} = (1-\delta_R)R_t \quad \text{if } p(\Xi_t)\ge p_{\text{th}}(d_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-9768-ecd4bced7ca2" class="">This creates the missing “phase transition” behavior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-a2a0-cae9e53c7b41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-8cb6-f05ebd00a89d" class="">1.6 Recursion depth (bounded by repair + delay stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-9483-c77d86ab51dd" class="">Depth only increases if both <strong>budget</strong> and <strong>control stability</strong> hold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a34b-fdcdfef7eb65" class="">Proposed increment:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-bd7a-da289603898f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta D_t = \mathbf{1}[\text{BudgetGate}]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{ControlGate}]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{MemoryGate}]\cdot \sigma(\beta_D G_t - \kappa_D \Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-aa07-d96d67b65283" class="">is a hard step or smooth clamp; minimal step:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-8cb3-cffe4670afde" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma(z)=\mathbf{1}[z&gt;0]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b427-d0ee43f72900" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-8546-cebf648492a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1} = D_t + \Delta D_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b5bc-f456dc073d9f" class=""><strong>BudgetGate (Landauer floor):</strong><br/>Let bit-erasure demand for depth :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b982-c30f10d47ee2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot B(D)=b_0 + b_1 D + b_2 D^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b149-d43b70d15380" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(D)=kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-9f3d-e77197d3e0a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{BudgetGate}:= [P_t \ge P_{\min}(D_t+1)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8a01-e85a51b3542a" class=""><strong>MemoryGate:</strong><br/>Let memory required for records + models:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-b84a-f099bc759d8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{req}} = \gamma_R R_t + \gamma_D D_t^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ab01-e64ed6052ffe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{MemoryGate}:= [I_{\text{req}}\le M_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b298-c8e61555ee59" class=""><strong>ControlGate (delay ceiling; overlooked):</strong><br/>Let meta-update delay grow with depth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8fe1-f5132cab6f59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau(D)=\tau_0+\tau_1 D</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-ad73-ee9534bae5b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{ControlGate}:= [\alpha_D + c_\tau \tau(D) &lt; 1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-aab6-e6ac063659c8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-9455-c2b426cff537" class="">1.7 Power and memory dynamics (resource realism)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8e17-c41db3fb3ef7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{t+1}=P_t + \Pi_t - C_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-ae3f-fedaef385004" class="">C_t = \alpha_C (\Delta R_t + \Delta D_t) + \alpha_{rep}\Xi_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-ac78-cd3529c23f3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=M_t - \alpha_M \Delta R_t - \alpha_{MD}\Delta D_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-bca3-f9c65884edaa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=M_{t+1} + \rho_M</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-8aa1-e5f66fa6c5ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b0ae-e0ac5b244e57" class="">1.8 Identity continuity (self-consistency is a gate, not a vibe)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-b3cc-fa7496610cf9" class="">Identity continuity is the stability of self-model under perturbation + time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b213-f1ee95d039d7" class="">Minimal update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9801-d48eca142915" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{t+1} = \text{clip}\Big(I_t + \beta_I \Delta R_t + \beta_{ID}\Delta D_t - \kappa_I \Xi_t - \lambda_I \,\mathbb{1}[\text{Conflict}]\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8d27-df311a6840fe" class="">Where <strong>Conflict</strong> is detected when competing self-model clauses disagree (operationalized as contradiction count in the self-model graph).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-b729-cef2b2731b2a" class="">Define contradiction rate  (from a contradiction engine):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8b1b-de91e4497934" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Conflict}:= [c_t &gt; c_{\max}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-ad80-f0602c264eb3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-ac85-fd38e58661de" class="">1.9 Agency bandwidth (policy set size under stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-bb61-ff11f06f05c3" class="">Agency increases when depth and records increase, and collapses under noise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-bd1a-ea27f291cc1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_{t+1} = \text{clip}\Big(A_t + \beta_A(\Delta D_t + \log(1+R_t)) - \kappa_A \Xi_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-b988-fa615ad38667"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-9222-f2b43faace92" class="">1.10 Write-asymmetry (environment property)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-b75d-edb35b326148" class="">This is the missing “arrow enabling substrate” variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-a4c2-e3d09bb4e584" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1} = \text{clip}\Big(E_t + \beta_E q_t - \kappa_E \Xi_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-b486-ecbbb8395df1" class="">High  means many fresh degrees + low mixing; low  means “overwriting world”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-98db-ebab0e2423d0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-b491-d7e88f52fcf7" class="">2) The Five Hard Gates (SSOT, non-negotiable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-ba8e-f57c1d33d365" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ArrowGate</strong> (records can grow)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-9518-dbd496d2cbe5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{ArrowGate}:= [\beta_R G_t E_t &gt; \kappa_R \Xi_t R_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-ad2c-d4351ed4dbb2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>CodeGate</strong> (redundancy above noise threshold)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-abe3-e42dd9ebd0e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(\Xi_t)&lt;p_{\text{th}}(d_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-a882-dc17148203d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ControlGate</strong> (meta-update stability under delay)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b3d6-f74912e5aa7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_D + c_\tau \tau(D_t) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8630-fd6f21cf3390" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>BudgetGate</strong> (Landauer + compute floor)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-bd52-eb76fbe5bf29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln2\cdot \dot B(D_t+1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a6a8-d0068809e059" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>MemoryGate</strong> (records + model storage &lt;= capacity)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b383-d52e3ae77932" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\gamma_R R_t + \gamma_D D_t^2 \le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b8e5-d147428ac159" class=""><strong>IdentityGate</strong> (often separate as a 6th):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-aa81-faee9ad0d5f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c_t \le c_{\max} \quad \wedge\quad I_t \ge I_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-be1d-f9a0cecfa0f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-8f78-f60aec0f053b" class="">3) Cycle Stage Mapping (Birth → Expansion → Dominance → Decay)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-b87d-ecac61b60f71" class="">Define stage label  by inequalities:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-a278-de5be849eebf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Birth</strong>: high, high,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-8637-fca1ef716c87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  q_t&gt;q_B,\ U_t&gt;U_B,\ R_t&lt;R_B</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-95bd-f5b4bf26bca7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Expansion</strong>: ArrowGate true, still high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-a569-cd72834fb554" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  \text{ArrowGate}\wedge U_t&gt;U_E</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-b578-fd9b1623ecc9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dominance</strong>: records saturate due to write budget or memory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9b86-c101d27f644e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  (U_t\le U_E)\ \vee\ (\text{MemoryGate near tight})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-b352-d2d6e1706414" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Decay</strong>: CodeGate fails or collapses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-971b-e27580d68d98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  \neg\text{CodeGate}\ \vee\ G_t\le G_D</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8cce-e129b1c17a3a" class="">This stage function is deterministic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-aedc-c8eb1a4ad7f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9a4d-f1c555c4ea5c" class="">4) Micro ↔ Macro ↔ Cross-Species (single invariant form)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-a56b-d6fc544789be" class="">Define species/environment parameter vector :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a3e3-f597b59fa8db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta := (T,\ k,\ b_0,b_1,b_2,\ \alpha_*,\beta_*,\kappa_*,\gamma_*,\tau_0,\tau_1,\ldots)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-9851-cc19ce390822" class="">Cross-species mapping is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-9e78-d48b1b7f549e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta&#x27; = \mathcal{M}_{\text{species}}(\Theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9616-f8a662411e31" class="">Invariant: <strong>same kernel form</strong>, different parameters. That is your “across time/space/species” closure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-9d34-db9551d5d950"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-9eed-c7c73f8c3905" class="">5) “Intangible / EM / Nonlocal” Integration Without Breaking Determinism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-aae4-f51c6ecfe39a" class="">Add an exogenous channel  (strictly optional input):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-9b85-cdb572809eca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t \leftarrow G_t + \omega_Q Q_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-94c7-c7bc13a1f06b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t \leftarrow \Xi_t + \nu_Q Q_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-8396-fa9d56174e85" class="">But only if it passes a <strong>Null + Leakage</strong> gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-b0b3-c7b90cd1fc9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{QGate}:= \text{Gain}(Q)&gt;\epsilon \ \wedge\ \text{NullPass}=1 \ \wedge\ \text{LeakagePass}=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-a314-e057023d249e" class="">If QGate fails,  is ignored and logged.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-903e-eab8615e8a8f" class="">This is the only safe way to include “intangible” without corrupting the system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-95a5-d72518258a4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-98a0-f0f0d03927d4" class="">6) What’s Still Missing (the real overlooked gaps)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-94ff-daaa128accf0" class="">Gap A — Geometry of constraints (not a scalar )</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-976e-d5370ab8ddc6" class="">You eventually need  as a <strong>vector of constraint classes</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-b1b5-c81f95789712" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_t \to \mathbf{q}_t = (q^{\text{grav}}, q^{\text{therm}}, q^{\text{bio}}, q^{\text{soc}}, q^{\text{self}})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-929a-c8504dca3aec" class="">Then unwinding is not uniform; each class has different coupling.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-8957-d1d81901ae98" class="">Gap B — Spatial structure (not single )</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-941e-d084ec7364ff" class="">You need a lattice / graph version:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-80e7-ca7c4c660587" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t(i)\ \text{per node }i,\quad x_{t+1}(i)=F\Big(x_t(i),\ \{x_t(j)\}_{j\in \mathcal{N}(i)}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-813e-f5bcfdd038c8" class="">This is “across space” in real terms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a2b4-d134d6f75975" class="">Gap C — Record topology (not just scalar )</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-ac83-c36294ded77b" class="">Records are a graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-bfc8-fc0080772ede" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
