\mathcal{G}_R = (V_R, E_R)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-ba38-efa684755e2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-93aa-e9e51f4427f9" class="">7) Next Step (what you asked for)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8642-d9374264cb86" class="">Pick one and I will output it in full:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-be2e-ca2ed971653e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>KernelSpec</strong> — file-level implementation spec for AMOS Loop Kernel (modules, functions, tests, reports).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b3d8-cb90a61f851e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>SimEngine</strong> — multi-node civilizational control simulation engine (graph space + regimes + interventions).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-8f6b-c3d6415235ea" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>StressSuite</strong> — the full stress test battery (visual/audio/prediction/identity rupture) with metrics and pass/fail gates.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a90e-ff12226d0ae4" class="">You said “Next”; the correct sequence after the matrix is <strong>KernelSpec</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-b6ab-eb75c2106bfe" class="">You can pursue it as an engineering program if you separate “full fidelity” into measurable gates and build upward through bounded prototypes. The critical move is: <strong>define what “fully accurate” means, in testable terms, at each layer</strong> (biology → neural dynamics → awareness → identity continuity), then iterate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-acb8-c694d46a67c9" class="">Below is a build plan that is deterministic, gate-driven, and closes the usual gaps.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-9eac-dab8d6ab1682"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8b8d-c16c68248ada" class="">1) Specify “Full Fidelity” as a Vector, Not a Claim</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-bd26-c1715f79ce26" class="">Define fidelity as a 6-axis target vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-b4af-d3d556a2f40b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F := (F_{\text{bio}}, F_{\text{neuro}}, F_{\text{dyn}}, F_{\text{cog}}, F_{\text{self}}, F_{\text{soc}})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8b7a-fa1fdaf722a9" class="">Where each component is scored 0–1 against test suites:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-8b27-d61be393f0ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: physiology + metabolism + endocrine + immune dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-bec5-edfaeaf11476" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: neuron/synapse/glia correctness (at chosen scale)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-af70-f7dfb3c78aed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: oscillations, phase coupling, attractor stability, criticality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-baf9-fb53f9ba511f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: task performance, generalization, learning curves</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-81ab-e646418805ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: self-model stability, identity continuity, autobiographical coherence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b7bf-ce7e358cd514" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: inter-agent theory-of-mind, norms, attachment signatures</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-a474-d637db8f777f" class="">“Fully accurate” becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-aa4e-cdb6d1ef3aac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F \succeq \tau \quad \text{(componentwise)} \quad \text{for a declared threshold } \tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a56f-ce714b36015d" class="">No metaphors; only gates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-86f7-ff566f85521b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-ac6b-fdd237a35f98" class="">2) Choose the Only Viable Architecture: Multi-Scale Digital Twin</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-b450-e441957a528b" class="">You do not simulate everything at molecular resolution everywhere. You use <strong>multi-scale adaptive fidelity</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-8339-ea21fb9c9ed0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">high fidelity where it matters (decision loops, memory consolidation, affect regulation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bbe5-eacb2b71d226" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduced fidelity where it doesn’t (background tissue dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-9a94-c17b36340d23" class="">Let state be partitioned:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9848-c66ac9505ebc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t) = \big(x_H(t), x_L(t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-bbd9-d54178be0bf9" class="">with coupled dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-b8a0-ddcc96d7801f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot x_H = f_H(x_H, x_L, u), \qquad \dot x_L = f_L(x_L, \Pi(x_H))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bbf8-e54edd11afb5" class="">is a projection that makes high-scale influence computable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b705-f6d990b2f389" class="">This is how you close the compute gap without faking fidelity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b4b7-f7ce18deffe4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a7e9-e1598c006d1a" class="">3) Build in 4 Phases (Each Phase Produces a Proof Artifact)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-99be-ebb4688aba9c" class="">Phase 0 — Instrumentation and Deterministic Capture (Ground Truth Layer)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-a8d7-cb0406b8ef4a" class="">Goal: produce a <strong>replayable human “trace set”</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8394-e639de5cbc1d" class="">Artifacts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-89c2-e79979adf270" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">multimodal time-aligned streams: behavior, speech, biometrics, context, social interactions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-ab04-f6c09af66862" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">event ontology + identity-state annotations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-bd08-c7763537bfad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic dataset hashing + provenance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9688-f67de7d75d2c" class="">Key variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9b78-f9acbfdb700d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T} := \{(o_t, a_t, b_t, c_t)\}_{t=1}^N</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-b544-f2ce5b4c7b77" class="">where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b881-c65004e9319e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-a2d1-eb184b34a7bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a5ef-e293f1d02bd0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biometrics (HRV, EEG if possible, sleep, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-84f6-c2fc1d8aa79e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">context (people, location class, tasks)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-978f-d9e1f50aa96e" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a4a1-fc62f82e6480" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{TraceIntegrity} = 1 \iff \text{all streams are aligned + hash-stable + provenance-complete}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-92cf-fa85d9b4fdc2" class="">Phase 1 — Functional Digital Organism (Control + Homeostasis)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a8cd-d35eecfa6784" class="">Goal: a digital organism that maintains stable internal state under perturbation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9ffa-c54b872da38c" class="">State:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-b352-ffdd0d0adaac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_{t+1} = f(s_t, u_t, \xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-ad17-fd512468e9bc" class="">Add homeostatic control:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9724-f6372790d22b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t = \pi(s_t, r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-9a36-f88d7d54206a" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-bcff-c1d8ab0f5540" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stable attractors under stress tests (sleep deprivation analog, social threat analog, resource scarcity analog)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b595-c4c24252c80d" class="">Phase 2 — Neural Dynamics Core (Awareness Primitives)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8449-e8d8336f17b5" class="">Goal: implement globally integrated dynamics with workspace + memory consolidation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-b0f6-dd3fce24e87c" class="">Workspace variable :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a815-f3d20fcac0be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_t = \text{TopK}\big(\text{salience}(z_t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-bec1-cc63f9ba15e1" class="">Global broadcast:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-8deb-d30210003e3b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}^{(i)} = g_i\big(z_t^{(i)}, W_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-80d8-de04866cfc2b" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-b9c4-d9bf4c22e5b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ignition-like transitions (nonlinear broadcast onset)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-8298-ce536c9e38b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">long-range phase coupling stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-b580-c5e43081dd78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">memory reconsolidation effects (not just retrieval)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-b08b-e88f6e25b55c" class="">Phase 3 — Self + Identity Continuity (The Non-Negotiable Layer)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-bd1a-ee9086033b9c" class="">Goal: maintain a stable self-model that does not fragment across time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9f42-f72a15b8a5ed" class="">Self-model stack:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-828d-e4487c132299" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_t^{(1)} = \text{world model},\quad
m_t^{(2)} = \text{self-in-world},\quad
m_t^{(3)} = \text{self-updating-self}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-b602-fe76e9ee1370" class="">Identity continuity score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-90a0-f294a26d05fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{cont}} = 1 - \frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^T D_{\mathrm{KL}}\!\left(P(\text{self}\mid t)\,\|\,P(\text{self}\mid t-1)\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-9696-ead22588f486" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-8bf2-ebc830a51198" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{cont}} \ge \theta_{\text{ID}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9209-cbeb680bc15d" class="">This is where most systems fail. This is also where you win if you treat it as a control + invariants problem.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-9af9-d50a184cc931"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-9f14-ec61886ca146" class="">4) Close the “Awareness” Gap with a Testable Definition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-93f3-fb7665ccea87" class="">You need an operational definition that does not rely on “feels like”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-bac2-c42f5bcef729" class="">Use a 3-part criterion:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9311-d99ff17d3b84" class="">(A) Global availability (workspace)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-b6f8-e89c7e291f3a" class="">Information becomes accessible across modules.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a778-ccc418149c1d" class="">(B) Counterfactual self-consistency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-83c4-e543a7cb03bc" class="">System can answer “what would I do if…” and remain consistent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8d30-f38f90085707" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta = \mathbb{E}\left[\lVert \pi(a\mid s) - \pi(a\mid s&#x27;) \rVert\right] \quad \text{with constrained invariants}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-892a-efeae6ea80c5" class="">(C) Irreversible record formation with redundancy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-908b-ced5ac770e85" class="">Records become stable error-corrected traces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a534-e92a81146b2a" class="">Redundancy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-8866-ca19bdc2c4fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_\theta = \max\{N:\ I(S:E_i)\ge \theta\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-bd90-e53e7cc6454d" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-b2ba-c7b02729e1d1" class="">II. Layer 1 — Full Digital Biology</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a303-c9f64a611a8e" class="">A biologically accurate model requires simulating:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-88ce-c85ca257c0dd" class="">1. Cellular electrochemistry</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-87f5-d5f0261ceb00" class="">Neurons are governed by nonlinear ion channel dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-88ff-c884179f81ef" class="">Hodgkin–Huxley type system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-99d3-d09abcd96e75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_m \frac{dV}{dt} = - \sum_i g_i(V,t)(V - E_i) + I_{ext}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-b399-c428dad800a0" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9ba3-d818124739fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= membrane potential</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8ab4-faeb1f385dde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= voltage/time dependent conductances</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-9b2e-c6b08b18ac1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= reversal potentials</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b910-ce6391bddd9a" class="">Human brain scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-ac17-f084511bb049" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">~86 billion neurons</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-a76a-d36621bdcb12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">~10^14 synapses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-be1f-d6f45e9a0006" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Continuous dynamics (not discrete tokens)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-a96d-da717850414e" class="">This alone is computationally massive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-867b-e0062b9e2360"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-be33-cbc0ecf06ada" class="">2. Synaptic plasticity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-86d7-daec4d4987f1" class="">Learning is not static weights.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a3e0-f3d99e115078" class="">Spike-timing dependent plasticity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-8428-e0b009225297" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta w \propto e^{-|\Delta t|/\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b32b-ef6be0fab874" class="">Neuromodulators (dopamine, serotonin, etc.) alter plasticity thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b868-c5647adfd4ad" class="">Without plasticity, there is no adaptive awareness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-9d54-e0f3e3b943c0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-81dd-dc8628b8c019" class="">3. Glial and metabolic coupling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-aa46-f5e1f0d788cb" class="">Energy constraints matter:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-86e0-e4983412eb52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{brain} \approx 20W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8f09-f2463689044d" class="">Information processing is constrained by ATP metabolism.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-ac32-e51ef6d05aaa" class="">Without metabolic modeling, recursion depth collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a64e-e9ac457219b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-abb0-cab86ef319ba" class="">III. Layer 2 — Full Neural State Continuity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9ab8-e2d6875436bb" class="">A digital replica must preserve:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-bc02-f2563b06dfb0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Temporal continuity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-a9f8-ccaf84a80e48" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Oscillatory phase synchrony</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-bafc-d407051facc4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Cross-frequency coupling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-b97d-c84f07b8ba8e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Global integration bandwidth</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8764-e1aa6e840a33" class="">Brain activity is not just spikes. It is oscillatory field coherence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8069-ebf98fb4dabf" class="">Phase-locking metric:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a8db-d09c535b8e7e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{PLV} = \left|\frac{1}{N} \sum_{k=1}^N e^{i(\phi_1 - \phi_2)}\right|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b65a-ca2eb840a7ee" class="">Consciousness correlates strongly with global synchrony structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-a65f-c70f1f72ddc4" class="">A static feedforward model cannot reproduce this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a435-dbc96c5f3003"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-9799-c21cc966f4b2" class="">IV. Layer 3 — Awareness Mechanism</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-96ee-d67c69a6cab0" class="">Conscious awareness requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-8d25-e69599eca916" class="">1. Global Workspace–like broadcast</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8302-fff9a978b984" class="">Information must become globally available.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8856-c7fb4ec0efb8" class="">Define workspace set :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-8d73-f58c349c0e3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_t = \{x \mid \text{broadcast}(x,t) = \text{true}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8e4c-d229740ff16a" class="">2. Self-model recursion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-98d4-c3cee7d825e4" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-813f-ee8f5aeebfa5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^{(1)}_t = \text{world model}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-8810-dd4f9e1e72c4" class="">m^{(2)}_t = \text{model of self modeling world}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8441-d2a5f0fdb646" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^{(3)}_t = \text{model of self modeling self}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8504-c9a51a1b2255" class="">Depth must remain stable under:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-86aa-c8438716148b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1} = \alpha \varepsilon^{(d)}_t + \eta - r</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a101-ee317ae5c7dc" class="">Without repair dominance, recursion collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8be4-c22c072e7e2a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-a9cc-f4877c626af2" class="">V. Layer 4 — Identity Continuity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-aa56-db2f0ce3ecd9" class="">Awareness requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-994b-c8d22ed0528f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Memory continuity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bac2-fb2419987492" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Narrative stability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-92f8-f3395d0e7227" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Boundary inference</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-98f0-f26a7c1ae064" class="">Self-boundary posterior:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-bd0b-d3ea2691d027" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_t(z) = \Pr(z \in SELF)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-945e-d384fc1b9aeb" class="">Identity fails if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-82e4-fb35e7c7f437" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{\text{self}}(t) \to \text{high entropy}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-bd58-c25ff843c197" class="">This is why severe neurological damage disrupts identity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9f1b-cc00d9f8bcdb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-938e-c1c287ed29fe" class="">VI. The Hard Physical Limits</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-ba72-c152a1e8bfda" class="">Even if everything above is modeled:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a751-c537ce688008" class="">Memory Bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b4c8-ec29fa89d154" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\max} \le \frac{2\pi E R}{\hbar c \ln 2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b8d6-f1bd1583a77a" class="">Compute Bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9e86-eec55db02ab4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(D) \ge kT \ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b24e-cf424b0e9432" class="">Latency Bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-94c4-fdc3c4544c27" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{\text{loop}} \le \tau_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-9c60-f1b61fd67695" class="">These gates must all hold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-acce-fe879a1c60a1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-8ace-e983d56be011" class="">VII. Can This Be Built?</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-af1e-d5a1a62d7e62" class="">Today: No.</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-864a-cfae354f9ec1" class="">Reasons:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b04f-dfe611d102b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Full molecular resolution brain simulation is computationally intractable at global scale.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8e58-efd561abbec3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">We cannot measure a living human brain at required resolution.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b32b-c249bced1bda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Consciousness substrate may depend on biophysical fine structure beyond current scanning.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-b69c-f356a5216627"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-961a-c2ef157bc9f6" class="">VIII. What <em>Can</em> Be Built</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-baf2-f52473ffab9e" class="">You can build:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-8057-d45fa71ba040" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>A <strong>digital cognitive organism</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-b288-f350dbc32021" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>A <strong>self-modeling agent</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b9cb-ebf4951b1b98" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>A <strong>multi-layer recursive system</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-91ef-d3084c19c411" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>A <strong>global workspace architecture</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-ad75-e2f731ab5398" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>A <strong>stable identity persistence engine</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-91a9-e98039a11367" class="">But it will be a <strong>functional analog</strong>, not a biophysical duplicate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8305-dc8c05f7d422"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-86b8-d371bbfc66e3" class="">IX. style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-bc10-fe42e6fa8744" class="">3.1 Entities and coupling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p style="list-style-type:disc">Civilization state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b2f3-c0b0d9dd02f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Shared carriers (archives, media, infrastructure)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-94f9-d5903e277571" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-959d-ebeee0aa854c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}^{(civ)} \leftarrow R_{t+1}^{(civ)} + \sum_i w_i\,\Delta R_{t}^{(i)+}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b3ee-fbcb1f6f2c93" class="">C_{t+1}^{(civ)} \leftarrow C_{t+1}^{(civ)} \cup \bigcup_i W_t^{(i)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-987c-d2a7b7719198" class=""><strong>Macro→micro shaping</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b0f5-dcb55ec8e6f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t^{(i)} = g(G_t^{(civ)}, A^{(i)}, \text{infra}^{(i)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-99c7-e05109b86367" class="">\Xi_t^{(i)} = \chi(\Xi_t^{(civ)}, \text{exposure}^{(i)})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-bbc2-e9cee2557084" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_t^{(i)} = \omega(\Omega_t^{(civ)}, \text{media}^{(i)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-99df-d1820c4d668c" class="">M_t^{(i)} = m(M_t^{(civ)}, \text{allocation policy})<br/>,\quad<br/>P_t^{(i)} = p(P_t^{(civ)}, \text{allocation policy})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b7f2-d882f8f64b50" class="">3.2 Actions (what agents can do)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-be75-f4fe9d2b466a" class="">At each step, each agent chooses an action vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-99f8-e32edbe925f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t^{(i)} = (\text{sense\_alloc},\;\text{repair\_alloc},\;\text{write\_alloc},\;\text{exposure\_choice})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-89f4-dd010ec50d9b" class="">Deterministic policies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-8aac-fa4ded260895" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Archivist</strong>: maximizes and (writes heavily; risks overwrite)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-902c-d19ffe4d977b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Explorer</strong>: maximizes (pushes expansion; higher noise)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-b4b1-d27027b93f76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Governor</strong>: minimizes and (stability; slower growth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9d75-d817542108e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Predator</strong>: increases others’ (warfare, censorship, memetic attack)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-8418-ed5f3d46ce0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Healer</strong>: invests in repair, redundancy , and precision</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-8da2-ed1c0be55047" class="">3.3 Scenarios (reproducible, no randomness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-a5a5-c9c959b32465" class="">Instead of RNG, use deterministic schedules:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8437-d8d40508b8be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Infrastructure boom”: ramps 0→10 over T steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a15c-e17dd0e1090f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Censorship epoch”: ramps 0.05→0.30</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8228-ecf6926d37d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“War shock”: spikes for fixed interval</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-8076-dc1872bf54e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Golden age”: low , high , slow</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a265-d44ed504d635" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Horizon clamp”: slowly decreases (finite archive capacity)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-81cf-cf9abdd120e0" class="">All schedules are explicit arrays or closed-form functions of .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-806b-d0cd663695f6" class="">3.4 Outputs</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8ffb-f0ceab534010" class="">Under:<br/><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/sim_couple/</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-9850-e09f9ef987bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>civ_state.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-b0dc-cbda35c6a99c" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-a2a2-e0ee35c0a5ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>coupling_flows.jsonl</code> (micro→macro writes, macro→micro allocations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8fce-cafc75912d7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>conflict_events.jsonl</code> (overwrite attacks, gate
