style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scenario_manifest.json</code> (all schedules, hashes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-a8bc-d37ad760cb22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code> + <code>issues.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-9dc9-f8e15ae9be79" class="">3.5 Termination criteria for coupled sim</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-8d08-fdf42ccccaef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong>: at least one stable civilization trajectory reaches Dc without catastrophic code failures, and at least one recovers from a shock (returns to E/Dc).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-8c91-e945aa19f5b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong>: system runs and logs deterministically, but cannot produce recovery under any provided scenario (issue: “recovery gap”).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b06b-f6abd7c93842" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong>: nondeterminism, missing logs, or SSOT violations.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-9345-fac99dda726e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b573-f3f2f3a5de8b" class="">Single “master prompt” to insert into AMOS as SSOT (for your agent)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-9a50-d66a85fbc59c" class="">Use this as the canonical instruction block for the Loop Kernel subsystem (no variants):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-9a9d-c207655e819f" class=""><strong>AMOS LOOP KERNEL — SSOT IMPLEMENTATION DIRECTIVE</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8d17-e0a97134d9cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Implement Matrix++ (Grand Unified Loop Kernel) exactly as specified:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8dcf-ec80eeaa9d11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Parameter set in §1 is the canonical default.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8522-eb6beb69bcc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">ImplementSpec in §2 defines SSOT file placement and required tests/outputs.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9bdc-d35c726e98f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">SimCouple in §3 defines multi-agent + civilization coupling and deterministic scenarios.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8a53-e00d896bfec5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No alternate prompts. No duplicate kernels. One registry entry: <code>GrandUnifiedLoopKernel.v1</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9971-f94914f68a33" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All outputs must be deterministic (sorted keys, stable float formatting, no randomness).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-859e-c4b06182f6d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Terminate into Valid / Bounded / Invalid with explicit issue logs.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-b6ea-ef7f12023f61" class="">If you want the next artifact after this: I can produce the <strong>exact schema definitions</strong> (field lists + JSON formats) for every output file so implementation cannot diverge.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9a0e-dc64c1bcfed6" class="">Matrix++ — Grand Unified Loop Kernel (computable SSOT spec)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-872e-ec547f5c4270" class="">0) State vector (single source of truth)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-afe8-f17be8f342b6" class="">Define the full system state at time  as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-a9db-c6e5d2794d62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t =
\Big[
q_t,\; \mathcal{W}_t,\; G_t,\; U_t,\; R_t,\; D_t,\; P_t,\; M_t,\; \Xi_t,\; \Omega_t,\; \Lambda_t,\; \tau_t,\; \pi_t,\; C_t,\; S_t
\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-831e-e1a7eb1ebc83" class="">Where (all scalars unless noted):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-9d64-fe11f06a5235" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Constraint density</strong>: (constraints per volume; “initial specialness” proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-a26d-d3fa08520b83" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Weyl proxy</strong>: (free gravitational DOF proxy; optional if not simulating cosmology)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8a90-d585e2dfe483" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Gradient</strong>: (available usable gradient/free-energy proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b31e-e6e38a2f88d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Write-capacity</strong>: (unused degrees that can be irreversibly written)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-b3df-e7eaabd92c2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Records</strong>: (stable redundancy/record mass)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-9d3d-cdadd184b1ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Recursion depth</strong>: (bounded self-model stack depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8314-d02135f4f31e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Power budget</strong>: (available compute+repair power)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-823e-eedd4f8664ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Memory budget</strong>: (available persistent memory bits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-86c1-df4ccdd39fdc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Noise</strong>: (aggregate perturbation/overwrite noise)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b6f5-de30a2492831" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Overwrite rate</strong>: (explicit record destruction/rewrite pressure)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9e33-ee0deecb3384" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Precision matrix</strong>: (modal precision; diagonal or block-diagonal)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9878-ecabf130ee4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Latency vector</strong>: (modal latencies)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-bc58-e0be0e4f6b2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Self-boundary map</strong>: (self vs non-self posterior for entity )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8118-f6b45b50174d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Carrier state</strong>: (propagating information outside the agent; defined below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-846c-e268ef1f86d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Stage label</strong>: (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8d86-ef7bb3a9ba40"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-a2de-e23e98da4f16" class="">1) Channels (close the “visual/sound/EM/social/information” gap)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-8594-c66e3a44254d" class="">Split environment/write/read into 4 channels :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-8e15-e707bcf728b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t = \sum_{c} U_t^{(c)}, \quad
R_t = \sum_{c} R_t^{(c)}, \quad
G_t = \sum_{c} G_t^{(c)}, \quad
\Xi_t = \sum_{c} \Xi_t^{(c)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-894a-dc85d094f764" class="">Sensor modalities , count .<br/>Precision:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b733-e3edf508ba46" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda_t = \mathrm{diag}\big(\lambda_t^{(1)},\dots,\lambda_t^{(K)}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-bfa0-f9e708ee8892" class="">Latency:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-8d05-d4baad2e7435" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_t = (\tau_t^{(1)},\dots,\tau_t^{(K)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8574-f8b13e1daebc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-91e4-e8f5c2a307e4" class="">2) Core update laws (deterministic, gate-driven)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-8bd7-e4983c8f35ec" class="">2.1 Constraint unwinding (arrow root)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8361-f02cc6138281" class="">Constraint density monotonically decreases when gradients are available (constraint “unwind”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-96c6-c73165fff763" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1} = \max\{0,\; q_t - \alpha_q\,G_t + \epsilon_q\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9c1f-eb351ad5e96e" class="">Optional Weyl link if you want cosmology coupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-826b-ed8ea3e20f97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{W}_{t+1} = \mathcal{W}_t + \alpha_w (q_t - q_{t+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-b06b-c1495c6dfbea" class="">Interpretation: constraints unwind → free DOF rises.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-b5bf-f4169dbe5050"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-99f9-e4c0b7604b96" class="">2.2 Gradient evolution (resource)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-858a-c75c763f981e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1} = \max\{0,\; G_t + \alpha_g(q_t - q_{t+1}) - \beta_g R_t - \gamma_g \Xi_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b824-e952bbf080fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constraints unwinding can create exploitable gradients (structure formation / differentiation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-875b-e8696bf001f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">high records impose maintenance cost</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8e59-ebefee03c3af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise destroys usable gradient</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-9911-ebeda7f30b20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-9f12-e29d2521c3c7" class="">2.3 Write-capacity depletion (write-once budget)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-b570-eb814b416ffc" class="">Write-cap is consumed by <em>positive</em> record growth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-af48-cad80bbf4f8b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}^{(c)} = \max\{0,\; U_t^{(c)} - \gamma_c\,\Delta R_t^{(c)+}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8eff-c6c7f510fc10" class="">U_{t+1} = \sum_c U_{t+1}^{(c)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a096-e23df5f6c930" class="">If , records cannot keep growing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-9870-f2d5e28ae667"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8788-fe6f22e9ec0b" class="">2.4 Record dynamics (with phase transition + overwrite)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-b257-d55f349ad05e" class="">Per channel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8055-c16c10049081" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}^{(c)} =
R_t^{(c)}
+
\underbrace{\beta_c G_t^{(c)}}_{\text{record growth}}
-
\underbrace{\kappa_c \Xi_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)}}_{\text{erosion}}
-
\underbrace{\Omega_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)}}_{\text{overwrite}}
-
\underbrace{\lambda_c \mathbf{1}[\Xi_t^{(c)} \ge \Xi_{th}^{(c)}(r_t^{(c)})]\,R_t^{(c)}}_{\text{catastrophic code failure}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8fe9-ed38ac77134a" class="">Aggregate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-9d93-e774d2fac5fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=\sum_c R_{t+1}^{(c)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-a0e3-d655cdbbe565" class=""><strong>Code threshold (error-correction)</strong><br/>Let redundancy  be implied by spend (or explicit variable). Define a monotone threshold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-802d-c16e14722d7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{th}^{(c)}(r)=a_c + b_c r</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-bb63-e067ce078af3" class="">Catastrophic failure is the overlooked “record phase transition”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-9fcf-cb5fa107f4b2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a308-fb29613818ce" class="">2.5 Precision update (visual/sound/prediction closure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-a738-e8a6b983ebd8" class="">For each modality , define residual:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b678-e03c85dd976c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta_t^{(k)} = o_t^{(k)} - \hat{o}_t^{(k)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8e43-fbfc135558f9" class="">Update precision deterministically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b32c-d9ec3df81243" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{t+1}^{(k)}=\frac{1}{\beta_\lambda \sigma_{t}^{2(k)}+(1-\beta_\lambda)\|\delta_t^{(k)}\|^2+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-bf7d-ec4702ae9950" class="">Where  can be a running estimate of sensor noise; if unavailable, omit and keep residual term.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a3e4-e23151da685a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a89a-f3efa46f5f33" class="">2.6 Latency gate (depth dies from delay)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-bd3c-fd3ea8e50481" class="">Define effective loop latency at depth :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-bf23-e841ee3a09b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{(d)} = \tau_0 + d\cdot \Delta\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-87eb-c28cb2b6154d" class="">Hard gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-ab20-d1b2180c2862" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_k \tau_t^{(k)} + \tau^{(D_t)} \le \tau_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-89fc-cbfd44cb43f4" class="">If violated:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a624-e96aa1da7112" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=\max\{0,\;D_t-1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-a8fb-de95b047d87a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-87f9-c9d19ce6c0d8" class="">2.7 Recursion depth dynamics (repair vs noise + control stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-ac3f-ea7435fe57ef" class="">Maintain per-depth error  (bounded stack error):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-a42a-daae82e39a9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon_{t+1}^{(d)}=\alpha_d \varepsilon_t^{(d)} + \eta_d(\Xi_t) - r_d(P_t,G_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-abb8-fae9ff14b77c" class="">Feasibility condition (must hold ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8bbb-c1da355af576" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[r_d] \ge \mathbb{E}[\eta_d] + (\alpha_d-1)\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon^{(d)}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9d8f-ce1afbcafca3" class="">Depth update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-8da8-d875c76a3155" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=
\begin{cases}
D_t+1 &amp; \text{if FeasibleGate holds and BudgetGates hold and LatencyGate holds}\\
D_t &amp; \text{if borderline}\\
\max\{0,D_t-1\} &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-91b2-fdaa22f561b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-b080-febf65ec7f2e" class="">2.8 Compute/repair budget (Landauer-style lower bound)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9ab9-c6860acf834d" class="">Define bit-erasure rate needed for depth  and record maintenance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9231-e776c6d60072" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{B}(D,R) = a_B D^p + b_B R
\quad (p&gt;1\ \text{to encode superlinear nesting})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-902c-db17ae054acd" class="">Minimum power:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-9bd7-c60b80f159e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(D,R) = kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot{B}(D,R)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9b78-eb6417c4be48" class="">Budget gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-ad1d-e0532f4a9bfa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge P_{\min}(D_t,R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8f1c-e47fb0f7d2a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-8415-f11b388d136e" class="">2.9 Memory budget gate (finite record capacity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-9ad6-e86cfd11a96c" class="">Let record information scale with redundancy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-98f2-de760010d9c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{records}}(R) = a_I R
,\quad
I_{\text{models}}(D)= b_I D^p</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-b65c-e514f17651e5" class="">Memory gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-ad88-e230505350be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{records}}(R_t)+I_{\text{models}}(D_t) \le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-80dc-fed134fe70dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-94f4-d28fcfde03f7" class="">3) Self vs non-self (immune + identity unified)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-9dba-e62bd31818e8" class="">Entities  include: body parts, other agents, tools, artifacts, “carriers”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-ac77-ced0cb1ee7e7" class="">Posterior update (log-odds):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-887a-df80055f3b75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\log\frac{\pi_{t+1}(z)}{1-\pi_{t+1}(z)}
=
\log\frac{\pi_{t}(z)}{1-\pi_{t}(z)} + \Delta \ell_t(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-bd70-fbcd5d0a5441" class="">Decompose evidence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8a1d-e88c53390a84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \ell_t(z) =
w_{ctrl}\,\Delta \ell^{(ctrl)}_t(z)
+
w_{home}\,\Delta \ell^{(home)}_t(z)
+
w_{cons}\,\Delta \ell^{(cons)}_t(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a8a5-dfb6e14ad67b" class="">Controllability evidence (species-agnostic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-bb31-d8d57f4291ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \ell^{(ctrl)}_t(z) \propto I(a_t;\, z_{t+1}\mid z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-ae66-e66c8713f1c4" class="">Homeostasis evidence (invariant preservation):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-bab6-c65962264904" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \ell^{(home)}_t(z) \propto -\| \Delta v_t \|,\quad v_t=\text{core invariants vector}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-bf57-f3e7522030ed" class="">Consistency evidence (identity constraint satisfaction):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-a9c5-e65f031c60c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \ell^{(cons)}_t(z) \propto -\mathrm{Violations}(\mathcal{C}_{id})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-b6fe-f5221f0ca909" class="">This makes “self/non-self” computable and testable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-aaba-e42ba7e24135"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-ad56-e7ca00c00028" class="">4) Carrier layer (before-birth / after-death closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8049-d5f391772b7f" class="">Define carrier state  as a multiset of externalizable records:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a48c-e2d62e70f4c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = \{(id_j,\;I_j,\;dur_j,\;acc_j)\}_{j=1}^{N_t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8261-e974f4400b53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: information content (bits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b9d3-e976747e267f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: persistence half-life proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9ca0-e14036766d7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: access/ownership gate (below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-9c4f-c20f26021a1b" class="">Carrier update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-83b7-e5cc582f507b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1} = T(C_t) \cup W_t \setminus L_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-9f64-c5f404bec60c" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-9142-da560f86c262" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: deterministic decay/persistence map using</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a5ea-f4792b41d299" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: writes produced by the agent/civilization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-8882-c8843b0a53a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: losses (deletion, censorship, entropy, overwrite)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-9ad1-df2f9b2337f8" class="">Crucial: carrier continuity does not require the organism state  to persist.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8245-de03d5c47a48"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-aa60-dce67b60ae09" class="">5) Ownership/access gate (information exists but may be inaccessible)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-8037-c6a26ba76eb3" class="">For each carrier item , define access .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-9ae8-e15e32db701d" class="">Accessible information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-bbf0-f4eaf06ef446" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_t^{(\text{access})} = \sum_{j} A_j(t)\, I_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b791-df8746d80598" class="">Ownership gate enforces the “all information has an owner” constraint as a hard boundary on what can be used by the engine at time .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-96c7-faeae1ba8c2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-b8e0-f78d064c9216" class="">6) Stage machine (Birth → Expansion → Dominance → Decay)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-96a3-f68d9636b18f" class="">Define stage by deterministic thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-bbe0-ffd1369901d9" class="">Birth (B)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b1ad-eefa5ece867a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=B \iff q_t \ge q_B \ \wedge\ U_t \approx U_{\max}\ \wedge\ R_t \approx 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b769-f5b842223efb" class="">Expansion (E)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-b356-fb569488f9ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=E \iff (q_t&lt;q_B)\ \wedge\ (G_t \ge G_E)\ \wedge\ (U_t&gt;U_E)\ \wedge\ (\Delta R_t&gt;0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-811a-d04219f2aac2" class="">Dominance (Dc)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8097-d6d29b9c1f39" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=Dc \iff (U_t \le U_D)\ \wedge\ (R_t \text{ high})\ \wedge\ (\Delta R_t \approx 0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-ae5b-d3fef7694723" class="">Decay (De)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a5cb-cc5b1273ddcf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=De \iff (G_t &lt; G_{min})\ \vee\ (\Xi_t \ge \Xi_{th})\ \vee\ (\Omega_t \text{ high})\ \vee\ (U_t=0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-9df6-eb8c0a8f1618" class="">Stage affects coefficients (e.g., overwrite rises in dominance/decay, gradients fall in decay).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-8f30-d4eccbc15a59"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-9ce6-d72709efbed6" class="">7) Civilization coupling (micro ↔ macro, same kernel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-af9d-f503489a51e2" class="">Let civilization state be  with same variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-8e5a-e736640870e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t^{(civ)} = (q,R,U,G,\Xi,\Omega,D,P,M,C,S)^{(civ)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8811-f4d16f0fb3d2" class="">Micro→macro write injection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b270-c9c25533141c" class="">Agents  contribute to civilization records:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-8529-dd97f474da16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_t^{(civ)} = \sum_i w_i\,\Delta R_{t,i}^{(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b761-c4f465af9b07" class="">R_{t+1}^{(civ)} \leftarrow R_{t+1}^{(civ)} + W_t^{(civ)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-9902-ce4ad927358a" class="">Macro→micro noise/gradient shaping</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9bbd-dd572c394dd6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t,i}=g(G_t^{(civ)},\text{access}_i,\text{infrastructure}_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-909e-ea8b8f0741e3" class="">\Xi_{t,i}=\chi(\Xi_t^{(civ)},\text{exposure}_i)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bc14-c78ffa1bf376" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{t,i}=\omega(\Omega_t^{(civ)},\text{media\_pressure}_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-985c-fa532b1748e6" class="">This is the civilizational control loop: civilization modifies individual feasibility gates; individuals write carriers back into civilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-bba8-ced0c099a736"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-ac16-fa8b8cd64aa4" class="">8) Cross-species mapping (species-agnostic scoring functions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-9e28-da62647f1073" class="">Define a species profile  containing constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-844d-da3cc9483790" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma = (B_{\text{met}},\;K,\;\tau_{\max},\;P_{\max},\;M_{\max},\;\text{channel availability})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-bc41-dc0faf6a0582" class="">Cross-species capability scores (all )</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-afa3-e685c9448e08" class=""><strong>Record Stability Score</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-ac33-cefa08a5fecc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{RSS}(\Sigma,t)=\sigma\!\left(
\frac{\sum_c (\beta_c G_t^{(c)} - \kappa_c \Xi_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)} - \Omega_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)})}{1+R_t}
\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-94c9-d4c7bbc6e3ce" class=""><strong>Depth Feasibility Score</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-b2d3-d0641da73b25" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{DFS}(\Sigma,t)=\mathbf{1}[P_t \ge P_{\min}(D_t,R_t)]\cdot \mathbf{1}[I_{\text{records}}(R_t)+I_{\text{models}}(D_t)\le M_t]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\max_k\tau^{(k)}+\tau^{(D_t)}\le\tau_{\max}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-86a0-c1555692208f" class=""><strong>Self-Boundary Clarity</strong><br/>Let boundary entropy over entities :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-80ab-c286c6c6ffc5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{\text{self}}(t)= -\sum_z \big(\pi_t(z)\log \pi_t(z) + (1-\pi_t(z))\log(1-\pi_t(z))\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-81dc-fe83e48c2033" class="">\mathrm{SBC}(\Sigma,t)=1-\sigma(H_{\text{self}}(t))<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-ac57-cc708849f924" class="">These scores allow comparison across species without claiming identical substrates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-884c-c881efcf2ed4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-98ad-f5dfafe3960d" class="">9) Prediction stress-tests (visual/sound/EM) — required kernel outputs</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-bb9d-fe56615b2fa9" class="">Define three stress batteries:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-bb9b-dd9d031f1d97" class="">9.1 Visual prediction test</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bf9c-ec27eef55d02" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{VPE}_t = \|\delta_t^{(vis)}\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9d6e-f21dc957ae05" class="">9.2 Audio prediction test</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-8d57-e29256d5684e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{APE}_t = \|\delta_t^{(aud)}\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-abb7-ffdd45f0b583" class="">9.3 EM inference test (WiFi-like carrier)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-bae7-cfdf7abdf5be" class="">Measure EM residual:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-8afb-c1bc65120db2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{EME}_t = \|\delta_t^{(em)}\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a325-e3f1cdbdf693"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-9101-ef8335ddd36b" class="">10) Hidden/“intangible” channel (bounded latent, not free-form)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9f86-e65e0a37fd62" class="">Introduce latent channel  only if persistent mismatch:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8d88-d9c093ff5e62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_t = \|o_t - \hat{o}_t\|_{\Lambda_t}^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-a63c-fee07e6eef5d" class="">\mathcal{E}_t &gt; \tau_E\ \text{for }L\text{ consecutive steps} \Rightarrow \text{enable latent } o^{(h)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8e49-e759b6baa3ee" class="">Bounded influence constraint (prevents unconstrained explanations):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9a0e-ed2aab25102b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Big\|\frac{\partial \hat{o}}{\partial o^{(h)}}\Big\| \le \kappa_h</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-a897-dcc804015c53" class="">This is the strict way to include “beyond instrumentation” without making the model non-falsifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bfd5-df29f9921b3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-baf0-fb9a4e1454d7" class="">Kernel Gates (must be evaluated every step; failures are explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b133-ffe6562597ea" class="">Define boolean gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-8422-c1eb4a7212c3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ArrowGate</strong> (per channel):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-8955-eee72a7d0e06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_c G_t^{(c)} &gt; \kappa_c \Xi_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)} + \Omega_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8276-c577fa0f9fac" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>CodeGate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-ba47-c9f5cb22b810" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t^{(c)} &lt; \Xi_{th}^{(c)}(r_t^{(c)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b285-f6b50785cefc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>LatencyGate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b13f-def7d93ac83c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_k \tau_t^{(k)} + \tau^{(D_t)} \le \tau_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-882a-f91b0eef9565" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>PowerGate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-a1fa-c342054b03ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge P_{\min}(D_t,R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-bab4-e490fb4c6caa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>MemoryGate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b4b5-e54b7add72dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{records}}(R_t)+I_{\text{models}}(D_t) \le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-be75-e93c3d501c69" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>OwnershipGate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-8f3b-f7bac6e006d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Any use of }C_t \text{ must be restricted to }A_j(t)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-9181-e2eda7804bba" class="">If any gate fails, update must record an issue and reduce  and/or  deterministically per fail-policy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9b7d-e71530a00c66"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a305-f90b18c8c8bd" class="">Required outputs (what AMOS must write from this kernel)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-83a9-ccf18a8793aa" class="">At each run (or episode), output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-8aad-c26b393936c2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>kernel_state.jsonl</code> — full each step (sorted keys, deterministic)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-b313-f7a7e1e57e25" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>gates.jsonl</code> — gate booleans + first-failure reasons</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-a103-db553604888d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>stage_trace.jsonl</code> — transitions + trigger variables</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-887b-d5645d5a5445" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>records_by_channel.json</code> —</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-a1ce-e919189160b6" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>self_boundary.jsonl</code> — top entities by and entropy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-bcf8-f40155a5a03c" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>carrier_manifest.json</code> — current with hashes + access flags</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-89c5-e126b383bc29" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><code>species_scores.json</code> — RSS/DFS/SBC per</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-95dd-ed5805c97408" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><code>stress_tests.json</code> — VPE/APE/EME traces + pass/fail</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9f33-f9a1fe54dc7c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ad0b-e54a0e4b8427" class="">Minimal “single-step” function (the executable idea)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-bdd6-ef145d42b1a1" class="">Define a deterministic step function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg=="
