id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8da6-fd834bf796c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(X_{t+1},\; \text{reports}_{t+1}) = F(X_t,\; u_t,\; \Sigma,\; \theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-9a43-c1064d05c14b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: actions (writes, sensing allocation, repair allocation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9261-f2e6184ae826" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: species profile</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9fb4-efdece9ab7bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: fixed coefficients, thresholds, gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-a350-d7887bb1e82d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b3f3-da9a6099e455" class="">If you want the next layer immediately</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b1c1-fc6816a8cd6c" class="">Choose one:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9ecf-cb65ed3d376f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Parameterize</strong> → give a canonical coefficient set that produces all four stages in simulation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-aea9-f8c0b93b7c11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ImplementSpec</strong> → file-level kernel spec mapping this Matrix++ into your AMOS 00–17 folders (functions + tests + reports).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-91f6-cbc467b61f88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SimCouple</strong> → multi-agent + civilization coupled simulator spec with reproducible scenarios.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-920e-e9b8d0124d09" class="">Add the missing layers (tangible + “intangible”), still computable</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-af27-d8a350ec9fa8" class="">A) Environment is not one thing: split it into 4 write-channels</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-ba41-feb5bad524b8" class="">Replace single  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-bb61-d5cf40cf1568" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_t=\big(e_t^{(m)},e_t^{(em)},e_t^{(s)},e_t^{(i)}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-81b1-f76b10982924" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: electromagnetic (light, RF, electric/magnetic coupling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-aa55-cc74b3db5e7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: social (other agents, norms, reputation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-a89f-e5325262ef7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: informational (symbols, media, protocols, “memetic” propagation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bf3a-cf0c85498dd3" class="">Observation decomposes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a1ab-f6a8835394aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
o_t^{(m)}\\ o_t^{(em)}\\ o_t^{(s)}\\ o_t^{(i)}
\end{bmatrix}
=
\begin{bmatrix}
h_m(e_t^{(m)},b_t)\\
h_{em}(e_t^{(em)},b_t)\\
h_s(e_t^{(s)},x_t)\\
h_i(e_t^{(i)},m_t)
\end{bmatrix}
+\nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-b8b3-cf5d7d20c8b4" class="">This closes the gap “visual/sound/EM/meaning” by treating each as a separate write/read channel.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-a386-e8226762d905"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b239-d83f40c0cb9f" class="">B) Add an explicit electromagnetic loop (energy + information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b4a8-c79ae20af382" class="">Introduce an EM state  (not “field” language—treat as measurable EM signal manifold):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-8da8-e37c107faab7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F_t \equiv (E_t,B_t,\Phi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-926d-e9970904d9ef" class="">Coupling into body + sensors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-97d5-f71c813c708d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_{t+1}=f_b(b_t,a_t,e_t^{(m)},F_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b539-c7cf93a9e272" class="">o_t^{(em)} = h_{em}(F_t,b_t) + \nu_t^{(em)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-9f7a-e5575d09a765" class="">Record channel capacity must now include EM carrier bandwidth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a31e-d3c8d4890ebd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t = U_t^{(m)} + U_t^{(em)} + U_t^{(s)} + U_t^{(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b70d-fc2f33ee377b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}^{(em)} = U_t^{(em)} - \gamma_{em}\,\Delta R_t^{(em)+}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-aef1-ec74c63de19d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t = R_t^{(m)}+R_t^{(em)}+R_t^{(s)}+R_t^{(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-be50-c0708aada474" class="">Arrow gate becomes channel-wise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b53e-e796c58a5f99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_c G_t^{(c)} &gt; \kappa_c \Xi_t^{(c)} R_t^{(c)} \quad \forall c\in\{m,em,s,i\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8e26-c528c2ad0ea9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-ba08-c736cb2dd1c1" class="">C) Visual + sound + proprioception are separate constraints (not one “perception”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-84ca-e45277d7cd4c" class="">Let the sensor stack be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-a26e-c2c20a810ab1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t = (o_t^{(vis)},o_t^{(aud)},o_t^{(prop)},o_t^{(intero)},o_t^{(em)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-addf-fc7c5a573798" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-a78d-f0213f8e8ae4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bandwidth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b4ca-f1a69ea2e923" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-83d1-ec25d09d4950" class="">Precision is a diagonal (or block-diagonal) matrix, not a scalar:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-bfbc-ccd8ba918223" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda_t=\mathrm{diag}\big(\lambda_t^{(vis)},\lambda_t^{(aud)},\lambda_t^{(prop)},\lambda_t^{(intero)},\lambda_t^{(em)}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8150-ef6b46cdb155" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{t+1}^{(k)}=\frac{1}{\beta\sigma_{t}^{2(k)}+(1-\beta)\|\delta_t^{(k)}\|^2+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a8a7-cba75177857b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-98ae-eb96f74a8a8b" class="">D) Self vs non-self is a boundary inference problem (immune + identity unify)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-85d7-f85cce27277d" class="">Define a boundary classifier  over entities  (including internal parts, other agents, artifacts):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8244-cd4afc99dfaf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_t(z) = \Pr(z \in \text{SELF}\mid \mathcal{D}_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8b53-f1dcc84a50a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\log\frac{\pi_{t+1}(z)}{1-\pi_{t+1}(z)}
=
\log\frac{\pi_{t}(z)}{1-\pi_{t}(z)} + \Delta \ell_t(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b2cb-dd71e115c480" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">controllability evidence (does action reliably change ?)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9217-c9c05f0d6947" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">homeostasis evidence (does maintain body invariants?)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9ca6-f514612ff7f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">consistency evidence (does preserve identity constraints?)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-af6d-ff38ce5ab9db" class="">Controllability term (core, species-agnostic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-896a-caf38a25534c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \ell^{(ctrl)}_t(z)\propto I\!\left(a_t;\, z_{t+1}\mid z_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-839d-d9863c8cb619" class="">This closes the “self and none-self” gap in a way that can be implemented.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-b3bd-d6cd2338d9f1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8dd1-e8aa1fc7a2d2" class="">E) “Intangible” becomes “unobserved channels” with gating (no hand-waving)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-8d07-e192059a97f6" class="">Add hidden channels  that are not captured by mainstream instrumentation for a given study:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-8d5c-c24c99e618e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t^{(h)} = h_h(e_t,b_t) + \nu_t^{(h)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8922-f97bbf1ca38e" class="">Residual energy (model mismatch):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-93ac-f56275650e37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_t = \|o_t - \hat{o}_t\|_{\Lambda_t}^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a611-e5e81b12e3f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_t &gt; \tau_E \ \text{persistently} \Rightarrow \text{introduce latent } o^{(h)} \text{ with bounded influence}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-8627-e1d6b6907550" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\| \partial \hat{o}_t / \partial o_t^{(h)} \| \le \kappa_h</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-8583-d1e8bdfa9231"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b38e-d2b734925465" class="">F) “Before birth / after death” becomes an information continuity accounting (records vs carriers)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-bf4d-f1dfb64ecdb1" class="">Define two inventories:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9d08-f665b8370f82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Recorded state</strong> : redundant stable traces inside reachable system</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-b333-d92c1583ceb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Carrier state</strong> : propagating information outside the agent (signals, artifacts, other minds)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-84cd-fb203cbe1561" class="">Carrier dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8de0-d2846fc79e4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1} = T(C_t) + W_t - L_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-98d0-d6427411f6b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: loss/decay (noise, deletion, entropy, censorship)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-b2a2-ee622cf60f20" class="">After biological death,  ends, but  continues if  had created persistent carriers. This is entirely within physics/information accounting without requiring any specific metaphysical claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-9a27-e870c1aebd82"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-935d-d18aab3be4f2" class="">G) Civilization-scale loop coupling (macro) + organism loop (micro) share the same gates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-94f1-e111be8728c4" class="">Let civilization state  reuse the same kernel variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-a70c-e87eb7265b97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t^{(civ)} = (R_t^{(civ)},U_t^{(civ)},P_t^{(civ)},\Xi_t^{(civ)},D_t^{(civ)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-9044-cf77916ece24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}^{(civ)}=
R_t^{(civ)}+\beta G_t^{(civ)}-\kappa \Xi_t^{(civ)} R_t^{(civ)}-\lambda \mathbf{1}[\Xi_t^{(civ)}&gt;\Xi_{th}]R_t^{(civ)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9e6e-ea88d8ed910d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_t^{(civ)}=\sum_{i=1}^{N} w_i\,\Delta R_{t,i}^{(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a5ef-d6f997c14e83" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t,i}=g(G_t^{(civ)},\text{access}_i),\qquad \Xi_{t,i}=\chi(\Xi_t^{(civ)},\text{exposure}_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-a643-d5b77c81c520"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-86ec-c4df0057fda7" class="">The “more overlooked” missing gates (hard failure modes)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-90b3-cf8bb4235b39" class="">1) Latency gate (depth dies from delay, even with power)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-bb8e-fdafa905127f" class="">For each modality  and each meta level , require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-806a-ee843e0cb2a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^{(k)} + \tau^{(d)} \le \tau_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-ba29-cba52829fb04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=D_t-1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-9c6d-e8c765ccb72a" class="">2) Overwrite gate (records are not monotone)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9fce-fac7c02f5249" class="">Define overwrite rate  (editing, rewriting, propaganda, memory reconsolidation):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-935d-ea234f065d23" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t + \Delta R_t - \Omega_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-98a7-f8f4b96fe70d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_t &gt; \Omega_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a346-cf4d0422d9b8" class="">3) Ownership gate (access is constrained)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-ae8e-f661b115233e" class="">Model “all information has an owner” as access control:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9329-f50707c1ba00" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A(z,t)\in\{0,1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a859-e3a85179ecf6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_t^{(\text{access})}=\sum_z A(z,t)\,I(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b1c1-ebc081222228"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 <strong>one</strong> output and I will write it fully:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-a844-eab362756829" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Matrix++</strong>: the full kernel as a single executable spec (all variables, all channels, all gates, all stage transitions), including the cross-species scoring functions and layout.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-b339-fa907b07ef8f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>SimEngine</strong>: the coupled src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-8297-e2c17d425085" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_{t+1}=A\,s_t + B\,u_t + \zeta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-bb6d-c9eeb9663e4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A=
\begin{bmatrix}
A_{xx} &amp; A_{xm} &amp; A_{x\lambda} &amp; A_{x\varepsilon} &amp; A_{xb} &amp; A_{xe} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{xD}\\
A_{mx} &amp; A_{mm} &amp; A_{m\lambda} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{mD}\\
A_{\lambda x} &amp; A_{\lambda m} &amp; A_{\lambda\lambda} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{\lambda D}\\
A_{\varepsilon x} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{\varepsilon\varepsilon} &amp; A_{\varepsilon b} &amp; A_{\varepsilon e} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{bb} &amp; A_{be} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{bP} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{eb} &amp; A_{ee} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{eP} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{RR} &amp; A_{RU} &amp; A_{RP} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{UR} &amp; A_{UU} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{PR} &amp; 0 &amp; A_{PP} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{IR} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{II} &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; A_{Dm} &amp; A_{D\lambda} &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; A_{DP} &amp; A_{DI} &amp; A_{DD}
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a3e6-ee1b953c2e97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B=
\begin{bmatrix}
B_{xa} &amp; B_{xr} &amp; B_{xs}\\
B_{ma} &amp; B_{mr} &amp; B_{ms}\\
B_{\lambda a} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
B_{\varepsilon a} &amp; B_{\varepsilon r} &amp; B_{\varepsilon s}\\
B_{ba} &amp; 0 &amp; B_{bs}\\
B_{ea} &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
B_{Ra} &amp; 0 &amp; B_{Rs}\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
B_{Pa} &amp; B_{Pr} &amp; B_{Ps}\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0\\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-8176-ea4bd85823f3" class="">Interpretation (what each block <em>means</em>, minimally):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-9c67-fb91b47b94be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: identity persistence / attractor stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-ae2e-d11a50070445" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: meta-control and depth affecting state evolution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-86e3-c24df47750a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: error accumulation; , : repair reduces error</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-871e-d7c308dd24c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: body/environment dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-aa39-de8b8071c459" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record decay; : power supports record refresh; : write-cap supports record expansion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-bcf7-f8d228bff0bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: write-cap depletion; : records consume write-cap</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bde8-d93d6e54c9ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: power persistence/decay; : record maintenance cost drains power</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-bea3-d1159c948e9a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory persistence; : records consume memory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8a02-cd72274123c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: depth persistence; : depth limited by power/memory; : depth depends on meta-stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b4bc-c2f0852d5303" class="">This is SSOT-friendly because every term maps to a single subsystem gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-bfb5-e44d102da157"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-a70c-e113b8697ab5" class="">1.3 Nonlinear “physical” instantiation (preferred for simulation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8771-c2d6ac6a0659" class="">For an implementable kernel, use the explicit nonlinear updates below. These are the <em>actual</em> equations you would code; the matrix above is the local linearization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-8364-d3472774ed43" class="">Observations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b92d-f0c674c33917" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t = h(e_t,b_t)+\nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-aa07-fc3958c681a8" class="">Identity dynamics (C1)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a77a-d0c037c3252d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = f_x(x_t,o_t;\theta_t,\lambda_t) + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a95e-eada58f672be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho\!\left(\frac{\partial f_x}{\partial x}\Big|_{x^\*}\right) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-93e7-fa8777832f3b" class="">Meta update and precision (C2)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b676-c965918625ab" class="">Prediction error:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8cfa-d0a396fd4ba1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta_t = o_t - \hat{o}_t,\quad \hat{o}_t=\mathbb{E}_{\theta_t}[o_t\mid x_{t-1},a_{t-1}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-816d-d05f4bc06e2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma^2_{t}=\beta\sigma^2_{t-1}+(1-\beta)\|\delta_t\|^2,\qquad \lambda_t=\frac{1}{\sigma_t^2+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-81be-c5a96f081eb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_{t+1} = f_m(m_t,\delta_t,\lambda_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9a15-d17185fe1b80" class="">Continuity/error repair (C3)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-bd3d-f3b50ad8ae76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon_{t+1}=\alpha\,\varepsilon_t+\eta_t-r_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-8d52-f54407f491f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = r_0 + r_s\,s_t^{(\text{repair})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9dac-f679b907d7ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon_t \le \varepsilon_{\max}\quad \forall t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b459-ce3b551a8056" class="">Body/environment coupling (C4)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b34d-cc7bed98437d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{aligned}
e_{t+1}&amp;=f_e(e_t,a_t)\\
b_{t+1}&amp;=f_b(b_t,a_t,e_t)
\end{aligned}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-87e7-de843b54528d" class="">Records + write-capacity (arrow kernel)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8897-f4f247a56e9a" class="">Define gradient supply  (free-energy gradient usable for writing/refreshing records).<br/>Record update with phase transition (code threshold):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-8e20-c38720e5eb04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=
R_t+\beta_R G_t-\kappa_R \Xi_t R_t
-\lambda_R \mathbf{1}[p(\Xi_t)\ge p_{\text{th}}(r_t)]\,R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-accb-ff7a2248ade4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma_R (R_{t+1}-R_t)^{+}
\quad,\quad U_t\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-abf0-ebe3f1c0233e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0 \Rightarrow \text{record growth can occur}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-8f77-eb814c1bf673" class="">Budgets (C5)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-aac6-c00c94b2f7bb" class="">Power:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-8292-f45dca0b4843" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{t+1}=P_t+P^{\text{in}}_t - P^{\text{maint}}_t - P^{\text{compute}}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9dd6-ce9a33876504" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{\text{compute}}_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8a31-f034a01ed19e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{t+1}=I_t - I^{\text{records}}_t - I^{\text{models}}_t + I^{\text{reclaim}}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-983f-cb7e0eb2b33c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)\quad \land \quad
I_t \ge I^{\text{records}}_t + I^{\text{models}}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8c5e-ea63203d592b" class="">Depth update with delay stability ceiling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-b9b5-c310a7baaa83" class="">Let depth be feasible only if delay-stability holds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-a833-f53d06228769" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta_d(t)-\rho_d u_d(t-\tau_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9542-d7198f7c0400" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=
D_t+\mathbf{1}\Big[\forall d\le D_t: \alpha_d+\rho_d L_d(\tau_d) &lt; 1\Big]
-\mathbf{1}\Big[\exists d\le D_t: \varepsilon^{(d)}_t&gt;\varepsilon^{(d)}_{\max}\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-939e-cd2e067be054"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-9498-d7db1ccbf84b" class="">1.4 Cycle-stage regime schedule (Birth → Expansion → Dominance → Decay)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-9086-ef686a42813d" class="">Define a single stage variable  driven by three scalars: constraint density , write-capacity , gradient supply .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-be87-ff52b05914c8" class="">Stage transitions (deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-be03-d72990219c36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Birth (B)</strong> if high and high and low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8ce3-cd36fe4177db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Expansion (E)</strong> if rising and and record-gates pass</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a1df-ecfc8c078a1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dominance (Dc)</strong> if low or saturating and near steady-state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-9fb9-e2dc732ab8d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Decay (De)</strong> if falls or rises so record inequality flips:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet"
