crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a20c-e62e3a366742" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_R G_t \le \kappa_R \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-8424-f7434b89ab0b" class="">This yields the single loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-b41c-e1deaf3f03a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(q\downarrow)\Rightarrow(U\uparrow)\Rightarrow(G\uparrow)\Rightarrow(R\uparrow)\Rightarrow(U\downarrow)\Rightarrow(R\downarrow)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a825-c272dee84a36"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-aa0b-e87d5aae29b9" class="">2) Cross-species grading equations (C1–C5 as observable proxies)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a8ab-dc8bd178fe1c" class="">Goal: compute a deterministic score vector</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-9718-fe21848b9c6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{S} = (S_{C1},S_{C2},S_{C3},S_{C4},S_{C5})\in[0,1]^5</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-915e-e9f28663838c" class="">Common observational setup</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9772-f3170d92617f" class="">For each individual (animal/human), collect time-series over horizon :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-ad0c-cd50bc921b36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Behavior state (pose/locomotion/choices; any embedding)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b012-c47e1f9adcff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Context (task, environment condition)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-929e-ca001c3956cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Perturbation markers (novelty, stressor, relocation, injury, social change)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-a847-d704de501a5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outcome (task success / homeostasis proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-be44-d60179dc661f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If available: physiology (HRV, sleep, temperature, glucose—species-specific mapping allowed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-a4c1-ee63d76d1b1f" class="">Define a learned or hand-crafted embedding  that is consistent within a species and comparable across time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-8e3b-c7b7c6e0c2f5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-afe0-e9dd61fa9da6" class="">C1 — Identity persistence score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a124-e5cccadd3371" class="">C1 proxy 1: Within-context self-state stability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-a0f4-cf2c2d027c12" class="">Compute within each stable context segment :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b6c1-faeab668126e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V_k=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{T}_k|-1}\sum_{t\in \mathcal{T}_k}\|\phi(y_t)-\phi(y_{t-1})\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8fee-ce57047d1b03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V=\mathrm{median}_k(V_k)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a2e7-ef5cae41fdca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C1}=\exp(-\alpha_1 V)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-a2fb-ec711542e027" class="">C1 proxy 2: Attractor count penalty (fragmentation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8b87-f24ee87c8910" class="">Cluster  into  modes; let  be the mode label. Define fragmentation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-bea7-e26516005af1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{FI}=\frac{H(z_{1:T})}{\log K}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-85b8-fb59ab42727d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C1}\leftarrow S_{C1}\cdot (1-\mathrm{FI})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-ad26-d6a6eb0de930"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-b553-e176af5a3975" class="">C2 — Metacognitive / uncertainty handling score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-8ef5-ead67c9038c3" class="">We cannot assume verbal confidence for non-humans. Use <em>behavioral calibration</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a406-dd0717d1e0a9" class="">C2 proxy 1: Risk-sensitive choice calibration</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-b9bb-eae69d501518" class="">In trials where the agent chooses between “safe” vs “risky” options with measurable success probability  (estimated from ground truth), infer implied confidence  from the choice model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9336-d4cad28f3463" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{choose risky})=\sigma(\beta(\hat{p}_t-\tau))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a749-ea05f87471d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{BS}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{p}_i-y_i)^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-8a62-cb6bc489f616" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C2}=\exp(-\alpha_2\,\mathrm{BS})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-8618-f527c3952922" class="">C2 proxy 2: Adaptive exploration under uncertainty</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-ae4f-cc4a1d707526" class="">Let  be cumulative regret in a bandit-like setting. Normalize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b508-fb4c09cd91e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C2}\leftarrow S_{C2}\cdot \exp(-\alpha_2&#x27; \,\mathrm{Regret}_T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-aed9-c0b025fb345e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-9fcc-d5d538e19154" class="">C3 — Continuity under perturbation score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a951-f645809c616d" class="">Define perturbation times . For each perturbation at time , compute recovery time to return within baseline band.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9216-e417c612ef98" class="">Baseline band (from pre-perturbation window ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-92d4-c3e479fa6d52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mu=\mathbb{E}[\phi(y_t)]_{t\in W},\quad
\sigma=\mathrm{std}(\phi(y_t))_{t\in W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-bf5d-f4765a2b97a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{\text{rec}}=\min\{\tau\ge 1:\ \|\phi(y_{t_p+\tau})-\mu\|\le k\sigma\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a2d2-f4cd248ed0e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C3}=\exp\!\left(-\alpha_3 \cdot \mathrm{median}_{t_p\in\mathcal{P}}(\tau_{\text{rec}})\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-96d7-c6e2351e8cb7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-a5f1-ccd2a7c9ece3" class="">C4 — Closed-loop agency score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8dcd-d41c7fa4cc2b" class="">Agency = measurable action→observation control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-a21a-e959ada577f7" class="">Estimate a causal effect proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-8636-c6dacaec2c74" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Ag}=
\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[
\left\|
\mathbb{E}[\phi(y_{t+1})\mid a_t=a]-\mathbb{E}[\phi(y_{t+1})\mid a_t=a&#x27;]
\right\|
\right]_{a\ne a&#x27;}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-8017-e4761fe284c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Vol}=\mathbb{E}_t\left[\|\phi(y_{t+1})-\phi(y_t)\|\mid a_t=\varnothing\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-a6ec-f098b508ccc5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C4}=\sigma\!\left(\alpha_4\left(\frac{\mathrm{Ag}}{\mathrm{Vol}+\epsilon}-\tau_4\right)\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-86ea-e1f63af2723c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a816-ebe4d22818dc" class="">C5 — Budget-constrained stability score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-aff2-eeb22d0acd05" class="">We cannot measure absolute Landauer budgets in animals directly, but we can measure <em>effective resource margin</em> via fatigue/overload signatures.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-b427-f2d36a61b586" class="">C5 proxy 1: Performance vs load curve</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8ca2-fa3cbfc0a7aa" class="">Let task load be  (difficulty/energy demand). Measure performance . Define margin as area under curve:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-8d17-f0d226366aea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M=\int_{0}^{L_{\max}} Perf(L)\,dL</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8fa1-cb5ad4face1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C5}=\frac{M}{L_{\max}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-b015-ccce8ed62f09" class="">C5 proxy 2: Maintenance duty-cycle sufficiency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-b574-e4f477ad726d" class="">If sleep/rest exists, estimate rest fraction  and see if it maintains continuity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a33d-e12fe6e528e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{C5}\leftarrow S_{C5}\cdot \sigma(\alpha_5(\bar{s}-\tau_5))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9907-f569d45e36f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-8275-d249163687a2" class="">Cross-species composite + classification</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a429-d2b25a4798c0" class="">Composite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-94b9-d56346d5a501" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{\text{total}}=\sum_{i=1}^{5} w_i S_{Ci},\quad \sum w_i=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-98c0-f3600cedf5b3" class="">Kernel classification (deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a83d-d616b154ae1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Structurally Valid</strong> if all gates exceed thresholds:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9994-e273c455c88f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{Ci}\ge \theta_i \ \forall i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-8ea1-d23b03d0152a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists i: S_{Ci}&lt;\theta_i \quad \land \quad \min_i S_{Ci}\ge \theta_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-bd7b-dc23b7ebc612" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min(S_{C1},S_{C3},S_{C4})&lt;\theta_{\text{collapse}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-bf81-f9ab0cfe888f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-9ab2-c87c2babc51d" class="">What you would implement next (AMOS-ready, file-level intent)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-86a3-fced854c9e75" class="">If you want this wired into AMOS Loop Kernel, the minimal SSOT module boundaries are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-9e93-c1d185ab78f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/</strong>: declare variables , regimes , gates.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-b70f-c0890de1ea5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/equations/</strong>: implement the nonlinear updates above + linearization.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-93df-d625b2eef06f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>07_METABOLISM/ingestion_pipeline/</strong>: ingest behavioral/physiology time-series into + compute scores.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8f6a-ea5855a8aa68" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>15_LAW_ENGINE/structural_integrity/</strong>: gate evaluation + classification rules exactly as written.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-89b4-cb5e47c12943" class="">If you want the next step, say: <strong>KernelSpec</strong>, and I will output the complete file-level spec (modules, functions, tests, and required reports) for implementing both the matrix engine and cross-species grader.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
